"Uhm 'kya bari muje ai sai idanun ki su gane miki"

"Amma ai ya kamata......"

"Kin san dai baxan sai dake ba,ko kuma na kaiki inda bai dace ba" ya katseta da fadin haka.

Jan bakinta tayi ta tsuke bata sake magana ba har xuwa lokacin da taga yayi parking motar a wani kata faren store.

Bude motar yayi ya fito sannan ya xagayo ya bude mata kofa yana fad'in    "fito muje"

"Uhm kawai kaje ni xan jiraka anan"

Kasa yayo da kansa dai dai face d'inta  yace   "haba kanwata kayan abinci fa kawai zamu siyo naga bamuda komai kuma kinsan ni bansan list din abinda mukeda bukata"

"Nifa banida bu......"


Yatsansa daya ya d'ora kan lips dinta sannan yafa magana kasa kasa    "haba khairat 'kinsan banason musu dan Allah kiyi hkr ki fito muje kinji plsssss"

Dariya yaso ya bata ganin yadda ya marairai ce,    "shikenan muje"

Tare suka shiga store din suka d'iba duk wani abu dasuke bukata, suka biya kud'in aka kawo musu kayan aka zuba a but din motar sannan suka shuga suka d'au titi.

Ji tayi kamar tace masa ya kaita gida tunda dare beyiba lokacin suna store akayi sallar ishsha'i, ganin hankali sa ba'a kanta yake ba ya sanya itama ta shareshe ta d'auko wayar ta kunna data ta fara karatun novel batare da tasake dago idonta ta kalli hanyar da suke d'auka ba.

Suna isa yayi parking motar a kofar gate din gidan sannan ya d'ago ya kalleta har lokacin hankalin ta yanakan wayar ta.

"Uhm munxo ki tashi ki shiga ciki, ni zanje na dawo amma ba dad'ew zanyi ba"

Rufe wayar tayi sannan tace    "toh shikenan saika dawo" ta bude motar ta fito.

Sai a lokacin ta kula da inda suke, wani farin ciki taji ya ziyarce ta, da sauri ta juyo ta kalleshi lokacin da yake bude motar yana fitowa.

"Wow amma Yaya naji dadi wlh kamar kasan yadda nake kewar Momcy da dady"

"Kara sowa yayi inda take tsaye ya fuskan ceta kana yasoma magana.

"Kamar yadda nayi tsammani zakiyi farin ciki matukar na kawoki gidan danku gashi  kuwa kinyi duk da baki wani jima da ganin su ba, nasan da cewar na bata miki kuma bansan wane abu zanyi miki Wanda zaisa ki rage jin zafin abinda nayi miki ba,shiyasa na kawoki gidan Ku a lokacin bai kamata ace na dauki hannu harna mareki ba,ko babu komai ke amana ce a wajena,na 2 kuma kina jin haushin nayi miki ba dai2 ba amma na gaxa baki hkr, bawai ha'kuri ne bazan iya baki ba, kamar yadda kika fada min d'azu ba,kawai bansan wace Kalmar zan furta miki ba ne"

Ta 'kosa ta shiga gidan shiyasa tace "karka damu yaya ai ya wuce"

"Shikenan amma dan Allah inki shiga ciki kar.....

" nasan abinda zaka fada,in dai nice karka damu babu Wanda zan fadawa wani abu"


Murmushi yayi ganin harta fahimci abinda zai fad'a mata "toh ki shiga saina dawo zan shigo"

Bata bashi amsaba ta juya tanufi gidan shi kuma ya bud'e motar ta shiga ya tafi.

Da sallama ta shuga falon babu kowa aciki sai kayan kallo da suke aiki a falon,dakin Momcy ta nufa, tana gab da shiga saiga dady ya fito shida rahama. Rahama na ganin ta ta saki hannu Dady da gudu ta taho ta rungume khairat.

"Aunty dama zakizo amma Momcy bata fadamin ba??"

"Ai ban fada mata zanxo ba shiyasa bata fada miki ba"

Dady ne ya karaso yana murmushi
 "mamana kece a gidan???

" Dady ina wuni???"

"Lpy qalau, ina sadeeq din yabar ki ke daya da daren nan haka??"

"Ah ai tare muke dashi ya d'an je wani wajene yanxu zai dawo"

"Owk"

Sm"shiga kiyi mata magana nasan batasan da zuwanki ba"

"Toh Dady" ta fada ta fara tafiya,rahama ce ta bita da gudu, Dady ne ya juyo yana fadin   "yanxu sbd kinga yar uwarki shiyasa kika fasa tayani kallon news d'in??"

"Uhm nidai Dady na fasa"

"Toh ai shikenan" ya fad'a yana tafiya, khairat na dariya ta shiga dakin Momcy.

A d'akin suka sameta tana gyara kayan da aka kawo mata daga wajan guga.

Momcy taji dadin ganin khairat sosai, nan ta tayata suka gyara kayan, sannan suka dawo falon,lokacin khairat ke tambayar ina sageer, Momcy ta sanar mata yana gidansu sadeeq, tashi tayi sbd batayi salla ba,tana idarwa Momcy ta xubo mata abinci taci, nan suka zauna firar yaushe gamo tunda wancan zuwan basu jima ba,shidai dady tashi yayi yabasu guri.

Kwata2 khairat bata bari Momcy ta fahimci komai tsakanin ta da sadeeq ba.

Sai wajan 10:00 sadeeq ya dawo, sun gaisa da Momcy da Dady a gurguje sbd dare yayi, sadeeq ne ya fara tafiya sannan khairat harta fita ta dawo tacewa Momcy ta xuba mata abinci a kula saboda tasan sadeeq baici abinci ba.

Tunda suka dau hanya khairat takejin wani mugun bacci,ganin idanunta sai lumshewa suke ya sanya ta kwantar da kujerarta ta kwanta bata jimaba bacci ya kwasheta.

Suna isa gida yayi parking motar ya jiyo xai yimata magana, tsayawa kawai yayi yana kallon ta shidai yaga lokacin da ta kwantar da kujerar ta amma baisan bacci tayiba,yayi tunanin ko xaman kujerar ne beyi mata dad'iba ta gyara.

Rasa yadda zaiyi ya tashe ta yayi kawai ya tsaya ya 'kura mata idanu,wani irin kyau yaga tayi masa  a hankali ya sauke wata ajjiyar xuciya ya maida idanunsa kan lips dinta,yanason sanya light pink din lipstick shi kuma hakan ba karamin tafiya yake dashiba, ji yake kamar yayi kissing lips din nata amma baxai iya,sai a yanxu ya dada tabbatar wa da Kansan yayi nisa akan son khairat shiyasa komai nata yake burgeshi, tun yana kasar waje yake ganin mata Kala2 amma bai taba ganin macen da komai tayi kyau yake mata ba kamar khairat.

Gabansa ne ya fadi tunawa dayayi  kwanaki kad'an ya rage wata daya ya cika,wata xufa yaji ta fara karyo masa, bai sani ba ko khairat xata matsa masa akan ya saketa haka xalika bai saniba ko xata iya hakuri kawai suci gaba da xamansu tunda dama aure sukayi wanda kowa ya shaida shi su biyune kawai suka san da maganar daga baya zai saketa sai kuma Abdul.

Jingina Kansa  yayi jikin kujerar yana ayyana abubuwa da dama a xuciyarsa....

AURAN WATA DAYA

💦💦💦💦💦💦💦💦💦💦💦💦💦💦💦💦




 *Written by neenat seif💞💞💞💞💞💞💞💞*




 *Dedicated to my blood sis Aisha😘*



 *Page  28* 




Tunda ya jingina kansa a jikin motar yaji wani irin yanayi yana shigarsa, gaba daya komai nashi ya sauya a hankali ya dago Kansa, dai dai lokacin khairat dake bacci ta bude idanunta a hankali da sauri ta mi'ke tana fad'in  "yaya ashe mun iso gida amma baka tashenj ba"

"Uhm" kawai yace mata ya bude motar ya fito,itama fitowa tayi,sannan ya rufe motar suka wace cikin gidan.

Kai tsaye khairat ta wuce d'akinta bata jira komai ba ta cire kayanta ta sanya na bacci, sai data kashe wayar ta sannan ta kwanta, da sauri ta mike tunawa da tayi da abincin da ta tahowa sadeeq dashi.

A falon ta sameshi ya kwata akan kujera Kansa yana kallon sama, da mamaki ta karasa kusa dashi tana fad'in "Yaya lafiya naga baka shiga d'aki ba?? Kuma naga kamar kanada damuwa"

Jiyowa yayi ya kalleta da sauri ya d'auke Kansa yace "Uhm babu komai khairat"

Har cikin xuciyarta bata yarda ba amma saita share maganar tace "Yaya na manta da abincin ka fa a mota wanda na taho dashi daga gida"

"Na koshi"

"Haba Yaya kar muyi haka da kai nasan bakaci abinci ba gashi dare yayi yanxu banaso ka 'kwana a haka"

Bai sake cemata komai ba ya tashi ya fita, ita kuwa guri ta samu ta xauna tanajin wani iri a xuciyarta game da yadda taga damuwa 'baro2 a fuskar sadeeq.

Bai jima da fita ba ya dawo hannu sa ri'ke da kular abincin,tashi tayi ta amsa kulan ta ajje sannan ta nufi kitchen, bin bayan ta yayi yana kallon ta da ace tasan meyake damunsa da bata fito da kayan bacci a jikinta ba.

Hannun ta d'auke da plet ta fito ta ajje gaban sa ta zuba abinci tace  "Yaya gashi"

Sakkowa yayi daga kan kujerar ya xauna a kan carpet d'in dake falon ya fara cin abincin, kasa tashi khairat tayi lafiya qalau suka taho daga can gida amma yanxu gaba daya ya sauya batasan meyake damunsa ba kuma batajin zai iya fad'a mata, ita kuma baxata juri ganin sa a haka ba har cikin xuciyarta takejin babu dad'i na ganinsa haka.

Kwata2 baici abinci da yawa ba ya miko mata yace ya koshi, kar'ba tayi ta hada da kular ta kai kitchen, sannan ta dawo falon,yana xaune inda yaci abincin ya dora Kansa jikin kujerar idanunsa a lumshe, karaso wa tayi gab dashi ta zauna a hankali ta furta "Yaya"

Bai amsa ba sai bude idanunsa kawai yayi, "dan Allah ka fad'amin meyake damunka jikina yana bani kamar ni nayi maka wani abu na laifi"

"Uhm babu abinda kikayi min banajin dadi ne kawai shiyasa, ya kamata ma kije ki kwanta 12:15 fa yanxu" ya nuna mata agogon dake manne a bangon falon.

"Allah ya baka lpy"

Tana fad'a ta mike ta shige d'aki ba tare da ta sake kallon sa ba.

Yajima zaune a wajan ba tare da ya koma d'akiba, sai da yaji wani irin sanyi na shigar sa alamun zazza'bi neman kamashi sannan ya tashi ya tafi daki.


Washe gari saida khairat taci baccin ta sannan ta fito falon wajan karfe 10:00 kasan cewar yau weekend.

Kitchen taso ta shiga ta had'a musu breakfast amma saita tuna basu shigo da kayan da suka siyo ba, don haka saita fara gyara gidan kafin sadeeq ya fito su kwaso kayan sai tayi girkin.

Harta gama shuru babu alamar sadeeq zama tayi a falon sama tana jiran sa kusan 1hrs tana zaune amma shuru, gashi ta fara jin yunwa,yanke shawarar xuwa ta tasoshi tayi domin ta gaji.

Kai tsaye ta bud'e kofar ta shiga kwance yake a tsakiyar gado ya lullu'ba da wani katon bargo.

Mamaki abin ya bata saboda ita dai tasan ba sanyi ake yiba,karasa wa tayi ta janye bargon  a 'kudundune ta ganshi fuskar sa tayi fayau da ita,da sauri ta furta " yaya bakada lpy ne??"

Bai bata amsa ba sai bud'e idanunsa yayi ya sake rufesu, da sauri ta zauna bakin gadon ta sanya hannun ta dafa goshin sa da sauri ta d'auke hannun ta jin zafi dau a goshin.

Da sauri ta sanya hannu ta d'agoshi tana fad'in ka tashi mu tafi hospital bakaji yanda jikinka yayi zafi sosai ba.

A hankali ya janye hannun ta daga jikinsa ya jingina da filo  sannan yace  " ba sai munje hospital ba barina kira wani doctor daga hospital d'inmu saiya dubani.

"Toh" kawai tace masa, wayar dake kusa dashi ya dauka ya nemo number da yake nema sannan ya kara a kunnen sa.

Sai da suka gama gaisawa sannan yace heesham inaso kazo gida ka duba ni ne tun jiya banida lpy.

"Owk bani 30mns ganinan zuwa,

" owk" kawai yace ya kashe wayar sa.

Sun dad'e a zaune batare da ya kuma cemata komai ba sannan itama bata tashiba.

Can wayar sa ta fara ruri yana dagawa yace "owk" ya kashe wayar kana ya kalli khairat kayan bacci ne a jikinta bata sauya kaya ba, "kije ki dauki hijab ki sanya saiki shigo da doctor heesham banajin zan iya sauka 'kasa"

Bata bashi amsa ba ta fita, zuwa can sai  gasu tare da doctor heesham, suna shigowa ko zama bata yiba ta fice daga d'akin ta koma falo.

Sai da heesham ya gama duba sadeeq sannan yace "doctor sadeeq basai na fada maka abin da yake damunka ba, abin mamaki kai da kake yiwa mutane fad'a akan su daina sanya damuwa a zuciyarsu saboda sanin illar da hakan ke haifar wa amma sai gashi kai da kanka kana neman illata kanka ta hanyar sanya wa kanka damuwa"

Ajjiyar zuciya sadeeq ya sauke sannan yace  " tabbas maganar ka haka take amma inaso ka sanani malami ne zakaga yana fad'akar wa akan wani abu Wanda shi kanshi yana aikata wa, just kawai inaso ka fahimci ita damuwa tana zuwa ma d'an adam ne bashi yake kiran ta da Kansa ba."

"Hakane amma dan Allah ka kiyaye, nasan kasan mugun gunan da kake bukata sbd haka zaka iya turawa a siyo maka saboda babu a hospital d'in mu bare nasa a kawo maka amma kafin lokacin ka nemi maganin da zai saukar maka da zazzabin nan tukkunna.

" shikenan heesham na gode sosai "

"Babu damuwa"


Doctor heesham na fita ta shigo tana fad'in "sannu yaya ko kana da bu'katar wani abu??"

"tea kawai zaki hado min amma kar kisamin madara"

"Owk barina je na hado maka"

Mintuna kadan ta kammala sai kwai data soya masa ta kawo masa, yana kammalawa yace ta mi'ko masa wani karamin box, karasawa wajan tayi ta d'auko ta kawo masa,yana budewa taga Ashe magunguna ne aciki, ruwa yasa ta miko masa yasha sannan yace ta hada masa ruwan wanka.

 *Page  29*



Ruwan da ya watsa a jikinsa ne ya sanya yad'an ji 'kwari, shiryawa yayi cikin wani d'anye boyel ya fito.

A falo ya sameta tayi tagumi,gaba d'aya ta manta da wani batun yunwar data soma ji d'azu,tun tana gida idan taga wani bashi da lafiya nan danan hankalin ta zai tashi ta kasa samun sukuni bare kuma yanxu da shashin xuciyarta ne babu lafiya😍

Zama yayi kusa da ita da sauri tace "haba yaya in wani abu kakeso kayimin magana mana kana sane baka da lpy fah"

Murmushin yayi saboda yaji dad'in maganar ta ko babu komai ta nuna damuwar ta a kansa,  "kar ki damu dama zazza'bin ne ya hanani fitowa amma yanxu dana sha magani na watsa ruwa naji yad'an sauka"

"Toh Allah ya kara baka lafiya"

"Ameen"

Kallon taya yayi sannan yace "tashi ki shirya zaki rakani anguwa"

Sakin baki tayi tana kallon sa

"Yes anguwa zaki rakani"

"Hmmm kaida bakada lpy"

"Na sani tashi ki shirya"

"Toh amma fa kasan banyi wanka ba"

"Karki wani damu zan jiraki"

"Toh shikenan"


Bata dad'e sosai ba ta fito ta gama shiryawa tsaf,fita sukayi kai tsaye suka nufi wata motar bata jiya ba saboda ko kayan da suka siyo basu 'kwashe ba.

Ma zaunin driver ya bude yana yimata nuni da tazo ta shiga, no'kewa tayi,da sauri ya bata face yace "bafa wasa nake miki ba"

Bata musa masa ba tazo ta shige shi kuma ya zauna a mazaunin mai zaman banza ta tada motar suka fice daga gidan.

Shiya nuna mata hanyar da zasubi har xuwa wani kata faren chemist tana parking d'in motar ya fita ya shiga wajan, mintuna kad'an ya fito ya gama siyo magun gunan da yake da bu'kata,motar ya shiga yace taja su tafi.

"Gida zamuje?"

"A'a"

Bata sake magana ba taja motar suka tafi,wanna karon ma shiya nuna mata hanyar da zatabi har suka isa wani gidan abinci sannan yace ta tsaya, jiyowa tayi tace "me zamuyi a gidan abinci kuma?"

"Saboda kinajin yunwa"

"Nina fad'a maka haka?"

"A'a amma kinsan ina sane da cewar bakiyi break fast ba kuma gashi yanxu karfe d'aya saura"

"Hakane amma ka barshi idan munje gida saina girka"

"Karki damu barina siya mana kawai" yana gama fad'a ya bude motar ya fita,yana fara tafiya taga ya tsaya ya dafe kansa, da sauri ta bud'e motar ta xagayo ta rikeshi tana fad'in "maiya faru yaya badai ciwon kan bane??" 

Ganin baxai bata amsa bane yasanya ta taimaka masa ya koma cikin motar sannan itama ta shiga zata tada motar.

Da sauri ya ri'ke hannun ta ya ciro kud'i  a aljihun sa ya mika mata "jeki siyo mana abincin"

Zatayi magana ya dakatar da ita, kar'ba tayi ta fice daga motar tashiga gurin abincin,takeaway 3 tayo sannan ta fito ta suka tafi.

Tun daga bakin gate ta ciro takeaway d'aya ta bawa baba mai gadi, suna shiga falo ta nufi kitchen ta dauko musu plet tazo ta ajje, kaya sadeeq yakeso ya sauya amma kuma bayajin zai iya taka steps d'in saboda haka ya fad'awa khairat, tashi tayi taje dakin sa ta dauki mas kaya marasa nauyi ta kawo masa sannan ta shige kitchen domin yasamu damar sakawa.

Saida ta tabbatar zuwa lokacin ya sanya sannan ta fito,samun shi tayi harya gama sakawa yana jiranta,'karasawa tayi ta zuba musu abincin suka ci,bai wani ci abincin da yawa ba yasha magani.


******************************


Yau Abdul ya tashi da niyyar zuwa gidansu maryam, don haka tun magariba ya shiraya yana yin sallar ishsha'i ya shiga motar sa sai gidan su maryam.

Yana zuwa gidan ya kirata ya shaida mata, kanin ta ta turo yayi masa ido zuwa d'akin ba'ki, saida ta gama shiryawa sannan ta nufi d'akin ba'kin, da sallama d'auke a bakinta ta shiga d'akin cike da nutsuwa.

Amsa mata yayi sannan ta samu d'aya daga cikin kujerun ta zauna ta gaishe da shi ya amsa,kusan 5mns babu wanda ya kuma magana a cikin su, xuwa can Abdul ya nisa yace. "Da farko dai basai nayi miki bayani akan waye ni ba kin sanin kinsan iyayena da kuma duk wani abu daya kamata ki sani a kaina.

" ko ban fad'a miki ba nasan kinsan abinda yakawo wajanki ina fatan wani bai rigani"

Murmushi tayi saboda ita maganar ma dariya taso ta bata.

"Bayan haka ni a yanzu banida wani buri daya wuce ace kin amince dani sannan ki soni kamar yadda nake sonki,maryam na jima ina sonki sanar mikine kawai banyi ba sai yanzu saboda kar naci gaba da yin sake wani yazo ya kwace min ke,amma idan bakiyi na'am dani ba karki boyemin ki sanar dani.

Murmushin tayi sannan tace " sanin kanka ne duk macen da tace bata sonka ta fad'a ne saboda tasan dama baxata sameka ba,baka da makusa ka hadu duk wani abu da mace take bukatar namiji ya kasan ce dashi nasaba, ilimi, kyau, uwa uba kanada aikin yi,saboda haka babu abinda zance sai dai Allah ya tabbatar mana da alkairi.

"Ameen ameen" ya fad'a yana mai jin dad'in kalaman ta, nan dai suka sha firarsu ta masoya sannan Abdul ya tashi ya tafi, maryam na shiga gida ta sanarwa hajiya yadda sukayi, hajiya taji dad'i sosai a ranta sannan tayi musu addu'a.

 *Page   30*



Tun daga wannan lokacin soyayya ta d'in ke tsakanin Abdul da Maryam soyayyar su sukesha basuda wata matsala.



*****************************

Kulawa sosai Khairat ke bawa Sadeeq saboda wani irin mugun ciwon kai dayake zuwa masa lokaci zuwa lokaci,wani mugu mugun tausayin sa da 'kaunar sa takeji a ranta, tayi tayi dashi akan a fad'awa su Momy da dad amma fur yaki.

Ya riga da yasan abin da ke samasa wanna ciwon kan,yawan tunanin da yakeyi ne ya sanya kansa yake sarawa a duk lokacin da ya matsawa kansa da tunani ga wani zafi da xuciyarsa ke yimasa, gashi doctor amma bayason shan magani ko a hospital idan time na shan maganin marasa lpy yayi zaije ya basu amma idan zasusha juyawa yakeyi baya 'kaunar ya kalla.

Khairat ce zaune a falo tana jere kayan break fast d'in su s kan Capet saboda yanzu Sadeeq baison zaman dining, sai da ta gama kammala komai sannan ta shiga d'akinsa zata kirawo shi.

A bakin mirro ta sameshi yana feshe jikinsa da turare da alama wanka ya fito yake shiryawa,'karasawa tayi kusa dashi ta gaishe shi ya amsa, ta kalleshi tace

"Amma ko kayimin magana nazo na had'a maka ruwan wanka shine ka hada da kanka bayan kasan baka da lpy"

"Karki damu naji sau'ki yau jikin nawa garau nake jinsa"

"Uhm kai dai kace bakaso na samu lada"

"Ladan me zaki samu?"

"Ladan had'awa mijina ruwan wanka mana, ko ka manta duk abinda mace tayiwa mijin ta lada take samu matu'kar yaji dad'insa?"

Jiyowa yayi yana kallonta, da sauri tace

"Sorry Yaya wasafa nake maka yayana xance nace miji"

Juyawa yayi batare da ya sake kallonta ba, ita kuwa 'kwafa tayi dama tana sane tayi mai haka.

"Dama na kammala break fast ne nace barina yimaka magana"

"Owk muje toh tunda na kammala"

Tare suka fito suka zauna ta zuba musu abincin, bayan sun kammala ta 'kwashe kayan ta d'ebo masa mugun gunan sa, tana kawo masa yafara yatsuna face ya amsa, da d'ai d'ai yake 'bare su yana runtse idanunsa ya a watsa wa a bakinsa kamar zaiyi amai.

Khairat dake gefe abin yabata dariya, a kuwa tayita 'kya'kyata abarta, kallonta kawai ya tsaya yanayi dariyar tayi mata kyau gwanin sha'awa, ko 'kifta ido bayayi, tari ta fara yi alamun 'kwarewa,ya dauka kawai just irin tarinnan ne kawai amma sai yaga abin ya'ki tsayawa harta fara hawaye,a rud'e ya d'auki ruwan daya sha maganin ya janyo ta jikinsa ya bata ruwan kad'an tasha har lokacin tarin takeyi hannu  ya sanya ya shiga bubbuga bayanta a hankali, sannu a hankali tarin yaringa lafawa har lokacin tana rungeme a jikinsa.

Kusan 20mins suna zaune a haka baiji ta motsaba kuma batayi magana ba, juyo da fuskar ta yayi ya kalla, mamaki ne ya rufeshi ganin bacci take karkad'a kai yayi a fili ya furta.

" sarakin bacci kenan, wai ita wannan bata gajiya da bacci??"

Ji yayi bazai iya tashinta ba, don haka ya jingina da kujera, ya gyara mata 'kwanciyar a jikinsa yana binta da kallo wani mugun soyayyar ta yakeji tana 'kara ratsa kowane sassa na jikinsa,kasa d'auke idanunsa yayi daga face d'in ta, a hankali ya fara lissafa 'kwana kin da suka rage musu, wata fad'uwar gaba yaji ta ziyarce shi da sauri ya kara rungume ta yanajin wani irin abu a ransa ji yake kamar inya saketa shikenan sun rabu a haka har bacci ya kwashe shi.         (Allah sarki har yabani tausayi😔)

*****************************

Momy na zaune a falo Aisha ta fito sanye cikin wani dakakken less tasha 'kwalliya da alama unguwa zataje.

Momy ce ta kalleta tace      "wow autana kinyi 'kyau fa"

Murmushin Aisha tayi tace "kai amma naji dad'in yabonki Momy" bata jira ta kuma magana ba tace

"Mom barina tafi kinga rana tanayi kuma karfe 3:00 zan shiga lecture"

"Oh daga can zaki wuce makaran tar??"

"Eh bakiga na d'auka jaka ba"

"Haka ne, toh Allah ya kiyaye hanya, ki gaida khairat d'in,shikuma kice masa har yau aikin bai barshi yazo ganin mu ba kenan"

"Toh Momy duk zan isar musu"

Kud'i Momy ta bata sannan ta tafi.


*****************************


Sai wajan 12:00 khairat ta farka a hankali ta bude idanunta hadi da salati....bata karasa salatin ya ma'kale a bakinta, binsa tayi da idanu har sai da ta gasgata tabbas a jikin Sadeeq ta gama sharar baccin ta,a hankali ta tuna dalilin daya sanyata bacci, murmushi tayi ta sake kallo Sadeeq wanda yaketa sharar baccin sa, a hankali ta shiga zame hannun sa daga jikinta daya zagayeta, ji tayi ya sake matse ta da sauri ta dago idanunta ta kalleshi, a hankali ya bud'e idanunsa cikin nata, da sauri tayi kasa da kanta dan wani abu ta hango cikin idanun nasa, kanta a 'kasa tace

"Kayi hakuri Yaya na tasheka kana baccin ka"

"Uhm dama ai ke kika sani baccin"

"Ni dai babu ruwana"

"Toh wayasa ki yin bacci a jikina bayan kinsan baxan iya tashinki ba??"

Cike da shagwab'a tace   " toh ai nima kai kasanya na 'kware har baccin ya tafi da ni"

"Toh dama........"

Sallamar Aisha ce ta katsesu,  da sauri khairat ta juyo jin muryar Aisha ai tana ganin itace da sauri ta mike daga jikinsa ta ruga ta rungume Aisha tana fad'in 

"Sis koki fad'amin zakixo"

"Sorry dear nima ban tashi da shirin zuwa bane kawai na shirya na taho ne"

Tare suka: karaso falon, shiko Sadeeq saboda haushi mi'kewa yayi ya wuce sama abinsa dan Aisha ta bata masa rai yau d'aya daya d'an samu kusanci da madarar ruhinsa😂 tazo ta bata masa bajat.

Su Aisha kuwa binsa sukayi da idanu, Aisha ce ta juyo tace "wannan ko haushi yaji na katse muku jin dad'in Ku?"

Kauda maganar khairat tayi  "yasu Momy da dady?"

Aisha ta kula khairat katse maganar tayi don haka ita ta basar sukaci gaba da firar su.

Suna kitchen suna girki Sadeeq ya sakko,sun gaisa da Aisha ta fad'a masa sa'kon Momy, cemata yayi ta fad'a mata yau zaizo,suna gama magana ya tafi masallaci.

Sai wajan 2:30 khairat ta rako Aisha zata tafi, lokacin Sadeeq ya shigo. Tambayar ta yayi tun yanzu zata tafi?

"Eh Yaya zan wuce school ne inada lecture karfe 3:00"

Duba time yayi yaga lokacin ya kusa dan haka yace muje na kaiki"

Sallama sukayi da khairat suka shiga mota suka fice daga gidan...

 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_



 *Page   31*


Suna fita ta koma cikin gidan, har akayi la'asar Sadeeq bai dawo ba har wajan 'karfe biyar, khairat kuwa ta cika fam,bawai saboda bai dawo bane taji haushi ba saboda ta ajje masa abincin ya barshi tun rana har zuwa yanzu, 'kwafa tayi a xuciyar ta ta ayyana komai hucewar abincin haka zaici, dan ma yaci sa'a a kula yake.


Sai da tayi sallar magrib sannan ya shigo, lokacin tana falon tana chart, da sallama ya shigo falon, ko kallon sa batayi ba ta amsa.

Binta kawai yayi da idanu ya samu guri ya zauna, sun jima a haka batare da tace masa komai ba, sai da ya gaji sannan yace

"Ina abinci na??"

"Yana falon kasa a dining"

Kamar yace ta d'auko ta kawo masa nan amma saiya fasa ya tashi ya nufi falon can 'kasa.

Yana kammalawa yaji ana kiran salla dan haka ya fice ya tafi masallaci.

Khairat naji ankira sallah ta tashi ta tafi d'akin ta tanayin sallah ta sanya kayan bacci, wani mugun bacci takeji saboda yau bata samu yin baccin rana sosai ba.

Shirin 'kwanciya take ta tuna Sadeeq bai d'auki maganin sa ba, sarai tasan halinsa sai ya'ki shan maganin musamman da yaga yau jikin nashi yayi normal har yawo ya samu damar fita.

Fitowa tayi taga baya falon sama, sakkowa tayi kasan nanma bayanan, 'kofar falon ta duba taga an rufe ta ciki, nan ta tabbatar ya dawo yana d'akin sa, komawa saman tayi kai tsaye ta nufi d'akin sa ta tura kofar had'i da sallama.

Zaune ta sameshi bakin gado yana danna laptop, tsayawa tayi tana kallon sa da alama baiji sallamar da tayi ba,sake mai maitawa.

Amsawa yayi sannan ya d'ago ya kalleta, 'kura mata idanu yayi harta 'karaso inda yake, har lokacin kallon ta yake, iska ta hura masa tace    "ya dai Yaya sauya maka nayi ne??"

Wayan cewa yayi yay murmushi yace "nop ina mamakin yadda kika sakko cikin sau'ki ne"

Bata rai tayi tace "au na manta ma fushi nake dakai barina koma" tana fad'a ta juya.

Hannun ta ya ri'ko tsayawa tayi tana murmushi amma bata juyoba.

Mi'kewa yayi ya tako zuwa gab da ita ta yadda har sunajin numfashin juna, runtse idanun ta tayi tanajin wani irin abu yana shigarta tun daga kanta har zuwa yatsun 'kafafunta.

Ganin ta runtse idanun ta ne yabashi damar 'kare mata kallo tun daga fuska har zuwa jikin ta, murmushi yayi yana yaba yadda kayan bacci sukayi masifar yimata 'kyau, wani irin shaukin soyayyar ta yakeji yana shiga kowace kafa da jini yake gudana a cikin jikin sa, 'kurawa face d'in ta idanun sa yayi, a hankali ta soma bu'de idanunta, tas ta bud'esu cikin nashi, murmushi yayi mata, batasan lokacin da itama ta mayar masa ba.

Gaba d'aya ya manta da komai 'kwa'kwalwar shi ta tsaya cak Khairat kawai yake gani, bai san lokacin da yasanya hannayen sa biyu ya rungume ta jikinsa ba tsam ya riketa yana shafa bayan ta.

Hawaye farin ciki  Khairat taji suna bin kuncinta hannu ta sanya ta goge, wani dad'i taji ya ziyarci ruhin ta tabbas zuwa yanzu tafara gasgata maganar maryam, babu wasa tabbas Sadeeq yafara sonta,murmushin mugunta tayi lokacin data tuna wahalar da tasha akan Sadeeq da  irin hawayen da tazubar ta dalilin sa.

Zamewa tayi daga jikin sa, sake ri'kota yayi,gani kamar ba'a hayyacin sa yake bane ya sanya da sauri tace  " yaya zan koma d'akina ne fah"

Sai a lokacin komai ya fara dawo masa dai dai, sakinta yayi ya koma bakin gadon ya zauna, harta juya zata tafi ta tuna da abinda ya kawota d'akinsa, dawo wa tayi ta nufi inda yake ajje maganin ta d'auko sannan ta had'o da ruwa, ajje wa tayi a 'kasa tace   "uhm dama nazo na tuna maka shan maganin ka ne"

Hannu kawai ya mi'ka mata yana kallon ta, da sauri ta bud'e maganin ta b'allo masa ta mika masa da ruwan yana shanyew ta mi'ka masa ragowar shima ya shanye, mamaki ya bata ko wannan b'ata ran dayake idan zaisha magani yanzu baiyiba.

'Kwashe magungunan tayi ta mai dasu inda ta d'auko, har lokacin kallon ta kawai yakeyi,  a xuciyarta tace anya kuwa wannan lafiyar sa d'aya??    Komawa tayi kusa dashi tace  "yaya ko bakajin dad'i ne??"

Murmushi yayi yace  "babu komai maganin dana sha ne"

"Owk Allah ya 'kara lafiya" tana fad'a ta mi'ke tace "sai da safe ni na tafi"

"Allah ya tashe mu lafiya" kawai ya fad'a ta fice daga d'akin.


*****************************

Haka dai rayuwa taci gaba da tafiya, gaba d'aya Sadeeq ya matsawa kansa da tunanin shin khairat zata yadda su cigaba da zamansu kuwa??       Wani mugu mugun sonta yakeji yarass gane maiyake damun sa,gashi ko wanda zai bashi shawara bashi dashi, harga Allah baxai iya zuwa wajan Abdul ba saboda bai manta yadda sukayi dashi akan maganar Khairat d'in ba,


Yau ta kama saura 'kwana 2 30dys d'in da Sadeeq ya d'iba musu ya cika, tunda ya fita office ya kasa zaune ya kasa tsaye, daga 'karshema tattarawa yayi yabar hospital d'in, gida yaso ya koma amma bazai iya komawa ba, 'kwana 2 Khairat ta isheshi da tambayar meyake damunsa yake wata muguwar rama, tayi juyin duniya amma yace mata babu komai.

Wani lokacin sai yaji kamar ya fito ya fad'a mata kawai a wuce wajan koshima ya samu 'kwanciyar hankali, fargabar sa d'aya shine amsar da khairat zata bashi.

Yanke shawarar zuwa ya gaida mom d'insa yayi saboda yasan yanzu Dady yana office, yana gama wannan tunanin ya nufi gidan nasu.

Mamaki momy tayi ganin sa a wannan lokacin kallon sa tayi tace " bakaje aiki bane yau?"

"Naje Momy kaina ke ciwo shiyasa na dawo"

"Toh Allah ya sawwa'ke"

"Ameen"

"Amma ai da kasani gidanka ka wuce ka 'kwanta kad'an huta"

"Mom gani nayi 'kwana 2 banxo ba shiyasa nace gwara na fara zuwa na dubaki"

"Toh ka kyauta kuwa"

Aisha Momy ta 'kira, bata jima ba ta fito ta gaishe da Sadeeq. yana amsawa tace  "yaya yana kayi wani mugun rama ne?"

Harara ya watsa mata yace "ina ruwanki?"

Zatayi magana Momy tace "kinga ba surutu na kiraki kiyi ba jeki kitchen ki d'auko masa kunun ayar nan danayi d'azu"

"Toh Momy"

Bata jima da tafiya ba ta dawo hannun ta d'auke da jug, tazo gaban shi ta ajje ta tsiyaya masa, d'auka yayi yanasha ita kuma ta koma d'akinta saboda karatu take.

Momy ce ta 'kare masa kallo tace "Sadeeq dan Allah karka b'oyemin ka fad'amin abinda yake  damunka"

ajje kofin hannun sa yayi yace " mom nifa babu abinda yake  damuna, me kika gani kikayi wannan maganar? "

Karkad'a kai tayi tace "haba Sadeeq karka manta ni mahaifiyar kace ko kallon ka nayi saina fahimci a wane hali kake ciki, bare kuma irin wannan ramar da kayi tayi yawa"

Kallon ta yayi tabbas bazai iya yiwa Momy 'karya ba amma bazai iya fad'a mata abinda yake damunsa ba.

Ganin yayi shuru ne tace "kayimin shuru bayan nasan kaji menace"

Sai da ya sauke nan nauyar ajiyar zuciya  sannan yace  "tabbas momy babu 'karya ina cikin wani hali wanda yake sanya ni cikin damuwa, amma dan Allah mom ki gafarce ni karki matsa a kan na fad'a miki abinda yake damuna, addu'ar ki kawai bake bukata"

"Shikenan tunda kace haka bazan matsa maka ba,amma inaso ka rage damuwa ka dage da addu'a komai yayi zafi maganin sa Allah"

Haka Momy tayita yimasa addu'a har lokacin da akayi sallah sannan ya baro gidan.

 *Page   32* 



 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_


Tunda ya doso gidan yake jin wata irin fad'uwar gaba, innalillahi wa inna ilaiyhir raju'un yaketa mai maitawa, saboda besan dalilin fad'uwar gaban dayake jiba, a haka dai ya 'karasa gidan yayi parking motar sa ya shiga gidan.

Yana shiga yaga bai ganta a parlours d'in ba nan ya tabbatar tana d'akinta, d'akin sa ya wuce yayi sallar la'asar ya watsa ruwa ya sauya kaya,sannan ya fito.

Dai dai lokacin khairat ta fito daga d'akin ta kallon sa tayi tace.

"Yaya dama ka dawo?"

"Eh ban jima da shigowa ba na duba ban ganki ba sai nashiga wanka"

Kauda kanta gefe tayi tace

"Uhm wai baka ganni ba kawai kace ka duba iya inda kasan zaka iya"

Kallon ta kawai yayi batare da yace mata komai ba ya samu kujera ya zauna ya lumshe idanun sa.

Sakkowa tayi ta nufi kitchen ta d'auko kulolin abincin taxo ta jeresu a dining sannan ta d'auko plates da drinks d'inda ta hada musu.

Ta kammalawa tayi masa magana, tasowa yayi ya janyo kujera ya zauna,sannan tayi serving d'insu  suka fara ci.

***********************

Da dare wajan misalin 8:30pm yana zaune a falo yana kallo bayan sun gama cin abincin dare, Khairat ce ta fito daga d'akin ta sanye da kayan bacci jikinta,tana 'karasowa ta zauna a kujerar da yake, remote ta d'auka ta sauya tasha domin taga alamar ba kallon ya keyi ba.

Tana sauyawa ya juyo ya kalleta, itama kallon sa tayi tace    "ai naga ba kallon kakeyi ba shiyasa na sauya"

"Toh" kawai yace mata, bai sake magana ba

Jitayi zaman baimata dad'i ba gashi a doguwar kujerar suke bazata iya cewa ya tashi ba,kallon sa tayi taga TV yake kallo amma da alama ba anan hankalin sa yakeba, 'kwanciya tayi ta d'ora kanta a cinyar sa, gani tayi baima san me takeyi ba da alama yayi a yawan tunanin da yakeyi 'kwana biyu.

A hankali ta shiga kiran sunan sa,da saurin ya kalleta itama kallon sa tayi tace  "yaya dan Allah tunanin me kakeyi haka?"

"Babu komai" ya bata amsa a ta'kaice.

"Kawai dai kace bazaka fad'amin ba, amma duk Wanda ya ganka yasan kana cikin damuwa musamman ma wannan muguwar ramar da kayi, dan Allah yaya kacire damuwa a zuciyar ka "

Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke yace "nasan da cewar ina cikin damuwa amma bazan iya fad'a miki ba, fatana gareki shine ki tayani da addu'a"

A zuciyar ta cewa tayi dan kaga na damune kaje kayi ta ri'ke damuwur ka karka fad'a d'in.

A fili kuwa cewa tayi  "toh insha Allah zan tayaka da addu'a Allah ya yaye maka"

"Ameen"

Kusan 20mins babu wanda ya kuma magana a cikin su, can ta kalleshi tace  "yaya"

Tunda ta kira sunan shi yaji wani irin fad'uwar gaba,wadda baisan dalilin ta ba, saida tasake kiran sa sannan ya amsa.

Shuru tayi sannan ta fara magana      "yaya kasan yau saura 'kwana 2 ya rage mana, amma kuma banji kana maganar ba ya kamata ace ka fara shirin tunkarar su Dady,duk abin da ka tsara zaka fad'a musu nayi al'kawarin zan baka had'in kai 100%.

Tunda ta fara magana yaji kansa ya fara sarawa runtse idanun sa yayi harta kammala baimasan abinda take cewa ba.

Ganin yayi mata shuru ne ya sanya ta ce    " yaya ba kace komai ba?"

Sai da ya tattaro nutsuwar sa sannan yace  "amma dai bakiga 'kwanakin sun cika ba batare da nayi wani abuba koh?"

"Nasan da haka amma kuma yaya kasan.. . "

Hannu kawai ya d'aga mata, sannan ya janye kanta daga cinyar sa ya mi'ke tsaye 

"Ni xanje na 'kwanta sai da safe"   yana gama fad'a ya wuce d'akin sa ya Bart's baki bud'e.

Gaba daya mamaki ya bata mai yake nufi?   Wani tunanin tayi take ta saki wani killer simile ta tashi ta kashe TV ta wuce d'akin ta.

Ta b'angaran Sadeeq kuwa yana shiga d'akin ya fad'a gado tare da dafe kansa dayaji kamar zai cire, gaba d'aya ya rasa me zeyi tawace hanya zai b'ullowa Khairat gashi bayaso ko d'aya iyayan su susan da maganar yafiso suyita ta 'kare dagashi sai ita amma da alama Khairat bazata fahimce shiba.

Har wajan 2:00am yana zaune ya kasa runtsawa juyi kawai yake yana Neman mafuta, ganin bashida wata mafuta ne ya sanya ya mi'ke ya shiga toilet ya d'auro alwala yazo ya fara jero nafila.

Yana zaune kan darduma har akayi assalatu, Malam na tada sallah ya tashi shima yayi sallah yana idarwa ya tashi yaje ya 'kwanta bai jima ba bacci ya 'kwashe shi.

Sai wajan 10:00am ya tashi,damuwa da yake ciki ta ragu babu laifi, sai dai wani ciwon kai daya tashi dashi,a daddafe yashiga toilet yayi wanka ya shirya.

Khairat na zaune a falo bayan ta kammala gyara gidan ta gama had'a break fast, ta mikawa baba mai gadi nasa ta zauna tana jiran fitowar Sadeeq, mamaki takeyi na ganin time d'in da yakai bai fitoba,kodai yau ba zaije aiki bane, tana wannan tunanin ya fito sanye cikin 'kananan kaya,yayi kyau sosai amma kana kallon shi zaka tabbatar yana cikin damuwa.

'Kamshin turaran sa taji da sauri ta d'ago sukayi 2 eyes dashi yana tsaye jikin 'kofar d'akin sa ya zuba hannayen sa cikin aljihu yana kallon ta.

Wani 'kyau yaga tayi masa,tana ganin sa ta mi'ke ta nufo inda yake, kallon kayan jikin ta yayi yaga riga  da wando ne amma wandon ba dogon ne ba, tana 'karasowa ta gaidashi ya amsa sannan tace   "Yaya yau bazakaje hospital bane?"

"'Kanwata wlh yau banajin dad'i ne"

"Allah sarki Yaya Allah ya baka lpy"

"Ameen"

"Amma dan Allah Yaya ka daina sanya damuwa a ranka nidai nasan ba haka kawai kake fama da ciwo 'kwana biyun nan ba"

"Hakane na fad'a miki addu'ar ki kawai nake bu'kata"

"Toh Allah ya rufa asiri"

"Ameen"


Wunin ranar haka yayi shi bashida mafuta dan yasan gobe 'karyar shi zata 'kare.

Khairat taso ta sake tuna masa amma saita tuna abinda yace mata jiya datayi masa magana, dan haka ta 'kyaleshi zuwa goben tayi taga wane irin tsari zaiywa su Dady, 

Da daddare ko bacci batayi ba saboda batasan wane hukunci zai yanke ba saboda sai a yanzu take dad'a jin sonshi yana ratsa ko ina na jikin ta ganin bata da  mafuta ne ya sanya ta tashi ta d'auro alwala ta fara nafila.

Ta bangaran Sadeeq ma hakan ce ta kasance daga 'karshe   shima alwala ya d'auro ya tada sallah.


Da sassafe Sadeeq ya tashi ya shirya ya  d'auki key d'in motar sa ya fice daga gidan,domin in  Khairat ta tashi baisan abinda zai fad'a mata ba....



 _Toh su Sadeeq ko ina za'aje🤔_

 *Page   33* 



 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_



Yana fita daga gidan yayi parking d'in motar yana tunani.

Zuwa can ya yanke shawarar zuwa wajan Abdul saboda babu yadda zaiyi bashida wani wanda xai bashi shawara akan wannan maganar sai Abdul dole ya jure duk wani abu da Abdul zaiyi masa saboda tun farko shiya jawa kansa.

Yana gama tunanin ya figi motar sa sai gidan su Abdul.

Yana yin horn mai gadi ya bud'e masa yana fad'in "yallab'ai kaine a gidan da sassafe haka?"

"Wlh nine fatan dai Abdul yana ciki?"

"Eh yana ciki"

"Toh shikenan na gode"

Yana fad'a ya wuce yayi parking motar sa ya nufi b'angaran da Abdul yake.

Yana shiga d'akin yayi turs ganin Abdul a 'kudun dune da bargo yana waya sai wani faman murmushi yake kamar tana ganin sa.

Wani 'karamin tsaki Sadeeq yayi sannan ya shiga d'akin ya zauna a gefen gadon yana fad'in "kaga Malam ka kashe wannan wayar karka b'atamin rai, saboda asara mutum ya zauna yana waya da sassafe"

Cire wayar yayi a kunnen sa yana fad'in "ohhh kaine da sassafe haka?"

Wayar ya mayar kunnen sa sukayi sallama ya juyo wajan Sadeeq suka gaisa sannan yace

"Mutumin lafiya dai naganka da sassafe haka"

Sosa 'keya yayi yana magana cikin in'ina "uhm.....uh..dama magana nakeso zamuyi da kai"

Tashi Abdul yayi yace "magana kuma?"

"Game da me kenan?"

Shuru Sadeeq yayi ya kasa magana, har sai da Abdul ya sake magana.

Cikin sanyin murya Sadeeq yace "dama akan maganar Khairat ne"

Wani kallo Abdul ya watsa masa yace "kamar yaya"

Gyara zama Sadeeq yayi yace   "haba abokina wai ka manta ne yau kwanakin dana d'ibawa Khairat a  gidana yacika fah"

"Ohhh ai na manta, ka sake d'in ne?"

"A'a"

Tashi Abdul yayi yace "ni zan shiga gida na gaida su Hajiya nayi shirin tafiya office"

Hannu sa Sadeeq ya rik'o yace  "haba Abdul amma dai kasan magana nazo muyi da kai akan wannan maganar"

Juyowa Abdul yayi yace  "wai ni mezan iya yimaka gameda wannan kawai kaje ka aikata abinda ka tsara tun farko"

Nannauyar ajiya zuciya Sadeeq ya sauke yace  "Abdul inaso ka sani a yanxu baxan iya rabuwa da Khairat ba, saboda ta riga da tazama wani b'angare na jiki na, wlh Abdul bazan iya rabuwa da Khairat ba"

Tunda ya fara magana Abdul yake kallon sa yana murmushi har zuwa lokacin daya tsaya,sannan Abdul yace

"Haba Sadeeq ai bani ya kamata ka kawowa wannan maganar ba,koka manta.....

Dakatar dashi Sadeeq yayi yana fad'in  " "dan Allah Abdul mubar wannan maganar ina cikin damuwa ka taimaka min mu ajje waccan maganar wlh bansan inda zan sanya kaina ba idan na rasa Khairat"

Zama Abdul yayi ya dafa kafad'ar Sadeeq yace  "shikenan na fahimta, amma yanxu ka sanarwa da ita Khairat d'in?"

"A'a ban fad'a mata ba"

" toh yanzu abinda za'ayi shine ka daurewa zuciyar ka ka tunkari Khairat ka sanar mata, amma fa   karma kaje da shirin zata amince maka a yanxu saboda Khairat ta kwashi shekaru tana wahala akan sonka amma baka tab'a tausaya mata ba,don haka saika bita a sannu sau'kin ta d'aya daya kasan ce Khairat tana sonka"

Ajjiyar xuciya Sadeeq yayi yace "shikenan zan jarraba amma wlh har tsoron tunkarar ta nake"

Dariya Abdul yayi sannan ya dad'a 'karfafa wa Sadeeq guywa akan maganar sai wajan 9:30 Sadeeq yabar gidan.

**************************


B'angaran Khairat kuwa tunda ta tashi da asuba ta kasa komawa tunanin abinda zata tarar anjima kawai take, gaba d'aya ta rasa inda zata sanya ranta,  fargaba tsoro had'i da matsanaiciyar fad'uwar gaba ce ta ziyar ceta.

Tana zaune taji fitar motar Sadeeq, da sauri tami'ke ta le'ka mai gadi ta hango yana rufe gate d'in, sakin labulan tayi tana fad'in    innalillahi wa'inna ilaihir raj'un  can kuma ta dafe kai na shiga uku badai gurin su Dady Sadeeq zai tafi ba?

Hawaye taji ya fara bin kuncinta barina wajan window d'in tayi ta zame bakin gado tana sakin wani irin kuka mai tsuma rai.

Ta jima a haka zuwa can taji wayar ta tana ringing, hannun ta mi'ka ta d'auko wayar sunan maryam tagani a kan screen d'in, da sauri ta share hawayen ta ta amsa wayar tarda sallama

Daga can b'angaran maryam ta amsa tana  fad'in  " sis tun d'azu nakeson kiran ki tun jiya ko bacci na kasa"

Kuka Khairat ta kashe  dashi tana fad'in  "sis na shiga uku sakina Sadeeq zeyi" ta 'karashe maganar tana d'ora hannun ta d'aya a kai.

Salati maryam tayi a razane tace  "kamar yaya ban fahimta ba"

Tana kuka take sanar mata tun d'azu taga Sadeeq d'in ya fita.

Ajjiyar xuciya maryam ta saki tace  "ohhh harna samu d'an sau'ki, abinda xa'ayi mujira ya dawo"

"Toh shikenan zuwa anjima zan kiraki"

Suna gama wayar ta tashi ta koma falon kasa, ko break fast takasa shiga kitchen ta had'a bare kuma ta gyara falon,ko kayan bacci bata cireba ta samu guru ta zauna.

Takai kusan 1hrs taji an bud'e kofar da sauri ta mi'ke suka had'a ido da Sadeeq..

 *Page   34* 



 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_




Takai kusan 1hrs taji an bud'e kofa, da sauri ta mi'ke suka had'a ido da Sadeeq, kallon ta yayi yaga ko kayan bacci bata cireba, hasali ma kanta ko hula babu sai gashinta daya zubo kafad'ar ta baki sid'ik, itama kallon sa kawai take tana jira taji yace tashiga ciki ta shirya tazo su taf.

'Karasowa falon yayi ya zauna kan kujerar dake kusa da ita, kusan 2mns amma bata zauna ba kuma bata daina kallon sa ba,ganin batada niyyar zama ne ya sannan ya janyo hannun ta ya zaunar da ita, ya 'kura mata idanu na tsawon lokaci sannan yace

"Meyake faruwa naganki haka?"

"Bubu komai" ta fad'a tareda sunkuyar da kanta.

"Toh ina break fast d'ina?" ya fad'a yana kallo ta.

"Nima banci ba saboda banyi ba"

"Uhm meya hanaki shiga kitchen ki had'a break fast bayan nasan babu abunda babu a cikin gidan?"

Shuru kawai tayi masa saboda batasan mezata cemasa ba, shima kallon ta kawai yake saboda ya rasa tawace hanya zai fara yimata magana, shiyasa ya 'bige da tambayar break fast?

Zuwa can yace "tambayar kifa nayi amma kinyi shiru"

Turo baki tayi tace  "toh bakai nake jira kazo mu tafi gida ba, nasan acan zamuyi break fast kafin ka sanar musu abinda ya kawo mu,kuma ma ni banajin yunwar"

Fad'uwar gaba maganar tata ta sanya shi, nan danan hankalin sa yakuma tashi, a zuciyar sa ya furta   ya salam  anya kuwa zan samu nasara akan yarinyar nan kuwa?  gaba d'aya ita jirama kawai take nace shirya mu tafi, anya kuwa Khairat bata daina sona ba?

Gani yayi bashida wata mafita data wuce ya sanar da ita, dan haka ya daure yayi ta maza yace  

"Toh ai mun fasa zuwa gidan"

Tashi tayi daga zaunen da take tana fad'in  "kamar yaya ban gane ba?"

Tashi yayi shima ya matsa gab da ita ya ri'ko hannayen ta cikin nashi cikin sanyi murya ya fara magana   " Khairat dan Allah inaso ki manta da duk wani abu daya faru tsakani na dake, da wanda ya faru a gidan nan da wanda ya faru a da can baya kafin muyi aure, wlh muddin nayi kuskuran rabuwa dake a wannan lokacin nasan bazan iya cigaba da rayuwa ba, Khairat kin riga da kin zama wani sashe na jikina wlh muddin na rasa tabbas kuma saikun rasani gaba d'aya, wlh ina sonki Khairat  I rally love u my Khairat".

Tunda ya fara magana Khairat take jin wani mugu mugun farin ciki yana mamaye zuciyar ta, musamman ma kalaman sa na 'karshe.

A zahiri kuwa wani mugun kallo ta watsa  masa sannan ta fizge hannun ta daga cikin nashi, ta matsa nesa dashi tana dad'a kallon sa amma kuma ta kasa magana.

Sake matsowa yayi ya ri'ko hannun ta yana fad'in   "dan Allah Khairat karki 'ki amincewa dani,wlh gaskiya ta nake fad'a miki har cikin raina, bazan ita rayuwa babu keba"

Sake fizge hannun ta tayi,saboda wani tunani datayi game da fitar da yayi a safiyar nan, sannan yazo mata da wannan maganar, tana gama tunanin ta fara magana  rai 'bace.

"Sadeeq karka maidani 'karamar yarinya mana, kodan kaga inada 'kananan shekarun da basu haura 20 ba shine xakayi wasa da hankali na?"

"Ohhh yanzu na fahimce ka yadda ya kamata,wato kaje gida wajan su Dady a safiyar nan baka samu goyon bayan suba, shine zaka zo kayimin dad'in baki da wai kana sona, dacan bakasan da hakan ba kenan sai yanxu?"

"Toh wlh tun wuri ka sauya tunani, domin ni bazanci gaba da zama da kai a cikin gidan nan ba, tun wuri na fad'a maka baka da matsala ta 'bangare na zan baka had'in kai dai-dai gwargwadon iyawa ta a wajan su Dady idan munje, don haka bana bu'katar 'bata lokaci na a kanka yanzu"

Mamaki Khairat ta bashi musamman Sadeeq d'in da ta kirashi dashi, babu ko kara na matsayin sa a wajan ta koda kuwa babu batun aure tsakanin su,  da sauri yabar wannan tunanin a ransa, saboda ba shine a gaban sa ba, kuma ko mai Khairat tayi bazaiga laifina ta ba, saboda shi kanshi yasan ya cancan ci hakan.

Dafe kansa yayi yace  "dan Allah Khairat ki daina wannan maganar, wlh ni ko gida banjeba, na dad'e inaso na sanar miku wannan maganar, amma kuma saina kasa, dan Allah.....

Dakatar dashi tayi tana fad'in  " wlh Sadeeq baxan saurari wannan maganar taka ba, zan shiga d'aki na shirya, kafin na fito kasan abin yin dan na rantse maka yau baxan kwana gidan ka ba"  tana kaiwa nan ta juya tanufi d'akin ta.

Bayan ta kawai Sadeeq yabi da kallo, tana gama hawa steps d'in ya zauna a kujera yana mai dafe kansa,gaba d'aya ya rasa mezai yiwa Khairat ta aminta dashi, yanke shawarar zaiyi mata duk abinda takeso yayi amma banda saki.

Yana xaune a wajan ta fito hannun ta d'auke da wata 'karamar jaka zataci kaya set 4, kallon ta  kawai yake harta 'karaso inda yake ta tsaya tana watsa masa harara,kallon ta yayi yace  "toh yanxu mekike so?"

Haka kawai taji yabata tausayin, saboda ita koda ace na 1mounth d'in xata sake zama dashi tana so,amma ya zama dole ta horashi,domin ta fahimci gaskiya yake fad'a mata,musamman data  tuna halin da take ganin sa a ciki kwana biyu.

Dakewa tayi tace  "gidan mu zaka kaini"

Ba tare daya tanka mata ba ya amshi jakar hannun ta yayi gaba ta bishi a baya,suna isa bakin mota ya bud'e mata 'kofa ta shige shima yashiga yana motar suka fice daga gidan...

 *Page   35* 



 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_



Tafe suke a hanya amma babu wanda yayiwa d'an uwansa magana, Khairat jitake kamar tace masa su koma amma kuma bazata iya ba, tambayar kanta take me shurun da yayi yake nufi? baice mata komai ba kawai ya biye mata ya d'auko ta anya kuwa ba wani abin ya shirya ba?.

Bata gama tsorata ba sai dataga yanufi anguwar su,hankalin tane ya dad'ac tashi kamar ta tsai dashi  amma sai ta dake.

Yana zuwa bakin gate d'in yayi horn gateman yaxo ya bud'e masa,suna shiga harabar gidan yayi parking motar sa, kusan 5mins babu wanda yayi magana a cikin su, sannan itama Khairat bata bud'e motar ta fita ba, ganin shurun yayi yawa ne ya sanya Sadeeq cewa

"Ya kamata ki fito mu shiga ciki"

Wani kallo tabishi dashi kamar xatayi magana sai kuma ta fasa ta bud'e motar ta fita,shima fitowa yayi daga motar ya wuce gaba tabishi a baya, gaban ta yana dukan uku-uku.

Suna shiga falon suka iske Momcy ita kad'ai a falo tana kallo, da sallama suka shiga falon.

Momcy na d'agowa ta gansu cike da fara'a ta amsa,k'arasawa sukayi suka samu guri suka zauna,

Sadeeq ne ya fara gaishe da Momcy, sannan itama Khairat ta gaishe da ita, 

Momcy ce ta kallesu tace "lpy kuwa Sadeeq nagan ku da wurwuri haka, bakaje aiki bane?"

A tsorace Khairat ta kalleshi taji wace amsa zai bawa Momcy,shima kallon ta yayi ya kauda kansa yace

"Dama Momcy tun jiya aka yimin waya akan cewar yau zamuje abuja ne saboda wata tattaunawa da zamuyi da wasu manyan doctors da zasuzo daga dubai, kuma sai gobe zamu dawo, shiyasa nace barina kawota idan na dawo saina zo na d'auke ta"

Salati Momcy tayi tace  "Sadeeq yaushe ka fara biyewa shirmen Khairat ban sani ba?"

"Kwana d'aya kawai shine saika biye mata ka kwasota ka kawota gida, wai baxata iya kwana ita kadai ba"

"Momcy wlh ba haka bane, hasali ma ba ita tace na kawota ba ninace mata ta fito zamuje anguwa, sai da muka shigo anguwar sannan ta gane nan zan kawota"

"Babban dalilin dayasa na kowata ma shine batada lafiya fa Momcy"

Ajjiyar zuciya Momcy ta sauke tace "ayya ai ban sani bane kuma ko jiya saida mukayi waya da ita, amma bata fad'amin bata da lafiya ba"

Duk wannan maganar da suke kawai kallon su Khairat takeyi, gaba d'aya ta kasa gane mai Sadeeq yake nufi, muryar shi taji yana fad'in.

"Momcy ni zan tafi saboda jirana akeyi"

"Toh Sadeeq Allah ya kaiku lpy, yaba da sa'a akan abinda akaje yi"

"Ameen Momcy saina dawo"

Kallon Khairat tayi tace  "ke kuma dakika zauna kin abin mutane da idanu bazaki tashi kije ki rakashi bane?"

"Dama naga wani miskilan ci kikeji tunda kuka shigo bayan gaisuwa ko a baki sake furta wa ba"

Mikewa tayi tana cinno baki ta fice daga falon.

A jikin mota ta sameshi ya rungume hannayen shi a 'kirjin sa, a hankali ta 'karaso inda yake ta tsaya batare da ta kalleshi ba.

Sai da suka jima a haka sannan ya fara magana

"Khairat badan wani abu na 'daukoki na kawoki gida ba, sai don furucin da kikayi a d'azu"

"Kinyi rantsuwa kan cewar wlh yau bazaki tab'a kwana cikin gidana ba, saboda haka bazan matsa miki ba, akan cewar saikin kwana acikin gidana yau.

" sannan inaso ki dad'a tabbatar wa da kanki iyayenmu basu san da wannan maganar ba, kamar yadda kikayi tunani akan cewar na fad'a musu sun'ki amincewa shiyasa na dawo gareki"

"Inaso kisan cewar wlh Khairat babu wasa a cikin magana ina sonki fiyeda yadda kike tsammani, baxan iya cigaba da rayuwa ba matu'kar kika gujeni, dan Allah ki rufamin asiri gobe idan na dawo kitaho mu koma gida, karki bari su Dady susan da wannan maganar, amma idan har kina jin baxaki iya cigaba da zama da niba bazan matsa miki ba, duk abinda kikeso shi zanyi miki"

Tunda ya fara magana take hawaye, yana kaiwa aya ya tsaya yana jiran abinda zata fad'a.

Magana takeso tayi amma ta kasa kuka ne yaci 'karfin ta da gudu ta juya ta shige gida,tana shiga falon ta zube a kujera tana kuka.  da sauri Momcy ta taso tazo kusa da ita ta janyo ta jikin ta tana fad'in

"Lafiyar ki kuwa Khairat kike kuka haka meya faru"

Sai a lokacin Khairat ta tuna da kwabar da takeso tayi musu, dan haka ta tsaida kukan nata cikeda shagwab'a ta cinno baki tana fad'in   " Momcy ba Yaya Sadeeq bane kina kallo ya tafi kuma wai kwana xaiyi"

Salati Momcy tayi tana fad'in "oh ni 'yasu haka kika koma dama, wai saboda miji ya tafi kike kuka ko kunya babu"

Tashi tayi daga jiki Momcy tana dariya ta shige d'akinta na da, domin ita dariya Momcy ta bata tayi tunanin da gaske kenan take.

Shi kuwa Sadeeq gajiya yayi da tsaiwa ya shige motar sa, yana zama yaga jakar kayan Khairat bata d'auka ba, murmushi kawai yayi ya figi motar ya fice daga gidan.

Khairat na shiga d'aki ta kwanta akan gadon ta tana goge rogowar hawayen dayake bin face d'in ta, wani irin farin ciki takeji yana mamaye duk ilahirin jikin ta, hannu ta daga sama tana godewa Allah, tabbas ta dad'e da cire rai daga samun soyayyar Sadeeq, sai gashi lokaci d'aya Allah ya amshi addu'ar ta, datafi shekaru biyar tanayin su, duk daran duniya saita tashi tayi nafila ta ro'ki Allah akan soyayyar Sadeeq, dare d'aya sai gashi yau Allah ya amshi addu'ar ta, wani hawayen farin ciki taji yasake zubowa kuncin ta.

Wayar ta ta d'auka ta kira number maryam, bugu d'aya ta amsa, dama abinda taketa jira kenan, a gaggauce tace   "beasty yaya kukayi dashi ne tun d'azu nake jiran 'kirwnki"

"Kinga maganar bata waya bace, kawai kizo kisameni ina gidanmu"

A rud'e maryam tace  "gidanmu kuma Khairat?"

Dariya Khairat tayi tace   "kinga malama kawai ki tashi ki taho" tana fad'a ta kashe wayar ta, ta mi'ke ta fito parlour.

A kitchen ta iske Momcy tana shirin fara girkin abincin rana,shiga tayi tana fad'in  "Momcy wai yanaga tunda nazo banga su sageer bane?"

Momcy na kunna gas. tace mata  "kin manta yau akwai school ne?"

"Oh Momcy na sha'fa ne"

"Ai dole ki sha'afa tunda kinzama mara kunya, nifa mamaki kika bani wai miji kike yiwa kuka, ke 'yar dad'i miji"

Rufe fuskar ta tayi da hannu tana fad'in   "Momcy nidai ki daina fad'a kar Dady ya dawo yajimu, yace banida kunya"

Wani kallo Momcy ta bita dashi tace  "wato nuce rasa kunya wacce ba'ajjn Kunyar ta kenan?"

"Momcy ba haka bane kinsan ai ita uwa tamkar 'kawa take"

shiga kitchen d'in tayi, suna fira suna girki, dad'i kamar ya kashe Momcy ganin yadda Khairat ke cikin farin ciki, sai godewa Allah take ganin 'yarta bata samu matsalar da tayi tunanin zata fuskan ta a gidan Sadeeq bayan auran su ba.

(Nikam cewa nayi sun ninke ki bai bai ne)

Sun kusa kammala girkin kenan, sukaji sallamar Mary am, ai da gudu Khairat ta fito daga kitchen d'in ta rungume Maryam tana dariya.

Kallon ta maryam kawai take har suka 'karaso, Maryam ta gaida Momcy, cikin zolaya Momcy ta kalli Maryam tace

"Uhm Maryam tuna akayi bikin Khairat rabon da kizomin sai yau data guntsa miki tana nan gidan"

"Dariya tayi tana fad'in wlh Momcy ba haka bane, school ce take b'oye mu, yanxuma daga can nabiyo nan"

" toh Allah ya taimaka". Momcy ta fad'a,  a tare sukace "ameen"  sannan suka wuce d'akin Khairat.

Suna shiga suka zauna bakin gado, suna zama Maryam tace  "Khairat fad'amin meya faru naganki a gida kuma?"

Dariya Khairat tayi sannan ta kwashe duk yadda sukayi da Sadeeq ta fad'a mata.

Ajiyar zuciya Maryam ta sauke tace  "kema dai kinada matsala meye na saurin rantsuwa kuma?"

"Amma ai da sau'ki tunda gobe zaki koma,  ni wlh harma ya fara bani tausayi"

"Wlh besty nima badan komai na ha'kura na dake ya kawoni gida ba, sai dan yadda nake jin tausayin sa, da yadda nake jin 'kaunar sa a raina bazasu barni na juya masa baya ba  lokacin dayake min magiyar sa"

"Kinfi kowa sanin yadda nakeso Sadeeq, ni kad'ai nasan farin cikin da nake ciki yau, amma yazama dole Sadeeq ya amshi gashi a wajena, domin ya tabbatar nima macace kamar kowace mace, bawai na biye masa kawai ba, daga baya ya d'auke ni shasha"

"Hakane amma ayi masa a hankali don wallahihi ni tausayin sa nakeji yanxu"

Haka sukayi ta fira Maryam bata bar gidan ba sai misalin 5:00pm.

Dady yana dawowa Momcy ta sanar masa zuwan Khairat, fad'a shima ya fara yi Momcy ta sanar masa ai Khairat d'in ce batada lafiya, sannan yayi shuru.

************************

Washe gari saida akayi sallar ishsha'i sannan Sadeeq yaxo d'aukar Khairat, tana d'akin ta Momcy ta shigo ta fad'a mata ta fito su tafi, bata musaba ta d'auko hijab d'inta ta fito.

Tana fitowa yabita da idanu, 'kasa tayi da kanta ta'ki bari su had'a ido, nan sukayi wa Momcy da  Dady sallama suka tafi.

A mota ma babu wanda yayiwa d'an uwansa magana, suna shiga gidan ko parking bai gama gyara wa ba ta fice a motar ta shige gidan, Kai tsaye ta shige d'akinta..

 *Page   36* 



 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_




Suna shiga gidan ko parking bata jira ya gamayi ba ta fice daga motar, tana shiga gidan kai tsaye d'akinta ta shige.

Zama yayi jim a motar yana mamakin khairat, zuwacan ya fito ya rufe kofar, ya nufi cikin gidan.

Tunda yaga bai sameta a parlourn ba ya tabbatar tana d'akin ta, tashi yayi ya shiga d'akin sa yayi wanka, sannan ya sanya kayan da zai kwanta dasu, fitowa parlour yayi ya duba ko Khairat ta fito, saboda yanaso suyi magana da ita, wayam yaga parlourn. juyawa yayi ya kalli d'akin ta, kamar ya shiga saboda yanaso suyita ta 'kare da Khairat, tunani ya shigayi wata zuciyar tana bashi shawarar ya shiga, wata kuma tana hanashi, dakewa yayi yacire komai a ransa ya tunkari d'akin nata.

Fitowar ta daga wanka kenan jikin ta d'sure da d'an 'karamin towel iya guywa, tana ji tayi an turo 'kofar da sauri ta d'ago kanta.

Ido biyu sukayi dashi, da sauri ta juya zata koma toilet d'in, da sauri ya 'karasa shigowa ya ri'ko hannun ta kasan cewar basuda nisa.

A razane ta juyo tana kallon sa ido cikin ido, kafin tayi 'kasa da idanun ta ta kalli d'an 'karamin towel d'in dake jikinta tana mammatse cinyoyin ta.

Shima 'kare mata kallo yayi sosai, wani irin ba'kon yanayi yaji yana ziyartar sa, lalubo nutsuwar sa yayi ya dake ya fara yimata magana.

"Haba Khairat meye abin gudu daga ganina?  bawanj abu zan bu'kata gareki ba, kawai magana nakeso muyi"

Janye hannun ta tayi daga nashi tana fad'in   "babu wata magana da xanyi da kai, kawai ka fitar min a d'aki, idan ba haka ba ni zan iya fitar maka daga gidan gaba d'aya"

"Meyayi zafi haka Khairat, zan fitar miki daga d'aki, amma dan Allah sonake ki sanar dani matsayin da kika ajje ni kona samu nutsuwar zuciyata"

Wata uwar harara ta watsa masa tace  "kaga nifa babu wani hukunci dana yanke bayan wanda na sanar dakai jiya, karkaga ban bari iyayenmu sunsan maganar nan ba na biyoka mun dawo, toh ba sauya shawara nayi ba, har yanxu inakan bakana na bazan iya cigaba da zaman aure da kaiba, kuma na biyoka ne kawai saboda nafiso kai da kanka ka d'auke ni ka kaini gaban mahaifin ka da nawa, ka sanar dasu zaka sakeni, saboda dama su suka had'a auran mu"

Cikin tashin hankali yace  "dan Allah ki fad'amin dalilin ki nacewa baxaki iya xaman aure daniba?"

"Saboda baka sona" tabashi amsa a ta'kaice

"Wallahi Khairat ina sonki kece rayuwa.....

Dakatar dashi tayi tana fad'in   " kar muyi haka dakai Sadeeq, na dad'e da sanin baka sona, baka 'kaunata, ada ban yarda baka sona ba, sai ranar danaji ka furta da bakin ka na tabbatar da cewar da gaske baka sona, saboda haka baxan iya yarda da maganar ka ba, da ace labari aka bani, ba da kunnena naji ka fad'a ba shine zan iya 'karyatawa har na amince dakai a yanxu"

Magana yayi niyyar yi ta dakatar dashi  "dan Allah Sadeeq ka fitarmin a d'aki kokuma nina fitar maka daga gidan.

Ba musu ya juya ya fita daga d'akin ya rufe mata 'kofa.

Kai tsaye d'akin sa ya wuce ya zauna bakin gado, yana tunanin yaushe ya ta'ba fad'awa Khairat kalmar baya sonta, shidai iya sanin sa bai ta'ba furta mata makamancin haka ba,  tunawa yayi ta fad'a masa cewar da kunnan ta tajishi yana fad'a,  take kwakwalwar shi ta tuna masa ranar da yake fad'awa Abdul shifa bayason Khairat don haka karya sake damunsa da wannan maganar, tabbatar suna cikin maganar ta shigo parlourn.

Dafe kansa yayi dayaji yana sara masa kamar zai fashe, a fili ya furta     innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraj'un  ya Allah ka kawomin mafita akan wannan lamarin.

Ganin bashida wata mafuta ne yasanya ya tashi ya d'auro alwala ya tada sallah.

Ita kuwa Khairat yana fita taje ta sanya kayan bacci tabi lafiyar gado.


****************************

Washe gari tun da sassafe ta tashi ta gyara gidan, kasan cewar ya 'kwana 2 ba'a gyarashi ba, tana kammalawa tashiga kitchen ta had'a break fast, dining tazo ta jereshi yanda Sadeeq inya fito zai gani, inyaci saiya tafi office dan ita bacci zata koma, 

'Daukar na baba mai gadi tayi ta mi'ka masa, sannan ta dawo ta wuce d'akinta ta 'kwanta.

 *****  ***** 

Misalin 10:am ta tashi, wanka tashiga tayi, ta kimtsa cikin wata pink d'in riga wacce tsayinta bai wuce guywa ba, ta sanya ba'kar hula a kanta, tana kammalawa ta d'auki wayarta ta fito parlour.

Kai tsaye dining ta wuce, tsayawa tayi tur ganin dining yadda ta tafi ta barshi, haka tazo ta sameshi,  'kwafa tayi tace lallai ma mutumin nan wato ko abincin bai ciba yayi ficewar sa.

Tunani tayi anyama kuwa ya fito daga d'akin nasa? Juyawa tayi ta nufi d'akin nasa.

Tana shiga ta hangoshi kwance kan gado ya lullu'be jikin sa,  'karasawa tayi daf dashi, taga idanun sa a rude da alama bacci yake, bacci har 11:00am ta furta tana kallon sa, kamar ta tasheshi yayi break fast sai kuma ta fasa ta juya.

Har takusa kaiwa bakin 'kofa taji ya kira sunan ta

"Khairat"

Juyawa tayi suka had'a idanu dashi, idanun shi sunyi ja, da alama ko bacci bai samuba jiya, wani irin tausayin sa taji ya ratsata, 'karasawa bakin gadon tayi ta gaishe dashi, amsawa yayi lokacin da yake jingina jikinsa ga flour d'in daya sanya jikin gadon.

"Dama na gama hada mana break fast ne tun d'azu, amma banga ka fito kaci ba"

"Na koshi kawai yace mata"

"Ban gane ka 'koshi ba, idan wani abun kakeso kaci ka fad'amin mana saina shiga na had'a maka"

"Banajin cin komai ne kawai"

Zama tayi kusa dashi tana fad'in  "dan Allah yaya meyasa bazakaci abinci ba?"

Bai bata amsa ba,sai janyota dayayi ya ha'ata da 'kirjinsa yana fad'in   "Khairat dan Allah ki tausaya wa zuciyata  ki janye wannan maganar taki, na runtse miki da Allah Khairat ina sonki,  kuma zan ita aikata duk wani abu da kika bu'kata matu'kar zai sanya ki yarda ina sonki, amma fa banda maganar saki"

Sanyata dayayi a jikinsa ne yasanya ta tabbatar bashida lafiya, saboda wani zafi jikin nashi yaje bayarwa, ga saitin kirjinsa inda kanta yake,tana jiyo yanda zuciyarsa ke bugawa da sauri da sauri.

Batabi takan maganar da yakeba tace  "tace Yaya bakada lafiya ne?"

Runtse idanunsa yayi yace  "lafiya ta 'kalau"

"Gaskiya ban yarda ba jibi yadda jikinka yayi zafi ga zuciyarka tana har bawa da sauri amma Kace min lafiyar ka 'kalau"

"Na fad'a miki lafiyata 'kalau babu abinda yake damuna"

Bata sake magana ba, sai 'kokarin barina jikinsa take, hannu biyu ya sanya ya zagayeta dashi yana fad'in  "har yanzu bakice min komai akan maganar dana fara yimiki ba?"

"Kaga nifa bazan sauya ra'ayina ba har yanxu ina kan bakana na baxan iya cigaba da zama dakai ba"

"Haba Khairat sau nawa kikeso na baki ha'kuri, nasan na yimiki ba dai-dai ba, tunda nagane kuskurena ya kamata ki tausaya min, ki duba halin danake shiga ta dalilin ki ki yafemin, ko Allah munayi masa laifi idan muka tuba ya yafe mana.

"Haba haba Khairat why not da bazaki iya yafemun ba?"
 
"Why Khairat?"

Kamar yayi kuka yake maganar, bata tanka masa ba, kawai ta mi'ke ta fice daga d'akin, d'akinta tashiga ta rufe 'kofar, dan yanxu tasan zai iya shigowa.

***** ***** ****

Tun daga wannan lokacin wasan 'buya ya shiga tsakanin Khairat da Sadeeq sam bata bari su hadu, har cikin zuciyar ta tayafe masa duk wani abu daya yimata, amma batason ta fara nuna masa a lokacin ne, abin da yake damunta shine idan tayi girki ta xuba masa yadda ta ajje haka take dawowa ta  sameshi.

Ta bangaran Sadeeq kuwa komai ya dagule masa, ko hospital yaje baya iya aikata komai, gashi ciwon kanda yayi kwanaki yakuma dawo masa, ko bacci baya samu yayi, da kansa ya d'aukk hutu saboda babu azabar ciwo dake damunsa, gashi gaba d'aya ya kasa gane mai Khairat take nufi.

Yau kusan 1week kenan rabon Khairat da Sadeeq, tunda ta fahimci kwana 2 baya fita yana cikin gidan ta daina fitowa, abinci ma bata fiya yiba.

Kamar kullum tana zaune a d'aki Maryam ta kirata a waya,tana amsawa suka gaisa cikeda raha Maryam tace   "ina yaya Sadeeq don nasan yau yana gida saboda weekend ne?"

Shuru Khairat tayi ta kasa bata amsa, saboda sunyi waya da ita kusan sau 2 tana bata shawara akan wasan 'buyan da takeyi da Sadeeq, akan ya kamata ta daina sudawo da rayuwar su kamar yadda kowane ma'aurata suke, tun kafin iyayansu susan da maganar.

Jin Khairat tayi shuru ne yasanya ta fahimci har yanxu Khairat bata sauyaba,  rai b'ace tafara magana

"Khairat tunda na fahimci kinfiso na barki kiyi abinda kikeso shikenan, insha Allah bazan sake fad'a miki wani abu akan wannan maganar ba, kije kiyi son ranki, amma kisani shi Allah babu ruwan sa da rayuwar da kikeyi, Allah bazai barki da ha'k'kinsa ba a matsayin sa na mijinki, kina xaune bakisan a wane hali yake ciki ba, wlh ki sauya tunani Khairat hanyar da kika d'auka bamai bullewa bace"

Tana gama fad'a ta kashe wayar ta, shuru Khairat tayi tabbas maganar Maryam haka take, amma ita gaba d'aya  intaje wajansa ne batasan mai zatace masa ba.

Tuno tayi da maganar da Maryam tayi akan cewar tana xaune amma batasan a wane hali yake ciki ba, da sauri ta mi'ke saboda tunowa da tayi ko aiki baya fita, da sauri ta fice daga d'akin ta nufi d'akin sa.

Tana shiga d'akin ta iskeshi ya fito daga toilet, yana dafa bango zai 'karasa wajan gado da alama ba'a hayyacin sa yake ba, da sauri ta nufi inda yake, kafin ta 'karasa jiri ya kwashe shi yayi 'kasa da sauri ta 'karasa ta ri'koshi tana kiran sunan sa.

Idanu kawai yake binta dashi amma yakasa furta komai, wani irin kuka ta rushe dashi tana fad'in  "dan Allah yaya kayi hakuri wallahi na ha'kura zanci gaba da zama da kai, dan Allah karka mutu ka barni.

Ganin idanun sa suna lumshewa ne ya sanya ta sameshi da gudu ta nufi hanyar fita zata kira Momy a waya.

Ki mai ta tuna kuma, saita dawo da gudu ta d'auki wayar shi tayi dealing number Abdul, yana amsawa ta fara magana.

" Abdul dan Allah kazo Yaya zai mutu, nashiga uku"

"Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihir raj'un" ya furta sannan yace   " ki kwanta da hankalin ki, ba mutwa xaiyi ba, yanxu kiyi sauri kiyiwa gateman d'in Ku magana ya tayaki Ku sanyashi a mota, saimu hadu dake a hospital d'in su"

Ai batama bashi amsa ba ta kashe wayar ta fito da gudu, tayiwa baba mai gadi magana, nan da nan suka shiga suka fito dashi suka sanya a mota, kana ta koma ta d'auko hijab ta shiga motar ta fice daga gidan...

 *Page   37* 



 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_




Tana fita daga gidan kai tsaye hospital d'insu ta nufa, tana shiga harabar hospital d'in ta hango Abdul yana gyara parking, ko gyara parking batayiba ta fito da sauri, Abdul na fitowa daga motar ya hango ta, da sauri ya 'karaso inda take, ya fiddo wayar sa ya fara  kira dictator Heesham kasancewar ya sanshi ta sular Sadeeq din,

Yana amsa wayar ya sanar dashi suna waje sunkawo Sadeeq ne, suna gama wayar sai gashi tare da wasu mutum biyu, cikin sauri suka 'karasa suka fito da Sadeeq suka shige dashi hospital d'in, banda kuka babu abinda Khairat keyi, gaba d'aya nadamar abinda tayi take, tana kuka ta bisu cikin hospital d'in, suna shiga suka wuce emagency dashi, binsu Khairat tayi Heesham ya dakatar da ita, da kyar ta ha'kura, amma sai faman kuka take, da kyar Abdul ya lallasheta tayi shuru.

Bayan 30mins Heesham suka fito, tun kafin su 'karaso Khairat dake zaune tana aikin kuka tunda suka shiga ta mi'ke tana fad'in

"Heesham dan Allah ka fad'amin meyake damun mijina, inma mutuwa yayi dan Allah karka 'boyemin"

Murmushi doctor Heesham yayi yace "karki damu madam babu abinda ya sami mijinki, amma muyi mashi allura ne, nan da 2hrs zai farka"

Tanajin haka tayi hanyar d'akin...

Da sauri Heesham ya dakatar da ita yana fad'in   "kafin ki shiga inaso zanyi magana daku a office d'ina"

Yana gama fad'a yayi gaba suka bishi a baya, suna shiga office d'in suka zazzauna sannan doctor Heesham ya fara magana

"Kamar yadda kukaji na fad'a muku cewar babu abinda ya samu doctor Sadeeq haka take, amma fa gaskiya akwai matsala"

"Wace irin matsala.... Khairat ta fada tana kuka yayin da doctor Heesham ya dakatar da ita.

" madam ai ban 'karasa magana taba" juyawa yayi ya kalli Abdul yace

"Abdul kaga kai abokin Abdul ne, wanda ya d'auke ka tamkar d'an uwa, sannan wannan matarsa ce" ya fad'a yana nuna Khairat.

"Hakane Heesham, mudai yanxu munaso muji meke damun Sadeeq d'in"

"Bawani abu ke damun Sadeeq ba, sai tsananin damuwa datayi masa yawa, wadda ke haddasa masa tunani, kwanaki naje na dubashi akan wannan matsalar tashi harna bashi magani, amma bansan meyasa Sadeeq yayi watsi da magana taba, ya cigaba da sanya damuwa a ransa"

"Abinda yasa na kirawoku dolene a cikin Ku a samu wanda yasan matsalar dake damunshi, dolene kuyi gaggawar tallafa masa matu'kar kunsan abinda yake bu'kata, inba haka ba duk wani magani dazamu bashi aikin banzane matu'kar bai cire abinda ya sanya a ransa ba"

"Dalilin damuwar dayake yawan yine abun ya fara taba xuciyar shi amma bawai ya samu wata muguwar matsala bane, ta d'an kumbura ne amma ba can ba"

Wani sabon kuka Khairat ta kuma fashewa dashi, da kyar Abdul ya rarrasheta saboda yanxu ya fahimci inda matsalar take, saboda haka yace ta tashi taje wajan Sadeeq d'in ammafa ta daina wannan kukan.

Kamar jira take ta tashi ta fice daga office d'in, tana fita Abdul sukaci gaba da magana da Heesham akan matsalar Sadeeq, domin yanzu jira kawai Abdul yake Sadeeq ya farfad'o ya shiga maganar tasu, domin yanzu yasan Khairat ta gama yin sanyi.

Tana fita tayi hanyar d'akin da aka shiga dashi,  wani doctor ne ya nuna mata wani d'aki, wanda aka maida Sadeeq d'in can.

Tana bu'de kofar ta hango shi kwance sai bacci yake, 'karasawa tayi ta janyo kujera ta zauna tana kallon sa, hawaye da take tsaidawa ne suka biyo kuncinta, saboda wata muguwar ramar da taga yayi, ga wani uban fari daya 'kara, wani tausayin sa taji ya 'kara mamaye xuciyar ta, sai yanzu take nadamar abinda tayi masa, tabbas idan tayi la'akari da wahalar da tasha akan sa kasan cewar shine mutum na farko data fara so a rayuwar ta, kuma so bana wasa ba, bai kamata ace tayi masa haka ba, kasancewar sa mutum wanda bai tab'a yin soyayya ba sai a kanta.


Tana zaune tanata sa'ka da warwara, taji anata kiraye-kirayen sallar azahar tashi tayi ta fita, ta samo ruwa tayi alwala, sannan ta dawo ta shimfid'a kallabin ta tayi sallah.

Takai kusan 30mins tana addu'ar Allah ya bawa mijinta lafiya, tana addu'a tana kuka, saboda tasan cewar shi Allah babu ruwansa da wani batun ramuwar da takeyi.

Tana zaune har yanxu bata tashiba sai tsiyayar hawaye takeyi, saboda kota tsaidasu bazasu tsaya ba. kanta taji ya fara yimata ciwo, sannan ta tashi ta koma kan kujerar ta zauna ta d'ora kanta jikin kujerar.

bata jima ba baccin da batayi ba ya d'auke ta,  tana bacci Abdul ya shigo hannunsa d'auke da kayan fruit da abinci wanda doctor Heesham yace ya siyo, saboda idan Sadeeq ya tashi zaisha magani. ganin bacci takeyi ne ya sanya ya ajje kayan ya wuce masallaci.

Tarin da Sadeeq keyi ne ya sanya ta farka, da sauri ta tashi ta dawo kusa dashi tana fad'in

"Sannu Yaya, barina kirawo doctor"

Hannu ta ya ri'ke, da sauri ta dawo ta zauna tana kallon sa hawaye na bin kuncin ta, magana yakeson yi amma ya kasa sai binta yake da idanu.

Ganin ya kasa magana ne ya sanya ta sake cewa  "Yaya ka bari na kirawo doctor d'in"

Jingina taga yana niyyar yi, da sauri ta tashi ta d'auki pillow ta d'ora masa sannan ta tallafa masa ya jingina a jiki, komawa kujerar take niyyar yi ya ri'ko hannun ta, bata ankara ba taji ya zaunar da ita a jikin sa, ya d'ora kanta a kafad'ar sa sannan ya zagaye hannun sa a jikinta sannan ya fara magana.

"Khairat meyasa kikayi kokarin ceton rayuwa ta bayan kinsan nafi bu'katar mutuwa lokacin?"

"Yaya meyasa zaka zab'i mutuwa akan rayuwa, bayan kasan akwai mutane da dama dasuke da bu'katar ka?"

Ajiyar zuciya ya saike sannan yace   "miye amfanin rayuwa ta matu'kar babu ke, kinriga da kin zama jinin jikina, bazan iya zama a cikin duniyar nan ba matu'kar kika gujeni"

" meyasa kika neman guje min a lokacin da nafi bu'katar ki a kusa dani?  shin kinsan halin dana shiga ta sanadiyyar hakan?"

Shuru tayi tana zubar hawaye amma ta kasa magana.  ganin ta kasa magana ne ya sanya ya kuma cewa.

"Dan Allah Khairat kiyimin magana, ki bani amsar danake son ji daga gareki ke kadai ce maganin da zai warkar da ni, dan Allah ko amsar da kika saba banice ki daure ki fad'amin ko nakuma tunawa da matsayin a wajanki"

Tana shirin bashi amsa aka turo 'kofar d'akin, da sauri ta d'ago suka had'a ido da Abdul tareda doctor Heesham cike da kunya tayi 'ko'karin tashi daga jikin Sadeeq amma sai ya 'kara ri'keta, da sauri tayi 'kasa da kanta....

 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_




Da sauri tayi 'kasa da kanta, ta kasa kallon su, murmushi doctor Heesham yayi yace" ashe ka farka, amma madam d'in bataje ta sanar mana ba"

"Ina za'a samu damar fad'a mana, kana kallon yadda yawani ma'kale mata, ya hanata tashi" Abdul ya fad'a yana kallon su.

Wata uwar harara Sadeeq ya hurga masa, doctor Heesham ne yace" toh yanzu dai kayi ha'kuri zan d'an duba kane, 2mins kawai ya isa"

Har lokacin baiyi magana ba, sai janye hannun shi dayayi daga ri'kon da yayiwa Khairat, ai da sauri ta tashi ta kona gefe.

Cikin 'kankanin lokacin doctor Heesham ya gama duba Sadeeq, sannan ya tambayi Abdul ya siyo magungunan daya rubuta  masa lokacin suna  office, nuna masa ledar daya shigo da ita lokacin da Khairat tana bacci yayi, ya sanar masa suna ciki, nan Heesham ya sanar musu su bawa Sadeeq abinci yaci, sannan saisu bashi magungunan yasha, shi zaije yanada wani aiki da zaiyi.

Doctor Heesham yana fita Abdul ya kalli Khairat yace" ga abinci nan a cikin wannan ledar saiki bashi, inya gama sai yasha maganin"

"Toh" kawai tace masa ta koma kan kujerar ta zauna.

Har Abdul ya kusa fita ya dawo yace" yawwa na manta na kirawo Momy na sanar mata kuna hospital bakada lafiya"

A tare suka d'ago suna kallonsa, d'aga musu kafad'a yayi yace" yes na fad'a mata kuma tana hanya, amma fa ban sanar mata abinda ke faruwa ba, na bawa doctor Heesham ya fad'a mata cewar 2 days kana kwanan hospital ne shiyasa malaria ta kamaka saboda kunada sauro a hospital d'in, idan tazo sai kusan abinda zaku fad'a mata"

Kallon sa Sadeeq yayi yace" ai daka sani baka kirawota ba kawai, tunda yau zamu koma gida"

"Doctor Heesham fa yace sai gobe zai sallame ka saboda kana bu'katar kulawa, bama saina fad'a maka ba tunda aikin kane ka fini sanin haka, batun Momy kuma dole ne a sanar mata, ka saba mata kullum sai kaje gida sannan kwana biyu bakaje mata ba, gashi baka da lafiya zaka iya d'aukar wasu kwanaki baka je musu ba"

Hararar sa Sadeeq yayi yace" yace toh d'an tsari sannu, amma gwara daka sanar mata, saboda xata iya turo Aisha ta duba mata, wancan karon dana cemata aiki ne yayimin yawa har gida ta turo Aisha, sai danaje gidan take fad'a mini"

Tunda suka fara maganar su Khairat batace komai ba, har sukayi sallama sannan Abdul ya fice, bayan ya shaida mata ta tabbatar ta bashi abincin da maganin yasha.

Yana fita Sadeeq ya juyo yayi mata nuni da tadawo kusa dashi, ta fahimta amma saita basar tace" barina zuba maka abincin" bata jira amsar da zai bata ba ta janyo ledar ta bud'e, kayan fruit ta gani sai wata leda wacce ke da'uke da sunan restaurant d'in daya siyo abinci, sai pallet guda biyu sai ruwan roba.

Daya ledar kuma magunguna ne a ciki, ledar abincin ta d'auko ta bud'e, tuwon shikafa ne da miyar agusi wadda taji nama, sai zuba kamshi yake, plet d'in data gani ta d'auke ta zuba tuwon a ciki, tana gamawa ta mi'ka masa, 'kin amsa yayi yana gyara kwanciyar sa.

Da sauri ta matsa kusa dashi tana fad'in" plsss dan Allah yaya karmuyi haka da kai, ka taimaka kaci abincin nan"

"Uhm nifa na 'koshi"

Hannun sa ta ri'ko tana fad'in" dan Allah ka taimaka kaci kaji"

Ko kallon ta baiyi ba bare ta sanya ran zaici abincin, ganin hakane ya sanya tayi murmushi tace" shikenan tunda bazakaci ba nikuma bazan matsa maka ba, amma yanzu zanbar hospital d'innan tunda zamana kusa dakai bashi da amfani"

Tana gama fad'a ta mi'ke, da sauri ya ri'kota yana fad'in" yi ha'kuri d'auko abincin, ammafa tare zamuci"

Bata musa mishi ba ta d'auko masa wata roba ta mi'ka masa tareda ruwa, wanke hannun shi yayi sannan ya kuskure bakinsa, kana ya mika mara robar ta shiga toilet ta zubar ta dawo.

Sunacin abincin yanata zuba mata surutu kamar ba mara lafiya, banda murmushi babu abinda takeyi amma ta kasa biye masa, shikuwa gogan ji yake kamar karsu bar hospital d'in, dan gani yake kamar don taga bashida lafiya ne ya sanya take lallab'ashi haka, musamman daya tuna har yanxu bai samu damar sanin inda maganar auransu ta tsaya ba.

A haka suka gama cin abincin, tana bude masa maganin da zaisha, Momy tayi sallama tareda Aisha, da sauri Khairat ta mi'ke ta rungume Aisha, tana yi musu sannu da zuwa,

Cikeda fara'a Momy ta amsa tana kallon yadda d'an nata yayi wata irin muguwar rama, tare suka 'karaso Momy ce ta zauna a kujera. Aisha kuma ta zauna a gefen gadon daga wajan 'kafafun sa, Khairat kuwa jikin bango ta samu ta rakub'e.

Murya kasa-kasa ya gaishe da Momy,  amsawa tayi tanayi masa sannu, itama Aisha gaishe dashi tayi had'i da yimasa sannu.

Juyawa yayi kusa da Momy ya kalli Khairat ya watsa mata harara, sannan yace"Momy kina kallonta dan ta ganki shine zata wani koma jikin bango kamar 'kadangaruwa"

Dariya Aisha ta she'ke da ita, ita kuwa Khairat cinno baki tayi kamar zatayi kuka, da sauri Momy ta jawota jikinta tana fad'in" ohh ni 'yasu dama har yanxu Khairat baki daina saurin kuka ba?"

"Kai kuma a gabana kakeyi mata tsiya, dama ai da shirin tafiya da ita nazo, saboda bazai yuwu kari'ka barmin yarinya a gida ba kana tahowa hospital kana kwana, wata 'kilama alhakin tane ya sanya sauraye suka cijeka harka kwanta ciwo"

Hararar Khairat yayi wacce ke dariya 'kasa-'kasa sannan yace" Momy ammafa kina gani magani Heesham yace ta bani, amma danta ganki shine ta ma'kale bayan ki"

'Dagota Momy tayi tace" tashi ki bashi maganin kinji d'iyata" tashi Khairat tayi ta bud'e magungunan tana cirowa tana mi'ka masa, yana gamasha wayar Momy ta fara ringing.

Tana dubawa taga Dadyn Sadeeq ke kira, da sauri ta amsa, tana fad'a masa sun 'karaso hospital d'in, jikin Sadeeq d'in ya tambaya, tace masa da sau'ki, kana ta mi'kawa Sadeeq.

Sai da suka gama gaisawa, ya tambaye shi jikin,  sannan ya shaida masa anjima zai shigo hospital d'in, suna kammala wayar Sadeeq ya tura Aisha ta kira masa doctor Heesham.

Bata jima da fita ba, suka dawo tare dashi, suna shigowa ya gaishe da Momy ta amsa, sannan ya tambayi Khairat tabawa Sadeeq maganin, gyada masa kai kawai tayi, sannan ya juya ga Sadeeq yana tambayar shi meyake bu'kata.

"Dama inaso ka taimaka min ne nayi alwala, naga har za'ayi la'asar, gashi kuma har yanxu ina d'an jin jiri"

Kafin doctor Heesham yayi magana Momy tace" wato mu nan duka babu wanda kakejin zai iya tallafa maka ko?"

"Haba Momy so kike na sanyaki aiki, ko kuma so kike ba sanya wad'annan yaran salon su rainani?"  ya fad'a yana nuna su Khairat.

Harara kawai Momy ta watsa masa dan baxata iya cewa komai ba, Khairat da Aisha kallonsa kawai sukayi, a xuciya Aisha ta furta, wai raini, shi kuwa dictator Heesham dariya kawai yayi ya karkad'a kai, sannan ya tallafa masa yayi alwala yayi sallah azahar tareda la'asar kasan cewar an fara kiraye-kirayen sallah.

Sai da suma sukayi sallah, sannan Momy ta kirawo driver d'insu ya shigo da abincin data dafo musu ta aje musu, sannan sukayi musu sallama, bayan Sadeeq ya fad'awa Momy tayi masa faten wake da alayyahu, idan Dady xai taho saiya taho masa dashi.

Suna zuwa inda sukayi parking motar su, suka had'u da Abdul da Maryam suna fitowa daga mota, da mamaki Momy take kallon su ganinsu a tare sai zuba firarsu suke irin ta masoya, murmushi  kawai Momy tayi, ta shige mota kasan cewar basu gansu ba, Aisha kuwa mamaki abin ya bata don bata tab'a samun labari, ba kuma kusan kullum sai sun had'u da Maryam a school.

Karkad'a kai kawai tayi itama ta shige mota suka fice daga hospital d'in..
