 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_




Su Abdul na fitowa daga mota, kai tsaye suka shuga cikin hospital d'in.

Momy na fita, Khairat ta dawo kan kujera ta zauna tana fad'in" yanzu maimakon kace na koma gida na dafo maka faten waken, shine zaka sanya Momy aiki".

"Chab ai keda komawa gida kuma sai an sallamemu".

"Uhm wai kana nufin a hospital d'in nan zan kwana?".

"Ohhh tambaya ma kikeyi?".

" nidai bazan kwana hospital ba".

Yana shirin yin magana, Abdul suka shigo, amsawa kawai Khairat tayi, domin a tunanin ta Abdul ne kad'ai, kasan cewar baya ta bawa 'kofar.

Abdul na shigowa ya zauna gefen gadon da Sadeeq yake, yana tambayar shi jikin nashi, Maryam dake tsaye a bakin 'kofa ce ta 'karaso a hankalin ta rufewa Khairat idanu, cikeda mamaki Khairat ta fara tunanin waye ya rufe mata idanu, a zuciyarta ta ayyana anya kuwa ba Maryam bace, Abdul da Sadeeq kuwa murmushi sukeyi, suna jira suga zata gane.

Wata dabara ce tazowa Khairat, hannunta ta sanya ta shafa hannun da aka rufe mata fuska, tana shafawa ta saki wani murmushi tana fad'in " beasty "

Murmushi Maryam tayi suka rungume juna suna dariya, muryar Abdul ce ta katsesu.

"Ikon Allah yanzu Khairat ta yaya kika ganeta?".

Bata bashi amsa ba, sai wani zobe data nuna masa a hannun ta, sannan ta ri'ko hannun Maryam ma ta nuna masa.

" wato dai zoben kika shafa kika gane, banda hakaba bazaki gane gimbiyar tawa ba".

"Uhm babu wani, ai tunkafin na shafa zoben na ganeta".


Sun jima a hospital d'in, sai gab da magriba suka tafi, Khairat tayi niyyar rakasu amma Sadeeq ya hanata zuwa, suna fita ta zauna a kujera tana kumbure-kumbure.

Yana kallon ta amma ya shareta, ganin ya'ki kulata ne ya sanya ta kalleshi tace" Yaya nifa gida zan koma".

Shuru yayi mata bai cemata komai ba sai juya mata baya dayayi, wani irin abu tajj ya tokare mata a ma'kigaro, haushi ya cikata, batasan sanda ta fara hawaye ba, ganin baisan tanayi babe ya sanya ta farayin mai sauti.

Sautin kukan ta kawai ya fara ji, da sauri ya juyo yana kallon ta, dafa kanshi yayi yace" ya salam Khairat meye abin kuka kuma?".

"Bata bashi amsa ba, sai 'kara sautin kukan da tayi, yun'kurawa yayi da kyar ya jingina da gadon, sannan ya kira sunan ta, bata amsa ba saida ya sake kiranta, murya 'kasa-'kasa ta amsa tana cinno dan 'karamin lips d'inta.

"Zonan ki zauna", ya fad'a yana nuna mata kusa dashi.

Kamar bazata tashiba, sai kuma ta mu'ke taje ta zauna nesa dashi, murmushi yayi yace " amma ai nan wajan nace miki", ya fad'a yana nuna mata.

Matsawa tayi kusa dashi, tana wani ciccin magani, murmushi kawai yayi sannan ya sanya hannunsa cikin nata yana wasa dashi, kana ya fara magana.

"Saboda nace bazaki koma gida ba, shine kike kuka?".

"Ko bakya son zama kusa dani ne, shiyasa kikeso ki koma gida"

Kai kawai ta kad'a masa, alamun a'a.

"Toh fad'a min me zakiyi a gidan".

"Uhm kana kallon fa kayan bacci ne a jikina, tun safe, kuma haka zan kuma kwana dasu, danma hijabin har 'kasa yake shiyasa ba'a gane kayan bacci na santa, gashi kuma inaso nayi wanka" ta 'kareshe maganar kamar zata kuma yin kukan.

Ajiyar zyciya ya sauke yace" na fahimta, amma dan Allah kiyi ha'kuri zuwa goben, kaya kuma barina kira Momy ta bawa dady kayan Aisha, ya taho miki dasu, tunda bai zama dole Abdul ya dawoba, bare na sanya ya tsaya ya  siya miki a hanya".

"Uhm kawai ka barshi ma, zan zauna haka kawai, tunda idan ankawo kayan ma, ba wankan zanyi ba, domin ni bazan iya wanka a nan ba".

Suna zaune haka har magariba tayi, Khairat ta tashi tayi sallah, sannan ta dawo tace masa" yaya kona taimaka maka ne kayi alwala?".

"Kawai ki kirawo min doctor Heesham, saboda bazaki iya d'agani ke kadai ba, jikina ne duk ya saki".

"Toh amma yaya anya kuwa bai koma gida ba, saboda tun zuwan su Momy naji suna sallama da Abdul".

"anya kuwa wata 'kila dai yaje ya dawo ne, saboda nasan bazai tafi batare da yayimin sallama ba, gashi baki d'auko min wayata ba ko?".

Sai a lokacin ta tuna da wayar sa data bari a mota.

" a'a na taho da ita, tana cikin mota ne"

"Oh my God, banaso ki fita harabar hospital d'in nan ke d'aya ne"

"Toh yaya ai larura ce, ha'kuri xakayi naje na d'auko na dawo"

Babu yadda ya iya haka ya harta ta tafi, amma sam bada son ranshi ba, domin bayaso wasu su gane mishi mata.

Bata jima ba, ta dawo ta mi'ka masa wayar, yana amsa yayi dealing number doctor Heesham, saida takusa tsinkewa sannan ya d'aga.

"Owk" kawai Khairat taji ya furta ya katse wayar.

"Yadai baya hospital d'in ne?" Ta fad'a tana kallon sa.

"Nop yad'an fita ne, amma yanzu yake shigowa hospital d'in.

"Owk" bata gama rufe baki ba, doctor Heesham ya turo 'kofar tareda sallama.

Sadeeq ne ya  amsa masa, sannan ya 'karasa shigowa, tambayar Khairat yayi jikin Sadeeq d'in, tace masa da sau'ki, sannan ya koma kusa da Sadeeq d'in yana tambayar shi yanayin yadda yakeji.

Bayan ya gama fad'a masa ne, ya d'ora da cewar" amma ina tunanin sai an sanya min drift, saboda yadda jikina ya saki, gashi har yanxu jirin da nakeji bai ragu ba"

"Hakane nima tunanin da nayi kenan, shiayasa nace sai gobe zamu sallame Ku"

"Owk amma barina fara yin sallah, sai a sanya min"

"Da kayi ha'kuri an sanya maka, nan da lokaci kad'an zai 'kare, sai kayi sallar tunda larura ke damunka"

"Nop Bari nayi kawai"

Dole Heesham ya kyaleshi, ya tallafa masa tayi alwala, sannan yayi sallar, kana ya tallafa masa ya mai dashi gadon.

Drift d'in ya had'a masa, tareda wasu ruwan allura aciki, sannan ya sanya masa ruwan yayi musu sallama, bayan ya sanarwa Khairat idan ruwan ya 'kare tayi masa magana, 

Bai jima da fita ba, Dady suka shigo tareda Abban Khairat, Dady ne ya fara shigowa bakinsa d'auke da sallama, cikeda kunya Khairat ta amsa, jin muryar Abban tane ya sanya ta d'ago kanta, ai tana ganin shi, ta mi'ke ta fad'a jikin sa tana fad'in" Dady ina Momcy? ".

Janye ta yayi yana fad'in" ohhh Khairat meyasa har yanxu bazaki girma bane?".

Shagwab'e fuska tayi tana fad'in" toh toh ba kai bane ka'ki zuwa da Momy, kuma ko sageer baka taho dashi ba, bare kuma autar Momcy".

Dadyn Sadeeq ne ya kalleta yace" uhm wato Khairat ni dayake ba dadyn ki bane shiyasa sai da aka b'oye fuska aka gaidani?"

Rufe fuskarta tayi da hannunta batare da tabashi amsa ba, Dady ne yace" ki bashi amsa mana".

Saida takuma rude fuskarta sannan tace" uhm Dady nifa kunyar shi nakeji".

Dariya sukayi gaba d'ayan su, Sadeeq kuwa da tunda suka shigo yake kallon yarintar da Khairat ke zubawa, dariya shima yayi, saboda ba 'karamin burgeshi tayi ba, jiyake inama  ace shi take zubawa shagwab'a haka, murmushi yayi a zuciyarsa yace, Khairat kenan insha Allah zanbi duk hanya data dace dan ganin farin cikin mu ya d'ore har abada.

Su Dady sun juma a hospital d'in gar gurin doctor Heesham sukaje domin 'kara tabbatar da lafiyar d'an nasu, doctor Heesham yayi musu bayanin cewar malaria ce tayi masa mugun kamu, kasan cewar baya son shan magani.

Da haka sukayi musu sallama bayan Dady ya bawa Khairat faten waken da Sadeeq yace Momy tayi masa.

Sai wajan 9;15 drift din da aka sanyawa Sadeeq ya 'kare, doctor Heesham Khairat ta kira ya cire masa, sannan yace ta tabbatar ta bashi abinci da magani, kana ya tafi office d'in sa.

Babu yadda Khairat batayi ba akan ya fara cin abincin, amma yace dole sai yayi sallah, haka ta ha'kura ta kyaleshi, da kanshi ya tashi ya shiga toilet, Khairat tayi mamakin ganin yadda har ya iya tashi da kansa, amma data tuna da ruwan da aka sanya masa, kuma bashi kadai ba, kawai sai tayi murmushi tanajin dad'i a zuciyarta ganin mijinta ya samu lafiya.

Tana zaune harya fito, tashi tayi ta shimfid'a mashi dardumar data d'auko a motar shi.

Yana idar da sallar ta d'auko faten waken sai zuba 'kamshi yake ta xuba musu, tare sukaci, suna kammalawa ta kwashe kayan sannan ta dawo ta zauna, zuwa lokacin harta fara jin barci, sai lumshe ido take.

Sadeeq dake kallon tane ya lura da hakan, yace" ya dai kanwa?".

"Uhm Yaya bacci nakeji, kuma babu abinda zan shimfid'a na kwanta".

"karki damu tare zamu kwanta a gadon"

Zare idanu tayi tana kallonsa..

 *Page   40* 



 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_



"Uhm Yaya bacci nakeji, kuma babu abinda zan shimfid'a na kwanta".

" karki damu tare zamu kwanta a gadon".

Zare idanu tayi tana kallonsa, 

"Miye na zare ido kuma?".

"Dama naga gadon bazai ishemu bane, kuma gashi bakada lafiya"

"Karki damu zamu iya yin managed a haka"

"Uhm nifa Yaya bazan iya kwanciya tareda kai bane" ta fad'a tana sosa 'keya.

"Uhm saboda me?".

"Ko kin manta ni mijinki ne?, koda yake baki gama amsata a matsayin mijinki ba".

"uhm nifa ba haka...

"Nop karki damu, barina kwanta a 'kasa kawai"

"A'a Yaya baza'ayi haka ba, naji na ha'kura mu kwana a gadon".

"A'a Khairat banason takura miki, kawai ki kwanta"

Bata jira cewarshi ba, ta tashi ta gyara gadon, jiyowa tayi da niyyar yimasa magana, wayar shi ta fara ringing, yana dubawa yaga sunan doctor Heesham, amsawa yayi yana fad'in" yadai mutumin"

"Babu komai, dama inaso naji ko kunada bu'katar wani abu ne, juyawa yayi ya kalli Khairat, kauda kanta tayi kasan cewar tana jiyo maganar tasu.

"a'a babu abinda muke bu'kata" ya fada masa.

Nan sukayi sallama, ya kashe wayar.

Ganin bai cemata komai bane, ya sanya ta kalleshi tace" Yaya ka taso mu kwanta, ni bacci nakeji"

"Kawai ki kwanta, idan na gama abinda nake zan taso na kwanta"

"Uhm uhm nidai kawai ka taso, nasan wayo kakeso kayimin, idan nayi bacci ka kwanta a " kasa, kuma ko shimfid'a babu".

Murmushi kawai yayi batareda ya musa mataba, ya tashi ya 'karaso wajan gadon ya kwanta, yana kwanciya ta samu gefen gadon ta zauna tana kallon 'kasa.

Kallon ta kawai yayi ya karkad'a kai ya gyara kwanciyar sa ya juya mata baya.

Tana jinsa sanda ya juya amma bata kwanta ba, saida tayi kusan 10mins a zaune, sannan ta tashi ta cire hijab d'in, ta shiga toilet ta sake d'auro alwala, sannan tazo ta kwanta a gefen gadon, rabinta a gadon rabi a waje, ra dun'kule 'kafafun ta.

Juyowa yayi ya janyota jikin sa, ya kwanta da ita a 'kirjinsa, kasan cewar yasan gadon yayi musu kad'an, sarai yana sane da cewar doctor Heesham zai iya sallamar su a yau, amma ya'ki bu'katar hakan, saboda jiyake kamar idan suka koma gida, Khairat zata koma masa yadda takeyi 2days d'innan.

Khairat najin haka tayi saurin runtse idanun ta, da tashi zatayi amma saita tuna da matsayin Sadeeq a wajanta, kuma tabbas ta sani idan ta b'ata masa rai Allah zaiyi fushi da ita, saboda mijin tane, wancan ma data aikata tana addu'ar Allah ya yafe mata.

Cire komai tayi a ranta, saboda tasan Sadeeq bazai yimata komai a gurin nan ba.

Gyara kwanciyar ta tayi, ta 'kara lafewa a jikinsa, shikuwa Sadeeq zagayeta yayi da hannunsa yana shafa bayan ta.

Lumshe idanunta tashiga yi, wani irin bacci na fizgarta, muryarshi ce ta sanyata bud'e idanunta.

"Khairat dan Allah meyasa bazaki yadda mu cigaba da rayuwar auran mu ba?".

"Wallahi nayi miki al'kawarin sanyaki farin ciki fiyeda yadda kike tunani, Khairat ya kamata ace zuwa yanzu kin sauya ra'ayin ki a kaina".

"Khairat ko kinaso na rasa rayywata a sanadin kine?".

Karkad'a masa kai tayi alamun a'a.

"uhm  magana nakeso kiyimin, konaji sassauci akan abinda nakeji a zuciyata" hannunta ta janyo ya d'ora a saitin 'kirjinsa ta yadda tajin yadda zuciyarsa ke harbawa, da sauri-sauri.

Runtse idanunta tayi tace" Yaya ka cire komai a ranka nayi maka al'kawarin insha Allah muna komawa gida Zan sanar dakai duk wani abu da kake bu'kata".

Wani dad'i yaji ya ziyarce shi, dukda baisan abinda zata fad'a masa goben ba, amma ko babu komai zai samu matsaya.

"Shikenan Allah ya nuna mana goben"

"Ameen"

'Kara rungumeta yayi, yabajin inama ace su dawwama a haka, wani ba'kon al'amari yakeji yana ziyartar shi,amma ya dake saboda bayaso yayi wani abu dazai b'atawa gimbiyar sa.

Itama Khairat wani farin ciki takeji, yana mamaye ta, kamar tayi tsalle dan murna, kasan cewar tariga da tagama samun cikar burin zuciyarta,  a haka bacci ta kwasheta.

Bata jima da bacci ba,shima bacci ya kwasheshi cikeda nishad'i.

********************

Washe gari da sassafe Momy ta turo driver ya kawo musu break fast.

Suna break fast d'in,  Abdul ya shigo d'akin, babu yadda Sadeeq baiyi dashi akan ya zauna suci, amma yace a 'koshe yake.

Suna kammalawa Sadeeq yace wa Abdul zaije wajan doctor Heesham saboda yanzu yakeso su koma gida.

Sadeeq na fita Abdul ya kalli Khairat yace" Khairat inaso zamuyi magana ne".

Maida hankalin ta tayi gareshi, tana fad'in" toh ina jinka", dob ita a zatonta maganar akan Maryam ne.

Gyara zamansa yayi yace" dama akan maganar kuda Sadeeq ne, dan Allah Khairat a tsaida abin haka, tabbas Sadeeq yayi miki laifi, amma a yanzu ya gane abinda ya aikata ba dai-dai bane, koda ba haka bama, ya kamata ku gyara zaman takewar ku, musamman yadda kikaga Sadeeq na naiman halaka kanshi ta sanadiyyar ki".

Abinda baki saniba shine, tun kafin kuyi aure Sadeeq ke kaunar ki, amma shima baisan da hakan ba, nasha fad'a masa amma saiya 'karyatani, wani lokacin ma har fad'a mukeyi dashi akan hakan, dole nake kyaleshi saboda nasan cewar baisan meye so ba, ni shaida ne Sadeeq bai tab'a yin soyayya ba, shiyasa ya kasa tantance so da burgewa, shi duk a tunanin sa kawai kina burgeshi ne, amma bawai yana sonki ba, sai a yanzu dayaga yana shirin rasaki gaba d'aya sannan ya gane me yakeji game da ke".

'Dagowa yayi yaji maizata ce,  idanunta ya gani sunyi rau-rau zatayi kuka.

"Haba Khairat miye abin kuka kuma?, kinga karkiyi kuka, Sadeeq baima san zanyi miki magana ba, don haka idan kinajin bazaki iya cigaba da zama dashi ba, saiku samawa kanku mafuta, idan kuma zaki ha'kura ku zauna ne toh shikenan"

"Uhm yaya Abdul aina ha'kura, insha Allah babu wanda zaiji kanmu dashi, dama can shiya guji zama dani, amma tunda shima ya ha'kura dolene nima na ha'kura, koda banason Sadeeq dole zan zauna dashi saboda d'an uwana ne".

Murmushi yayi yace" kai amma naji dad'in haka, Allah ya albarkaci rayuwar auran ku, ya baku zuri'a ta gari, haka sukaci gaba da fira har Sadeeq ya dawo, zuwa lokacin firarsu ta gangara kan Maryam.

Sadeeq na shigowa yace" toh baban soyayya kazo ka cika min kunnen mata da firar budurwar ka".

"Hmmm Malam bakasan yadda nakeji da ita bane shiyasa kake ganin nafiya takura muku da maganar ta".

"naji"

"Ke kuma saiki shirya mu wuce gida", ya fad'a yana kallon ta.

Bata cemasa komai ba, ta tashi ta had'a  kayan da Momy ta kawo musu abinci ta xuba a leda tareda magungunan Sadeeq, sannan sukayiwa doctor Heesham sallama suka fito.

Gida Abdul ya wuce, Khairat kuma tayi driving d'insu suka wuce gida.

********************

Tun daga bakin gate baba mai gadi yayiwa Sadeeq ya jiki, suna shiga cikin gidan, Sadeeq ya zauna a parlour, Khairat kuwa kai tsaye ta wuce d'akin Sadeeq.

Saida ta tabbatar ta gyara ko'ina na d'akin sannan ta fasheshi da room freshener, tareda turarukan wuta masu kamshi.

Tana gamawa ta shiga toilet ta gyarashi, kana ta had'a masa ruwan wanka, tana fitowa ta zab'a masa kayanda zai sanya.

Sakkowa tayi parlourn tazo ta sanar masa, tashi yayi yanufi steps d'in.

Yana tashi ta shiga gyara parlourn, saida ta kammalawa komai sannan ta koma d'akinta ta gyashi, tana gamawa ta shiga wanka, lokacin data fito har anfara kiraye-kirayen sallah, dan haka ta koma ta d'auro alwala, tana idar da sallah ta shirya kanta, cikin atamfa dinkin riga da sket, feshe jikinta tayi da turaruka sannan ta fito, ganin bata sameshi a parlour bane ya sanya ta wuce kitchen.

Tunanin mezata dafa mai sau'ki tayi, kasan cewar lokaci ya 'kure, jalof d'in taliya ta yanke shawarar dafawa.

Tana yanka albasa tajiyo kamshin turaransa, ko ba'a fad'a mata ba, tasan kitchen d'in ya taho, tanajin motsin shigowar sa amma ta'ki juyawa.

Bata ankaraba, taji ya rungumeta ta baya. dora hab'arsa yayi kan kafad'ar ta, yana d'an jijjuyata, wata irin kasala ta faraji, saboda yadda yake goga mara gashin dake hab'ar tashi.

Runtse idanunta tayi, ga "kamshin turaran sa dake dukan hancinta, murya 'kasa ya fara yimata magana.

''Meyasa baki yimin magana nazo na tayaki girkin ba?''

'' Uhm  gani nayi bakada lafiya, kuma ma ai dama ni kad'ai nakeyi girkin, baka tab'a tayani ba''.

Shuru yayi mata, ba tareda yace komai ba, saboda baisan amsar dazai bata ba, sakinta yayi tace'' kawo na tayaki yanka albasar''

Da mamaki ta kalleshi tace'' a'a yaya ka barshi kawai zanyi da kaina".

Ko kallonta beyiba ta janyo abin yanka albasar ya amshi wu'kar hannunta ya fara yanka albasar.

Kallon sa kawai take, daga baya ta kama wani abun, a haka suka 'karasa girkin tare, da kamshi ya d'auki na baba mai gadi, sannan ita kuma takai musu dining.

 *Page   41* 



 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_




Tana jere abincin ya shigo, 'karasowa inda take yayi ya kalleta yace'' uhm nifa bancin abinci a dining, kawai ki aje mana a carpet''

Murmushi tayi tace'' kai dai yaya bansan meyasa 2days baka son cin abincin a dining ba''

''Kawai nafi jin dad'in ci a kasan ne''

'' Uhm '' kawai tace ta d'auko kukan abinci, shi kuma ya d'auko pleats d'in ya buyota.

Suna zama ta d'auko pleats guda biyu, kallonta yayi yace'' naga kin d'auki pleat guda biyu?'',

'' eh nawa da naka ne ''.


'' indai raba mana abinci zakiyi toh nidai na 'koshi''

Kallon sa kawai tayi, bata bashi amsa ba ta aje d'ayan, ta xuba a guda d'aya ta sanya spoon guda biyu, sannan ta  aje a gabansu.

A tare sukaci abincin, suna gamawa ta kwashe kayan takai kitchen ta wanke, sannan ta fito.

'Dakinsa ta wuce ta d'auko maganin sa ta kawo masa, b'ata face yayi yace'' dan Allah ki maida maganin nan ca nake na samu lafiya''.

'' Yaya ammafa kasan....''

Dakatar da ita yayi yana fad'in'' dan Allah Khairat ki bar maganin nan wallahi banaso''

Zama tayi kusa dashi ta marairaice fuska tace'' dan Allah yaya kadai na cewa bazaka shaba, idan kai baka bu'katar lafiyar ka, ai akwai masu bu'katar ka da yawa, bayan.....''.

Dakatar da ita yayi yana fad'in'' yawwa anzo wajan''

'' meyasa kike yawan cewa akwai masu bu'katar naci gaba da rayuwa?''.

'' saboda nasan cewar mahaifan ka suna bu'katar ka, yan uwanka suma suna bu'katar ka, karka manta ku biyu Allah ya bawa mahaifan ka, sannan kaine d'a namiji tilo a wajansu.''.

Shuru yayi na d'an tsayin lokaci sannan yace'' duk wannan bayanin da kikayi, banji kin ambaci cewar, keda kike matsayin matata wane irin hali zaki shiga a lokacin da kika rasani ba''.

'' Khairat ki taimaka ki sanar dani matsayin auran mu dake ko hankalina zai kwanta, Khairat maganin dazaki bani babu abinda zai 'karamin saboda bashi bane maganin cutata ba, Khairat kece kadai maganin cutar dake damuna, ya fad'a yana nuna ta.

Kasa cemasa komai tayi sai 'kasa tayi da kanta, matsowa yayi kusa da ita, ya sanya hannunsa ya tallafo fuskar ta, had'a ido sukayi dashi da sauri tayi 'kasa da kanta.

"Meyasa bazakiyi min magana ba, Khairat kinaso na rasa rayuwa ta ne saboda ke?''.

Karkad'a masa kai kawai tayi.

'' toh ki sanar dani mana, idan kika'ki amincewa  kinsan bazan yimiki dole ba, juyin duniya yayi  yayi da Khairat amma ta'ki magana, 'karshema kuka ta fara yimasa.

Gaba d'aya ya rasa yadda zaiyi da ita, zuwa yanzuma tafara bashi haushi, don haka ya aiyyana a ransa zai yimata tambaya ta 'karshe idan ta'ki bashi amsa kawai zai sauwwa'ke mata, inyaso imma soyayyar ta zata illatashi ne, yaji zai jure.

Da kakkausar muryar da babu wasa a cikinta ya soma yimata magana.

'' Khairat inaso zanyi miki tambaya ta 'karshe wacce daga ita indai baki bani amsa ba, zan sawwa'ke miki aurena batare da naje wajan mahaifan mu ba, saboda hakanne zai tabbatar min da bakyason zama dani, kuma idan nakaiwa mahaifan mu magar zasu ita tursasa miki zama dani, don haka zanyi miki tambaya ta 'karshe''.

Saida yasake d'ago kanta suka had'a ido sannan yace'' Khairat kinason zama dani a matsayin mijinki uban 'ya'yanki?''.

Tana hawaye ta shiga kad'a masa kai alamun eh,  wani dad'i yaji ya ziyarce shi, amma kuma saita b'ata fuska yace'' magana nakeso kiyimin da bakin ki, sannan kibani tabbacin kin amibcene saboda kina sona bawai saboda tausatawa ki 'yan uwan taka ba''.

Sautin kukan ta ta 'kara, da sauri yace'' amsafa nace miki ina bu'kata ba kuka ba''.

Tana kukan ta fara magana'' Yaya har cikin zuciyata na amince zan zauna dakai marayin mijina, kuma uban 'ya'yana na har abada, kai shedane ne tun ina 'kananun shekaru Allah ya jarabceni da soyayyar ka,wacce na rayu da ita tsayin shekaru, don haka sai dai nayiwa Allah godiya''.

Tunda ta fara magana yake murmushi saboda fad'ar yadda yakejin maganar tata a zuciyarsa bazai misaltu ba, tana kaiwa  aya ya rungume ta yana hamdala ga Allah mad'aukakin sarki, hawayen farin ciki yaji yana fitowa daga idanunsa.

Itama hawayen takeyi, jin zubar hawayen sa a kafad'ar tane, ya sanya ta d'ago, suka had'a ido cikeda shagwab'a tace'' uhm Yaya toh   mene abun kuka kuma?''.

'' Khairat hawayen farin ciki nake, dole na godewa Allah daya mallaka min ke, a matsayin matata, da ace na rasaki bansan halinda zan kasance a ciki ba, tabbas dole na godewa su Dady da sukayi 'kokarin ganin na mallaki abinda xuciyata ke muradi, wanda ni a lokacin sam bansan da hakan ba, tabbas Khairat Allah ya rubuta ke matata ce ta har abada, insha Allah zan kasance mai saki farin ciki, bazan tab'a barinki kiyi kuka ta dalilina ba, kisa a ranki kece farin cikina, idan na b'ata miki tamkar na b'atawa zuciyata ne''.

Wani farin ciki taji ya baibayeta, murmushi kawai take amma ta kasa magana saboda yadda takeji a zuciyarta, ji take kamar tayi tsalle ta rungume shi, amma baxata itaba, saboda wata irin kunyarshi takeji.

Ganin ta kasa magana, sai zuba murmushi take, ya sanya ya kalleta yace'' ya dai my Deejah ko bakin ya mutu ne?''.

Sunan daya kirata dashi ne ya 'kara sanyata farin ciki, runtse idanunta tayi ta rungume shi taba fad'in'' tabbas bazan tab'a mantawa da wannan rana ba, saboda ta kasance rana mafi muhimmanci a rayuwata, ranace wacce zuciyata ta samu 'yanci, ta fita daga cikin kuncin da ta dad'e a cikin sa''.

Hannayen ta ta d'aga sama tana fad'in'' Alhamdulillah Allah ba gode maka daka nunamin wannan rana, tabbas Allah Kaine mai yaye ba wanka daga cikin duhu, ka sanya shi a cikin haske, Allah ka 'karawa annabinka daraja''.

''Ameen my Deejah'', ya fad'a yana juyo da ita suna fuskan tar juna, hannun ya sanya ya lakuce mata hanci yace'' uhm dama kinaso amma shine saida kika bani wahala sannan kika ansamin koh?''.

B'oye fuskar ta tayi a kafad'ar shi tana dariya.

 *Page   42* 



 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_


B'oye fuskarta tayi a kafad'arsa tana dariya, shima dariyar yayi yana dad'a rungume ta, sun jima a haka, kowannen su da abinda yake sa'kawa a zuciyarsa. wayar Sadeeq ce ta fara ringing, hannun ya mi'ka ya d'auko wayar, 'kokarin barin jikinsa Khairat tayi, amma ya 'kara ri'keta, sannan ya kara a kunnan sa yana amsa sallamar da akayi.

'' Momcy ina wuni''.

Jin ya ambaci sunan Momcy ne ya tabbatar mata da wayake magana. 

'' ya jikin my son?''.

''Lpy qalau Momcy'', ya fad'a yana kallon Khairat datayi 'kuri tana kallon sa, jira kawai take ya mi'ko mata wayar.

'' jiya naso na biyo abbansu Khairat muje hospital dubaka, amma bansamu zuwa ba saboda rahama batajin dad'i''.

'' ayya ai babu komai Momcy, ai jikin nawa da sau'ki dama maleria ce''.

'' ya jikin rahama yake''.

Jin ya fad'i hakane Khairat ta d'ago kanta tana fad'in'' yaya rahama ce batada lafiya?''.

Bai cemata komai ba, har saida suka gama wayar sannan ya mi'ko mata.

Cikeda murna ta amsa kamar ba kullum sai sunyi waya da Momcy ba.

'' Momcy ina wuni''

'' lafiya 'kalau Khairat, ya jikin Sadeeq d'in?''.

'' alhamdulillah Momcy''.

'' Momcy wai meyake damun autarki?''.

'' kedai bari, kwana biyu zazzab'i ke damunta''.

'' ayya toh Allah ya bata lafiya''.

'' amee ''

'' Momcy ina take mu gaisa?''.

'' ai bata jima da yin bacci ba''

Kallon Sadeeq tayi taga yadda ya 'kura mata idanu, yana murmushi, murmushi itama tayi sannan tace'' Momcy anjima zanzo dubiya fah''.

Wata harara ya watsa mata. yana karkad'a kai alamun zaki sani ne.

Momcy kuwa cewa tayi'' indai mu zakizowa duniya mun yafe, kuda za'a zuwa dubiya shine zaki nemi ki matsa masa ya kawoki ko, toh karki sake naga 'kafarki''.

'' uhm yanzu Momcy bakyaso nazo?''.

'' ba haka nake nufiba, inaso ki zauna ki kulada mijinki, yana bu'katar kulawarki, musamman yanzu dayake cikin halin rashin lafiya''.

'' toh Momcy''.

''Allah yayi miki albarka''.

''Ameen Momcy''.

'' toh sai anjima, naji ana kiran sallah''.

'' toh Momcy ki gaidamin Dady anjima zan kirawo shi, Sageer kuwa nasan yanzu yana islamiyya''.

'' ai kuwa sai 6:00pm zai dawo''.

'' toh a gaidamin shi idan ya dawo''


'' tom shikenan''.


Aje wayar tayi tana fad'in'' Yaya lokacin sallah yayi''.

Murmushi yayi yace ''toh amma fa banajin zan iya zuwa masallaci''.

'' babu komai ai sai kayi a gida''

'' toh barina tashi''.


***** ***** ****


Misalin 8:20pm suna zaune a dining suna cin abincin, Khairat ce ta kalli Sadeeq tace'' uhm wai Yaya a ina ka koyi girki?''.

Kallonta yayi yace'' a ina ke kika koya?''.

'' ni ai Momcy ta koyamin''.

'' toh nima Momy ta koyamin''.

'' hmmm nidai ban yarda ba, kawai bazaka fad'amin bane''.

'' wasa nake miki, tun ina india na koyi girki''.

''Owk ashe dolace ta sanya ka koya''.

'' eh naji ''. ya fad'a yana hararar ta.

Suna kammalawa tace'' Yaya barinaje nayi wanka''.

'' owk barinaje nayi nima kawai''.

D'akinta ta shiga, ta cire kayan jikinta ta d'aura towel ta shige toilet, tana fitowa taje ta shafe jikinta  cream da turaruka masu kamshi, rigar bacci ta d'auko mara nauyi wacce bata kai 'kasa ba, sannan ta sanya hula a kanta.

Harta juya zata fita, sai ta dawo ta d'auki hijab ta sanya. saboda yau wani irin kunya Sadeeq takeji, ba yau ya saba ganinta da kayan bacci ba, amma batajin yau xata iya tsayawa ya kalleta a haka, tana sanya hijab d'in ta bud'e 'kofa ta fito''.

Tana fitowa shima yana fitowa daga d'akinsa, kallonta kawai yake, dariya taso  ta bashi ganin ta da hijab, amma saiya basar, kujera ya samu ya zauna yana kallon ta, 'karasowa tayi ta zauna kusa dashi, saboda tasan bazai barta ta zauna nesa dashi ba.

'Kara matsota yayi yana fad'in'' wannan hijab d'in fa?''.

Shuru tayi ta kasa bashi amsa, ganin bazatayi magana bane ya sanya yasa hannun ya cire hijab d'in, rufe idanunta tayi, shikuwa 'kamshin turaran tane  ya daki hancinsa, lumshe idanunsa yayi ya bud'esu a kanta, rigar baccin ya kalla yaga yadda tayi matu'kar yimata kyau, pink colour da milk ce rigar, 'kasa yayi da kansa, yasa bakinsa dai-dai kunnenta yace'' yanzu wannan kyawun ne ba'aso na gani?''.

Murmushi tayi tace'' a'a kunyarka nakeji ne''.

'' uhm nidai dan Allah ki aje kunyar nan, saboda zata takuramin ne''.

'' toh'' kawai tace masa.

Zama sukayi suna kallo suna fira cikin nishad'i, sai wajan 11;00pm Khairat ta kalleshi tace'' Yaya bacci nakeji''.

'' uhm toh barina tashi nima, saboda zaman bazaimin dad'i babu ke ba''

Mi'kewa yayi yace'' muje ''.

D'akinta ta nufa tana fad'in'' sai da safe''

Kallon ta kawai yayi, baice komai ba, harta shige d'akinta, saida ya jima a tsaye, sannan ya koma kan kujerar da suka tashi, kwanciya yayi akan kujerar saboda yasan koda Yakima d'akinsa bazai samu iya yin baccin ba.


Khairat nashiga d'akinta ta kwanta, kusan 15mns amma ta kasa yin bacci, tunani take kamar bata kyauta masa ba, data wuce d'akinta kai tsaye, kenan ma hakan yana  nufin basu daina rayuwar da sukeyi ba.


Tashi tayi da niyyar fitowa parlour, bawai dan tasan abinda xatayi a parlourn ba, ita dai tasan bazata iya bud'e d'akinsa ta shiga ba.

Tana bud'e 'kofar tayi turus, ganin Sadeeq kwance a kan kujera, 'karasawa tayi inda yake, idanunsa a lumshe amma daga gani kasan ba bacci yake ba, tayi tunanin baisan da zuwanta ba, ganin baiko motsa ba.

Shiko Sadeeq tunda ta taho yaji kamshin turaran ta.

Du'kawa tayi ta sanya hannunta tana shafa kansa, a hankalin ya bud'e idanunsa ya waresu a kanta, murmushi tayi masa, shima maida mata martani yayi, mi'kewa tayi zatayi magana, hannunsa ya sanya ya jawota ta fad'a jikinsa.

''Uhm yaya yanzu danaji ciwo fa?'' ta fad'a taba cinno baki.

Magana zaiyi amma saiya fasa, ya sanya d'an yatsansa yana zagaye lips d'inta, lumshe idanunta tayi saboda wani ba'kon yanayi da takeji a jikinta.

Hannun sa ya d'auke daga kan lips d'inta saboda yadda yakeji a ransa, gaba d'aya wani mugun feeling yakeji na addabarsa, da kasalalliyar murya yayi mata magana.

'' my Deejah tashi kije ki kwanta kinga dare yayi''.

Kallonsa tayi tace'' Yaya nifa banaso na barka kai d'aya kaga bakada lafiya''.

'' uhm hakadai kikace bayan yanzuma tafiyar ki kikayi kika barni''.

'' kajika bagashi na dawo ba''.

'' toh zaki kwana a d'akina?''.


Gyad'a masa kai kawai tayi alamun eh, 'kokarin tashi take ya sanya hannunsa ya ri'keta, tashi yayi ya d'auketa kamar 'har baby ya wuce d'akinsa da ita.

Ita kuwa rufe idanunta tayi, suna shiga d'akin ya kwantar da ita, sannan shima ya kwanta, rage hasken d'akin yayi, sannan yayi addu'a ya tofe d'akin, sannan yayiwa Babyn tashi, sannan ya jawota jikin sa ya rungume, sannan ya jawo blanket ya lullubesu yana shafa kanta, a haka bacci yayi yawan gaba dasu.

 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_



Da asuba Khairat ce ta fara tashi, mamaki ne ya rufeta jin har yanxu rungume take a 'kirjin Sadeek, shi kuwa baccin sa kawai yake, face d'insa ta 'kurawa idanu tana godewa Allah daya mallaka mata Sadeek a matsayin miji, ji take kamar tafi dukkan mata sa'a, a gidan duniya.

Zame jikinta tayi zata tashi ta shiga toilet, ri'kota yayi, da sauri ta juyo ta kalli fuskar shi tana mamakin yadda akayi ya tashi.

Murmushi kawai yayi mata, ya mi'ke ya shiga toilet.

Saida ya fito sannan ta shiga, tana fitowa ana tayar da sallah, hijab ta sanya tabi jam'i a sallayar daya shimfid'a musu.

Bayan sun idar da sallah, Khairat tace'' Yaya ina kwana?''.

''Lpy kalau, hope dai ina fatan kin tashi lafiya?''.

'' lafiya 'kalau''

Shuru sukayi zuwa wani lokaci Khairat tace'' uhm Yaya nifa bacci zan koma, tunda nasan dai yau bazakaje hospital ba''.

'' eh gaskiya bazan jeba ai bangama warkewa ba''.

'' uhm kaidai kawai kace baza kaje ba, amma ka warke''. 

'' eh naji ''.

Tashi tayi zata tafi, ri'ko hannunta yayi yace'' yanzu baccin ma tafiya za'ayi ayishi batare daniba, naga alama ba'aji dani fa''.

'' kwantar da hankalin ka, tashi muje, akan baccin safiya zakace banajin da kai''

Bai cemata komai ba ya tashi ya haye gadon, kallon sa kawai tayi taje ta cire hijab d'in, sannan ta dawo ta kwanta.

Jawota yayi jikinsa yana shafa bayanta, tareda sakin wata nannauyar ajjiyar zuciya, gaba d'aya hankalin sa a tashe yake, yana bu'katar Matarsa, amma kuma bazai iya yimata komai a yanzu ba, yanaso ya tabbatar mata da cewar ita d'in yake so, ba wani abu na jikinta ba, gani yake idan ya bu'kaci wani abu a wajanta a yanzu zataga kamar dama abinda yakeso kenan.

Wani irin ba'kon yanayi takeji a jikinta, daurewa kawai take, kasan cewarta  mace mai 'karfin sha'awa, mamaki take bata tab'a tunanin Sadeek zai barta batare da yayi mata komai ba, a jiya.

Iska taji ya hura mata a fuska, bud'e idanunta tayi, fuskarta d'auke da murmushi, shima murmushin yayi mata yace'' my Deejah murmushinki yana matu'kar burgeni''

'' uhm Yaya dan bakada naka bane''

'' anya kuwa?''.

'' sosai ma''.

Murmushi ta sakeyi, a karo na biyu, hannunsa ya sanya, yana zagayeta lips d'inta, runtse idanunta tayi tana murmushi, bata ankara ba taji bakinsa cikin nata, cikin kwarewa yake kissing d'inta, jin yana 'kokarin kai hannunsa jikinta ne, ga numfashin ta har wani sar'kewa yake, da sauri ta zame bakinta daga nasa tana maida numfashi.

'Dago jajayen idanunsa yayi ya kalleta, da sauri tayi 'kasa da kanta tana fad'in'' uhm Yaya bacci nakeji fah''.

Bai cemata komai ba, sai jawota yayi, ya dad'a rungumeta kamar zai maidata ciki, wani mugun tausayin sa taji, kasan cewar yarda takejin bugun zuciyarsa.

Shafa kanta ya shigayi, har zuwa lokacin bai iya cemata komai ba, sun jima a haka, har zuwa lokacin da bacci ya d'auketa.

Saida ya jima kafin shima baccin ya kwashe shi.


***** ***** ***** *****


Misalin 'karfe 9:00am Sadeek ya tashi, ganin Khairat bata tashi bane, ya sanya ya zame jikinsa daga nata a hankali, toilet ya shiga yayi brush, yana fitowa ya fice daga d'akin.

Parlour ya fito ya samu waje ya zauna, bai jima da zama ba, ya mi'ke kamar wanda aka tsikara, sakkowa yayi zuwa parlourn 'kasa, kai tsaye ya wuce kitchen.

Yana shiga ya kunna gas, indomei da kwai ya soya musu, sannan ya dafa musu ruwan tea ya zuba a plaks, had'a na baba  mai gadi yayi ya mi'ka masa, sannan ya koma d'akin.

Yana shiga ya tadda  Khairat ta tashi tana salati, kallonta yayi yana murmushi, itama murmushi tayi masa tana fad'in'' shine koka tasheni, na had'a mana break fast, gashi yanzu har 10:12am fa''.

'' karki damu aina had'a mana ''

Murmushi kawai tayi tana fad'in'' barinaje d'akina nayi brush'', dan a tunanin ta wasa yake.

Bai cemata komai ba, ta tashi ta fice. shima parlourn ya fito.

Bai jima da zama ba ta fito, zama tayi kusa dashi, kallonta yayi yace'' ko nan zaki kawo mana break fast d'in?''.

Kallonsa tayi kawai tace'' toh '', mamaki take ganin yana magana cikeda gaskiyar sa.

Tana shiga kitchen taga kulan daya juye indomei d'in, dakuma plaks d'in tea d'in, murmushi kawai tayi ta d'auko kulan ta fito, don ita duk a tunanin ta wasa yake.

Tana zuwa ta aje tana fad'in'' ashe da gaske kake, ammafa kayi 'ko'kari''.

'' Allah koh ''.

'' eh mana ''.

'' hmm karfa ki zugani ''.

'' haba ai kawuce zuga a wajena''.

Tana fad'a ta juya tabar parlourn, dawowa tayi hannunta ri'ke da pleat, zama tayi ta zuba musu abincin suka soma ci.


***** ***** ***** *****


1 week kenan da shiryawa Sadeek da Khairat, rayuwar su tayi matu'kar sauyawa, soyayya suke nunawa junansu, bazaka tab'a tunanin cewar wani abu ya tab'a faruwa tsakanin su ba, zuwa yanzu Sadeek ya warware, ya koma aiki.

Ta bangaran Khairat kuwa batada wata matsala, sai dai har yanxu ta rasa dalilin dayasa har zuwa yanzu Sadeek bai bu'kaci ha'k'kinsa a wajanta ba, sai dai idan abin ya motsa masa yazo ya rage zafi da abinda ba'a rasa ba, zuwa yanzu harta saba da wannan d'abi'ar tashi.


Yau kwana 8 kenan da sallamar Sadeek daga hospital, kuma yauce ranar da Sadeek ya kuduri niyyar karb'ar ha'k'kinsa wajan Matar shi, don zuwa yanzu ya gaji da kwalelen da yake yiwa kansa, koba hakaba yasan yana shiga ha'k'kin Khairat ta wannan bangaran, domin ya lura tanada 'kafin sha'awa.


5:00pm dai-dai Sadeek ya dawo daga hospital, Khairat na zaune  parlour Sadeek ya shigo bakinsa d'auke da sallama, da guda Khairat ta taso ta rungume shi tana fad'in'' ur welcome Yayana, shine yau baka dawo da wuriba ko?''.

''Yi ha'kuri my Deejah, aikine ya ri'ke ni ''

'Karasawa sukayi har d'akisa, zuwa tayi ta had'a masa ruwan wanka, sannan ta dawo ta tayashi rage kayan jikinsa, zuwa yanzu harta saba, idan zai shiga wanka saita tayashi cire kaya, in ta'ki kuwa saiya ce bazaiyi wankan ba, tun banajin kunya harta saba.

Yana fitowa ya taddata tana fito masa da kayan da zai sanya, bataji fitowar shiba sai jinshi tayi ya rungumeta ta baya, jiyowa tayi tana fad'in'' Yaya harka fito?''.

Hannu yasanya ya ja siririn hancinta yace'' uhm wai sau nawa zan fad'a miki banason wannan sunan?''.

'' Toh zan daina '', ta fad'a tana cinno baki.

Bakinsa yayi 'kokarin kaiwa nata, da sauri ta kauce tana fad'in'' uhm muje kasa kaya, sai kaci abinci''.

Hannunta ya ri'ke yana fad'in'' yarinya gwarama ki daina wani gocewa, dan yau babu fashi saina karb'i abuna''.

Bata cemasa komai bata fice daga d'akin, 

Bayan sun gama cin abincin suna zaune suna fira, kallonta yayi yace'' my Deejah yanzu idan aka kawowa amarya kifi a matsayin kazar da ake kawowa ta amarci zata amsa?''.

Dariya magar tashi ta bata, saida tayi mai isarta sannan tace'' kifi fa Kece?''.

'' eh mana ''

'' chab yaseen idan nice bazan ciba''. 


Murmushi yayi yace'' ai gwara ita, watama ko kafin bata samu ba ''.

Ta fahimci da ita yake dan haka tace'' oho dai wad'an da basu samu d'inba, suma sunanan har sai ankawo musu sannan mutum zai fanshe kayansa''.

Dariya yayi yace'' insuka kwantar da hankalin su tasu special ce''.

'' Hmmm '' kawai tace masa ya tashi ya shiga kitchen, kallonsa kawai tayi tana mamakin mai zaiyi a kitchen.

Kusan 20min bataga ya fitoba, dan haka ta tashi ta bishi kitchen d'in, tana shiga taga alamun girkin zeyi, kallonsa tayi tace'' Yaya mezaka d'ora baka fad'amin nayi maka ba''.

Batare daya kalleta ba yace'' wannan aikin nawa ne, karki damu''.

'' toh'' kawai tace masa ta fito daga kitchen d'in.

Tana fitowa ana fara kiraye-kirayen sallah, ganinsa shima tayi ya fito, saida ya sanar Mata ya tafi masallaci sannan ya fita.

Tana zaune a parlour ya shigo, kai tsaye kitchen ya shiga kallonsa kawai tayi tana mamakin mai yakeyi a kitchen.

Tana zaune taji kamshi ya cika parlourn, kamarta le'ka amma ta share saboda tasan duk runtsi sai taga gani.

Sai bayan  sallar ishsha'i sannan ya kammala, lokacin daya fito tana d'akinta tana wanka, don tasan yau babu girkin dare, saboda tayi tunanin girkin yake musu.

Yana ganin batanan ya wuce d'akinsa ya watsa ruwa, sannan ya fito ya zauna a parlour.

Bai jima da zama ba ta fito, tana 'karasawa ta samu kusa dashi ta zauna, tana fad'in'' barka da dare Yaya''.

Matsawa yayi kusa da ita, ya janyota jikinsa yana fad'in'' wai yaushe za'a daina 'kirana da sunan Yaya ne''.

Rude fuskarta tayi tana fad'in'' toh ai manta wa nake ''.

Bakinsa yakai dai-dai saitin kunnen ta yace'' my Deejah mekika tanada min ne kinsan yau zan amshi ha'k'kina''.

Shuru tayi, tana tunanin maganar sa, gani take kamar wasa yake, don haka tace'' Yaya yunwa nakeji''.

Harara ya watsa mata yace'' T girki ne?''.

'' oh toh ba Kai bane ka shiga kitchen d'in ka'ki fitowa ''.

'' kazar amarcinki na tanada miki, saboda kince bazakici kifi ba''.

'' Yaya dan Allah ka daina wannan wasan, ka d'auko mana abinda ka dafa muci''.

'' au kin d'aukar wasa nake miki?''.

'' tunda baki yarda ba shikenan, saiki tsaya ganin zahiri''.

Shuru tayi saboda ta fuskanci gaskiya yake fad'a mata, jikinta ne yayi sanyi, wani tsoransa taji ya shigeta, juyawa tayi ta kalleshi tace'' ni xanje na kwanta''.

Kallonta yayi yaga yadda yanayinta ya sauya, tawani yi kalar tausayi, tausayi ta bashi, hannunta ya ri'ko yana murzawa cikin nashi, murya 'kasa-'kasa yace'' Khairat banason ganinki cikin damuwa, ki kwantar da hankalin ki, indai dan tanine, karki damu bazan tab'a yimiki abinda bakyaso ba, zanyi ha'kuri har zuwa lokacin da kika amince mini''.

Shuru tayi tana tunanin, tabbas idan ta'ki amince masa bata kyauta mai ba, yayi ha'kurin da ba kowane namiji ne zaiyi mata irinsa ba, koba wannan ba, tana tsoron fushin ubangiji a kanta.

Kanta ta d'ora a 'kirjinsa tana fad'in'' Yaya bazan tab'a yimaka iyaka, da wani abu nawa ba, bare wannan ha'k'kin kane, bakada matsala dani, duk lokacin daka bu'kata zan amsa maka''.

Ba 'karamin dad'in maganar ta yaji ba, rungume ta yayi yana samata albarka.


Misalin 10:00pm  Khairat ta tashi ta shiga d'akinta, ruwan wanka ta had'a wanda yaji turaruka masu kamshi, saida ta gama dizar jikinta sannan ta fito, mayuka masu kamshi, da turaruka da humra ta shafe jikinta dasu, rigar bacci Mara nauyi ta d'auko ta sanya, harta juyo xata fita ta tuna da wani had'in zuma da Momy ta bawa Aisha ta kawo mata, d'aukowa tayi ta aha, sannan ta fito d'aga d'akin.

Bata sameshi a parlour ba don haka ta wuce d'akinsa, tana shiga yana fitowa daga wanka, taba ganin sa tayi 'kasa da kanta, don zuwa yanzu tsoron sa take, kawai tana dakewa ne.

Bai cemata komai ba ya wuce, ya kimtsa kansa, jallabiya ya d'auko ya sanya, taba zaune tana kallonsa ya d'auko sallaya ya shimfid'a,  sannan ya kalleta yace'' kinada alwala?''.

Kai kawai ta d'ago masa, yace ta sanya hijab suyi sallah, tashi tayi ta d'auko hijab d'in ta tsaya bayansa suka tada sallah.

Raka'a biyu sukayi, suna idarwa ya daga kanta yayi mata addu'a, suna kammala, yashiga yimata tambayoyi akan abinda ya shafi addini, babu laifi ta cinye jarabawar, hakan kuma ba 'karamin dad'i yayi masa ba.

Tashi yayi ya fita, tana zaune ya shigo da wani pleat wanda yake a rufe, tareda madarar hollandia ya aje a gaban ta, sannan shima ya zauna, hannun yasanya ya bud'e pleat d'in, wata katuwar kazace a ciki saizuba  'kamshi take.

Kallonsa kawai tayi, d'aga mata gira yayi yace'' yau kinzam amaryar da zataci kazar da mijinta ya had'a mata a first night d'inta.

Murmushi kawai tayi masa, cups ya d'auka ya zuba masu madarar, babu yadda baiyi ba Khairat ta sanya hannun suci kazar amma ta'ki, 'karshe dai shi da kansa yadinga yagar naman yana bata, tareda madarar.

Taci naman da d'an yawa babu laifi, kwashe kayan yayi ya kai kitchen.

Yana dawowa ya taddata harta kwanta, ta du'kun 'kune kanta, murmushi kawai yayi, yaje ya kashe hasken d'akin, sannan ya cire jallabiyar ya kwanta.

Janyota jikinsa yayi, yanajin yadda gabanta ke fad'uwa, bakinsa yakai dai-dai kunnen ta yana fad'a  mata wasu kalamai wanda ni kaina banji abinda yake fad'a mataba.

Hancisa yakai dai-dai wuyanta yana sha'kar daddad'an kamshin turaran ta, daga haka salon ya fara sauyawa, tun Khairat tana no'kewa harta saki jiki.

Gaba d'aya Sadeek ya gama rud'ewa, ganin yana shirin jefa 'kwallonsa a raga ne ya sanya na jamusu 'kofa nace asuba ta gadi.

 *Page   44* 



 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_





Iya gurzuwa Khairat ta gurzu, tun tana kuka tana bashi ha'kuri, tana kiran Momcy, Momy, Dady, har saida ta kasa magana, hawaye kawai takeyi,

Misalin 3:00am na koma d'akin, Khairat  na gani kwance a 'kirjin Sadeek, sai lallab'ata yake, yana zuba mata kalamai masu dad'i, banda zubar hawaye babu abinda take, gaba d'aya jikinta ya mutu, kwantar mata da hankalin ya shiga yi, yana shafa bayan ta, har bacci yayi gaba da ita.

Yanajin tayi bacci ya 'kurawa face d'inta ido, wani nishad'i yakeji wanda bai tab'a jin irinsa ba, tabbas bashida abinda zai iya sakawa Khairat dashi, sakamakon killace masa kanta da tayi, harta kawo masa budurcin ta, tabbas Khairat ta cika mace.

Sake kallonta yayi,  yana yiwa Allah godiya, daya bashi mace ta gari, dole yayi alfahari da ita a matsayin matarsa, saboda ta gama had'a abinda yake so, asali, ilimi, kyau, uwa uba kuma ga tsabta.

A hankali ya zameta daga jikinsa, toilet ya shiga, ya tsarkake jikinsa sannan yayi alwala, yana fitowa ya shimfid'a sallaya, sallah raka'a biyu yayi, yashiga mi'ka godiyarsa ga ubangiji.

Yana zaune har akayi assalatu, yana tada sallah Khairat ta farka, tareda salati a bakinta, 'ko'karin tashi tayi amma ta kasa, jitake ko ina na jikinta ciwo yake,  kuka ta shigayi mai 'karamin sauti.

Sadeek na sallamewa yayi addu'a ya shafa,  sannan ya tawo wajanta, janyota jikinsa yayi yana fad'in'' my Deejah meya sameki?''.

Tureshi tayi tana fad'in'' ba kai bane, inata baka ha'kuri amma ka'ki saurarena, gashi yanzu ko tashi na kasa yi''. takuma fashewa dawani kukan.

'' Oh yi ha'kuri toh bazan kuma ba, kinji''.

Batace masa komai ba, ya sake cewa'' barina kaiki toilet kiyi wanka, sai kiyi sallah''.

Gar dama ta shiga yimasa, dan haka ya kyaketa, ya tashi ya shiga toilet, ruwan mai d'an zafi saisa-saisa ya had'a mata ya zuba dettol a ciki,sannan ya dawo, bai cemata komai ba ya sanya hannun biyu ya ciccib'ota, ya nufi toilet da ita, sai zille-zille take, yana shiga ya zare rigar data lullub'e jikinta da ita, cikin ruwan ya sanya ta, 'kara ta saki xata fita daga ruwan, yayi saurin ri'keta.

Saida yaga ruwan ya gama ratsata, tafara jin dad'in sa, sannan ya fita ya jamata 'kofa, zanin gadon ya cire, ya sauya wani, sannan yaje d'akinta ya d'auko mata, riga, ya aje mata, sannan ya samu waje ya zauna.

Khairat kuwa saida ta sake had'a wani ruwan, ta shiga sannan tayi wankan tsarki, tayi wanka, ta d'aura alwala.

Tsayawa tayi ganin babu abinda zata sanya, sai wani d'an 'karamin towel da bai wuce cinya ba, ganin babu yadda zatayi ne, ya sanya ta d'auko shi ta sanya ta fito.

Yanajin fitowar ta ya waigo, 'kura mata idanu yayi, da sauri ta kauda kanta gefe, jikin bango ta samu ta rakub'e, rigar daya d'auko mata ya d'auka ya mi'ko mata, kallonsa kawai tayi ta d'auke kai, murmushi yayi ya taso ta mi'ka mata, amsa tayi ta sanya. ta d'auki hijab ta sanya, sannan ta tada sallah.

Tana idarwa, ta mi'ke ta nufi 'kofar fita, da sauri ya tashi ya ri'ko ta.

'' ina zakije kuma?''.

'' Uhm zan koma d'akina ne ''.

'' Kiyi ha'kuri kizo ki kwanta, nasan bacci bai isheki ba, anjima sai koma d'akinki''.

Bata musa masa ba, ta cire hijab d'in, taje ta kwanta, zuwa yayi kusa da ita ya kwanta, da sauri ta juya tana kallonsa, murmushi yayi mata yace'' Uhm matsoraciya babu abinda zanyi miki, kike saurin jiyowa''.

Bata cemaka komai ba, sai 'kasa da tayi da kanta, hannunsa ya sanya ya janyota jikinsa, bata musa masa ba, ta kwanta tana sha'kar kamshin turaran sa. shafa kanta yayi yace'' nagode sosai da jiyar dani, da'din da ban tab'a jin kwatan kwacin saba, Allah yayi miki albarka, ya bamu zuri'a d'ayyiba, yasa ki haifa min d'iya mai kama da ke''.

Murmushi tayi, murya 'kasa-'kasa tace'' ameen ''.

Haka yaita zuba mata surutu, da sanya albarka, har bacci ta kwashe ta.

Lokacin data tashi, harya had'a musu break fast, har d'aki ya kawo mata, abaki ya ringa bata abincin, ira kuwa sai narkewa take, tana zuba shagwab'a, banda rarrashinta babu abinda yake.

Wunin ranar ko aiki bai jeba, yana tare da my Deejah tana zuba masa shagwab'a.



***** ***** ***** *****

Ban garan doctor Heesham kuwa, tun sanda Aisha sukazo hospital, ya d'ora idonsa a kanta ya kasa samu nutsuwa.

Gaba d'aya tunanin Aisha ya gama addabarsa, yanason yiwa Sadeek maganar ta, amma saiya kasa, ganin bashida yadda zaiyi ne ya sanya ya yanke shawarar samun Sadeek d'in.


***** ***** ***** *****


Khairat kuwa soyayya Sadeek yake nuna mata, kamar babu gobe, zuwa yanzu basuda wata damuwa, zuwa yanzu ita ta cire kunya, nunawa mijinta kulawa take ta ko'ina, damuwar ta d'aya shine Sadeek ya fiya,'kunata, tun tana bijire masa harta ha'kura ta saki jiki, saboda tasan cewar bashida wata bayan ita, dole ta jure bu'katuwar sa.


Kamar kullum Khairat na zaune a parlour, misalin 12:30, taji sallamar Sadeek, da sauri ta juyo ta amsa tana fad'in'' hubby lafiya na ganka yanzu?''.

Bai cemata komai ba, itama tashi tayi ta d'auko masa, juice mai sanye tareda cup, aje wata zata bud'e ta zuba, da sauri ya ri'keta yana fad'in'' uhm nifa banshan juice d'innan''.

'' Toh me kakeso?'', ta fad'a tana kallonsa.

'' Kinfi kowa sanin meya dawo dani gida'', ya fad'a yana kashe mata 1 eyes.

Sare ta gane mai yake nufi, amma saita basar tace'' toh nidai ban sani.......


Bai jira ta 'karasa ba ya had'e bakinsu, babu musu ta fara maida masa marrani, ganin zaman parlourn baxai yimusu bane ya sanya, ya d'auketa yayi d'aki da ita.

Bayan komai ya kankama, Khairat na kwance jikin Sadeek yana, shafa kanta, muryar ta na rawa ta fara yimasa magana.

''Hubby dama inaso zamuyi magana ne''.

''Owk ina sauraron ki''.

'' Dama akan komawata school ne, kaga yanzu baifi 9 mouths mu kammala ba''.

Shuru yayi mata, bai cemata komai ba, ya tashi ya shiga toilet, binsa kawai tayi da idanu, tana tunanin toh mai yake nufi?.

Tana zaune ya fito, lokacin ana kiran sallah, jallabiya ya sanya, ya shimfid'a sallaya ya tada sallah.

Tashi tayi itama ta shiga toilet d'in.

Lokacin data idar da sallah bayanan, yana parlour, fitowa parlourn tayi ta sameshi tsaye yana jiranta, zai koma hospital, kallonsa tayi da mamaki tace'' Hubby badai hospital zaka koma ba?''.

'' Eh can zan koma''.

'Bata rai tayi tace'' dan Allah Hubby karka tafi''.

Bai cemata komai ba, ya juya abinsa zai fita, tsugunawa tayi ta fashe da kuka, da sauri ya juyo, ya sanya hannun ya d'agota yana fad'in'' toh miye abin kuka kuma?''.

Cikeda shagwab'a tace'' ba kai bane, da idan nace karka koma zama kakeyi, amma shine yau zakayi tafiyarka, ko kulani ma bakayi  ba''.

''Shikenan yi ha'kuri bazan sakeba''.

'' A'a hubby niya kamata na baka ha'kuri, bansan cewar maganar komawa ta school zata b'ata maka, rai ba, shiyasa nayi''.

'' Amma kayi ha'kuri insha Allah bazan kuma ba, indai maganar komawa school ne, na ha'kura''.

Murmushi yayi yace'' my Deejah ba ina nufin bazan barki ki koma school bane, amma kinsan inada matu'kar kishi, kiyimin al'kawarin zaki kulamin da kanki kinji''.

Cikeda murna tace'' insha Allah ba d'auka maka al'kawarin kula maka da kaina''.

Rungumeta yayi yana fad'in'' Allah ya baki iko, Deejah nah''.

'' Ameen Hubby''

 *Page   46* 



 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_



Suna isa gida Khairat ta zauna a parlour tana fad'in'' Hubby wallahi duk na gaji''.


Zama yayi kusa da ita yaja hancinta yace'' ai ke kika siya, don haka babu ruwana kawai ki tashi, kije ki d'ora min abinda zan ci, dan babu abinda naci, tun break fast''.


Shagwab'e fuska tayi kamar za tayi kuka tace'' haba Hubby,  baka ganin yadda na gaji ne, da yawa, ka taimaka ka shiga kayi mana''.


Murmushi yayi yace'' na yadda zanyi miki girki, amma fa sai mun shiga ciki an bani abinci na na safe da aka hanani, ana d'okin zuwa school''.


Tashi tayi tana fad'in'' na yafe, indai saboda nace kayi girki ne toh zanyi da kaina''.


Dariya kawai yayi mata, ya tashi ya nufi part d'insa.



***** ***** ***** *****


Bayan sallah isha'i Khairat na zaune a parlour tana kallo, Sadeek ya fito sanye cikin dakakkiyar gizner light blue, sai zuba 'kamshi yake, a hankalin Khairat ta d'ago tana kallonsa, wani mugun kyau taga yayi, kamar bashi ba, a zuciyarta  godiya tayi ga ubangiji daya mallaka mata Sadeek a matsayin miji, babu 'karya gaskiya Sadeek kyakykyawa ne babu 'karya.


'Karasowa inda take yayi ya zauna yana fad'in'' my Deejah na sauya miki ne naga kin 'kura min idanu''.


'' Uhm ko d'aya, kawai dai naga kayi shiga ne, kamar wanda zai je tad'i ''.


'' Kai my Deejah gaskiya kin iya hasashe, harkin gano inda zani''.


Kallonsa kawai tayi tace'' toh a dawo lafiya, in kaje ka gaida min ita''.


Tana fad'in haka ta mi'ke da niyyar barin parlourn, ri'kota yayi, ya zaunar da ita akan cinyarsa yana murmushi yace'' my Deejah ba dai kishin ne ya motsa ba?''


'' Toh ni na ce maka ina kishi? kawai cewa nayi ka gaida min ita''.


'' toh amma ko kice zaki rakani?"


Haushin maganar ta fara ji, don abin ya fara isar ta haka.


'' Kaga ni bazan hanaka zuwa ba, amma dan Allah idan zakaje, karka kuma fad'a min, wai ina ruwana ma da zuwan ka tad'i?"


Dariya kawai yayi yace'' ohhh Allah, my Deejah ai bansan haka kike da kishi ba, wasa nake yi miki fa, babu gurin wadda zanje, don haka ki sanyawa ranki ke kad'ai ce mata a gurin  Sadeek, bana tunanin zan iya yi miki kishiya, amma bawai nayi miki al'kawari bane, saboda kana taka ne Allah na nashi, kuma nashin shine dai-dai''.


Kallonsa kawai tayi, domin taji dad'in maganar sa, a shagwab'e tace'' toh amma ina zakaje haka?.


'' Zanje gida ne, saboda inaso zamuyi magana da Dady''.


Sai a lokacin taji nutsuwarta ta dawo gangar jikinta, don haka ta sa masa daru ita a dole sai yaje da ita, babu yadda ya iya dole, ya sanya ta d'auko hijab suka tafi.


Sun jima a gidan, har misalin 10:20, Sadeek bai b'ata lokaci ba wajan sanarwa, dasu Dady maganar doctor Heesham da kuma waye shi.


Dady ya amince da maganar, ya bashi damar yazo wajen Aisha ya nemi soyayyar ta, idan ta amince toh shikenan, idan kuma bata amince masa ba, sai yayi ha'kuri saboda baza su yiwa d'iyar ta su auran dole ba.


Godiya Sadeek ya yiwa Dady, sannan Momy taje ta kira Khairat a d'akin Aisha suka tafi.


***** ***** ***** *****


Washe gari Sadeek da zuwa hospital ya sanar da doctor Heesham yadda sukayi da Dady, doctor Heesham yaji dad'in hakan, yayiwa Sadeek godiya akan cewar zaije gidan, kasancewar yasan gidan, shiga ne kawai be tab'a yi ba.


Yau ce ranar da ta kama Dadyn Abdul sun je nema masa auran Maryam, basu wani sha wahala ba, kasancewar an san da zuwan nasu, babu wani b'ata lokaci aka tsaida lokacin auran nasu, aka sanya 9 months, bayan ta kammala school kenan.


Zo ku ga farin ciki gun Maryam da Abdul, dan Abdul ji yake kamar yama rigaya da ya samu Maryam kenan, godiya kawai suke mi'kawa ga ubangiji.



Khairat na kwance jikin Sadeek tana zuba masa shagwab'a, bayan sun dawo daga siyo ice cream d'in data matsa masa, suka fita suka siyo, shi kuwa gogan sai lallab'ata yake, dan ba 'karamin burgeshi take ba idan tana wannan shagwab'ar.


Ta cikin shan ice cream d'in, wayarta ta shiga ruri, ta mi'ka hannu zata d'auka Sadeek ya d'auke wayar.


Marairaice fuska tayi tace''  Uhm hubby dan Allah ka bani na amsa''.


'' Na'ki d'in, duk wanda ya kiraki ai yasan ba'a kiran matar aure after sallahr isha'i ''.


'' To naji, amma kuma idan Momy ce ko Momcy fa?''


Shiru yayi, kana ya mi'ka mata wayar, tana dubawa taga sunan Maryam, juyowa tayi tace'' Hubby Maryam ce, na kira?''


Shiru yayi saboda yasan maganar da zata fad'a mata, dan gaba d'aya ya manta bai sanar mata ba.


'Daga mata kai kawai yayi alamun eh.


Kiran wayar Maryam d'in tayi, ringing 2 ta amsa, saida suka gaisa sannan Maryam tace'' besty ai nayi fushi, ko ki kirani ki tayani murna''.


'' murnar me kuma?.


'' kar dai kice Sadeek bai fad'a miki ba, kinsan na fad'a miki yau Abdul yace min Dadyn su zasu zo nema masa aurena''.


'' Eh kin fad'a min, sunzo ne?''


'' Eh mana har an sanya lokacin bayan mun kammala school ''.


Ihun murna Khairat tayi tace'' Kai beasty amma na tayaki farin ciki, Allah ya sanya alkairi, kice dolane muyi shaku shaku'', ta fad'a tana yiwa Sadeek gwalo.


'Kwafa kawai yayi yana kallonta har ta gama wayar ta kashe, kallonsa tayi tace'' uhm Hubby yau tamu da kai a cikin gidan nan ba zatayi kyau ba, tunda ka'ki sanar dani sa ranar besty''.


Bai bata amsa ba, sai cewa yayi'' ohh har wani shaku- shaku zaki kwasa koh?.


Dariya tayi tace'' Eh mana, ai ranar ko 'yan mata sai dai su gan mu su kyale''.


'' Hmm Allah yasa lokacin kinada 'katon ciki, irin 8 month d'innan''.


'' Uhm toh ai kuwa ba zanyi cikin ba, tunda haka kace''.


'' Toh idan zai shiga fad'a miki zaiyi?''


'' A'a amma daga yau bazan kuma bari ko d'aki d'aya mu kwana ba''.


'' Haka kika ce?''


'' Eh haka na fad'a''.


'' Toh bari ki gani, yanzu ma za'a fara'', ya fad'a yana 'ko'karin tashi.


Ai da gudu ta tashi, binta yayi suka fara zagaye parlourn, sai data gaji sannan ta zube a kujera, tana maida numfashin, zama yayi ya janyota jikinsa yana fad'in'' kin gama guje- gujen?.


'' Yi ha'kuri Hubby na gaji ne''.


'' Kinga kuwa yanzu zanyi abinda nace''.


'' Uhm Hubby dan Allah......


Bai jira ta 'karasa ba ya had'e bakinsu guri d'aya, itama biye masa tayi, ganin abun bana 'karewa bane ya sanya ya d'auketa sukayi d'aki.


***** ***** ***** *****


'Bangaran doctor Heesham kuwa, yau yayi shirinsa tsab ya nufi gidansu Sadeek.


Yana zuwa yayi horn gate man ya 'karaso inda yake, nan ya sanar masa yazo wajan Aisha ne.


Babu b'ata lokaci ya shiga cikin gidan, Momy ya gani a parlour ya sanar mata, tanaji tasan waye don haka tace yayi masa iso, zuwa sashen ba'ki.


Yana fita ta shiga d'akin Aisha ta sanar mata tayi ba'ko, har tace ba zata je ba, Momy tace taje ta duba taga waye, bata musa ba, ta tashi ta shirya ta fito.


Lokacin da taga doctor Heesham ta ganeshi tsab, don haka ta 'karasa ciki ta zauna ta gaidashi, da fara arshi ya amsa, bayan shuru na 'yan mintuna ya ratsa tsakanin su, doctor Heesham ya gyara zama ya sanar mata abinda ya kawosa.


Murmushi kawai tayi tace masa Allah y tabbatar masu da alkairi, yaji dad'in haka saboda yasan cewar maganar data fad'a itace ta dace, domin yasan a yanzu bazata furta masa kalmar so ba...

 *Page   46* 



 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_



Bayan sun d'an tab'a fira,  *HEESHAM* yayiwa *AISHA* sallama bayan sunyi exchange number, cikeda farin ciki *HEESHAM* ya bar gidan yana addu'ar Allah ya nuna masa ranar da *AISHA* zata nuna masa tsantsar so.



****** ****** ****** ******


Lokaci na tafiya, rayuwa na sauyawa, abubuwa sun faru, masu dad'i da marasa dad'i, kimanin 3 months kenan da komawar *KHAIRAT* school.


 *KHAIRAT* ce zaune a parlour tana kallo, *SADEEK* ne ya shigo, da alama yanzu ya dawo daga aiki, a bakin 'kofa ya tsaya, yayi sallama.


'Dagowa *KHAIRAT* tayi ta amsa sallamar tana kallon sa, bud'e mata hannayen sa yayi, alamun ta taso tayi hugging nasa, ma'kale kafad'a tayi, alamun a'a.


Marairaice fuska yayi, ya ri'ke kunnansa alamun tuba nake, murmushi tayi ta taso da gudu tayi hugging nashi, d'agata sama yayi yana fad'in'' Baby yanaji kin 'kara nauyi ne?.


'' Kodai ajiyata ta samu gurbi ne, a nan?, ya fad'a yana shafa 'kasan mararta.


Dariya tayi tace'' babu wata ajiya a jikina, da akwai ai kaizaka fara sani, kawai dai tsantsar kulawar da kake bani ce''.


'' Uhm nifa na 'kosa naga kin fara haifamin kyawawan baby's''.


'' Uhm nifa idan na biye maka, anan zamu kwana, muje kayi wanka, sai kaci abinci...


 *KHAIRAT* ta fito daga d'aki, bayan tayi wanka, tai sallah, *SADEEK* ta iske harya dawo daga masallaci, zama tayi kusa dashi tana fad'in'' *HUBBY* harka dawo daga masallacin?.


'' Eh yanzu na shigo''.


Tashi tayi ta shiga kitchen ta had'o masa coffee, ta kawo masa, saida ya gama sha, sannan ya kalleta yace'' my *DEEJAH*.


'' Na'am '' ta fad'a tana maida hankalin ta gareshi.


'' Kiyimin al'kawarin idan na fad'a miki abinda nazo dashi, bazaki sanyawa ranki damuwa ba''.


Shuru tayi kafin tace'' insha Allah bazan sanya damuwa a raina ba, duk yadda kakeso haka zanyi''.


'' Dama d'azu muyi meeting a hospital, akan za'a tura mutum 3 zuwa Cairo zasuyi wani aiki ne, na tsawon 3 weeks''.

'' Matsalar da aka samu shine harda ni, a cikin mutum 3 da aka zab'a''.


Shuru tayi, idanunta suka ciki da kwalla tace'' kanaso ka fad'a min, cewar zaka tafi ka barni har tsawon 3 weeks?.


'' Hakane amma kinsan bada son raina zan tafi na barki ba, babu yadda banyi ba akan cewar a samu wani yaje, amma sun'ki, naso su zab'i doctor *HEESHAM* amma sunce ni zan tafi''.


Kuka ta shiga yimasa, ita gaskiya bazai tafi ya barta ba, lallashin ta, ya shiga yi, amma fur ta'ki.


'' Haba *KHAIRAT* tun kafin na fad'a miki, saida nace kiyimin al'kawarin bazaki sanyawa ranki damuwa ba, amma gashi zaki sab'a al'kawarin da kika d'aukar min''.


Hannu ta sanya ta shiga goge hawayen, tana fad'in'' shikenan na daina insha Allah, Allah ya kaiku lafiya ya dawo daku lafiya''.


'' Amma yaushe zaku tafi?.


'' Gobe misalin 11;09am''.


'' Toh Allah y kaimu''.


'' Ameen ''.



Gaba d'aya daran ranar *SADEEK* kasa ganewa *KHAIRAT* yayi, gaba d'aya ta'ki sakin ranta, ko ishashshan bacci bata samu ba.


****** ****** ****** ******


Washe gari *KHAIRAT* ta tashi duk tanajin wani irin babu dad'i a ranta, amma tayi niyyar farantawa mijinta, domin karya tafi da damuwa a ransa.


Tana tashi daga baccin da suka koma, ta shiga kitchen, break fast ta shirya musu,sannan ta nufin d'akin *SADEEK,* ruwan wanka ta had'a masa, sannan ta tashe shi, ya shiga wanka, itama d'akinta ta shiga domin tayi wanka.


Saida ta kimtsa sannan ta fito, a dining ta iske *SADEEK,* 'karasawa tayi ba tare da tayi mai magana ba, ta soma serving d'insu.


Kawai kallonta *SADEEK* yake har suka kammala, kwashe kayan tayi ta nufi kitchen, sai data wankesu sannan ta fito, d'akin *SADEEK* ta nufa ganin baya parlour.


Tana shiga ta iskeshi yana had'a kayan dazai tafi dasu, 'karasawa tayi ta tayashi, had'a kayan, har suka kammala.


Janyota jikinsa yayi yana fad'in'' my *DEEJAH* yanzu zan tafi, kinga har anfara 'kirana''.


Dakewa tayi, ta nemo jarumtaka tace'' toh *HUBBY* insha Allah zan kasance mai yimuku addu'a a tafiyar ku, Allah ya tsare ku a cikin tsare warsa, ya kareku a cikin kariyarsa''.


'' Ameen my *DEEJAH*''.


'' Naji dad'in addu'ar ki sosai, amma gaskiya zanyi missing naki sosai, ban tab'a tunanin wani abu, zai raba mana makwanci da keba, amma yamuka iya neman halak ne''.


Hawaye ta soma yi, hannunsa ya sanya yana goge mata, yana dad'a kwantar mata da hankali.


Suna zaune haka har aka sake kiran wayar shi, amsawa yayi yana fad'in'' gani nan, yanzu zan fito''.


Yana kashe wayar ya  tashi yana fad'in'' my *DEEJAH* zamu tafi''.


Bata iya cemasa komai ba, ta d'auki kayan nashi suka fito, har bakin mota ta rakashi, yanata lallashin ta.


Motar ya shiga, yayi mata key, murmushi *KHAIRAT* ta 'ka'kalo tana daga masa hannu, shima murmushin ya shiga yimata, tana ganin ya doshi hanyar fita, ta juya tana goge hawayen da suka samu damar fitowa.


Kwata-kwata bataji bud'e motar *SADEEK* ko rufewa ba, sai ganinsa kawai tayi a gaban ta, cikin gidan kawai taga  ya shiga, binsa tayi da idanu ta kasa binsa.


Mintuna kad'an da shigarsa, taga ya fito hannun sa ri'ke da d'an 'karamin trolley d'inta, sai hijab, key ya sanya ya rufe 'kofar, inda take ya 'karaso, bai cemata komai ba, ya sanya mata hijab d'in, sannan yaja hannun ta zuwa mota.


Tana tambayar sa ina zasu, amma yayi mata shuru ya bud'e motar ya sanya ta, sannan ya bud'e gidan baya ya aje trolley d'in nata, kana ya shiga yaja motar, suka fice daga gidan.......

 *Page   48* 



 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_




Tundaya d'auki kwalta bai tsaya ba har saida yakai kofar gidan su, horn yayi gateman ya le'ko, yana ganin motar *SADEEK* yayi saurin bud'e masu gate, cinna hancin motarsa yayi zuwa harabar gidan yayi parking.


 *KHAIRAT* kuwa kallonsa kawai takeyi, yana tsayawa, ya bud'e motar ya fito, zagayowa yayi b'angaran *KHAIRAT* ya bud'e mata, fitowa tayi, shi kuma ya bud'e gidan baya, ya d'auko trolley d'inta.


Hannunta yaja suka shiga cikin gidan, basu samu *MOMY* a parlour ba, don haka ya nufi d'akinta, da sallama ya shiga d'akin.


 *MOMY* dake shirin kwanciya ta d'an rage bacci, ta amsa tana fad'in'' *SADEEK* har yanzu baku tafi bane?.


'' Uhm yanzu zamu tafi, ajiya na kawo. miki''.


'' Ajiyar me kuma, meyasa baka bawa *KHAIRAT* ba?.


'' *MOMY* ai saikin fito parlour zakiga ajiyar tawa, konace amana''.


Tashi *MOMY* tayi suka fito parlour, suna shigowa taga *KHAIRAT* zaune idanunta sunyi ja, alamun kuka tayi.


Da sauri *MOMY* ta 'karaso inda take, ta janyota jikinta tana fad'in'' *SADEEK* me kayiwa d'iyar tawa?.


Zama yayi yace'''' *MOMY* dan Allah ga amanar *KHAIRAT* na baki, ki kulamin da ita, nasan da cewar zaki iya, shiyasa na kawota wajanki''.


'' Dan Allah karki bari tayi kuka, abinda yasa ban kaiwa *MOMCY* ita ba, nasan cewa zatayi saimun koma, ai zata iya zama ita d'aya, kinga kuma kad'aici zai dameta''.


Murmushi *MOMY* tayi tace'' karka damu insha Allah *KHAIRAT* bazata kuma yin kuka ba''.


'' Ka tashi kaje, kaga sai kiran wayar ka, akeyi''.


'' Toh *MOMY* ''.


Ita dai *KHAIRAT* batace komai ba, har *MOMY* tagama yimasa addu'a, sannan ya juyo yace'' my *DEEJAH* ko rakiya babu?.

Murmushi tayi ta b'oye fuskarta a jikin *MOMY*.


Murmushi itama tayi tace'' tashi ki raka mijinki, kinji''.


Cikeda kunya *KHAIRAT* ta tashi ta bishi.


Har bakin mota ta rakashi, yanata lallashin ta, zuwa yanzu ta daina kukan, sallama sukayi, yayi hugging nata, tare da yimata kiss a kumatu, sannan ya shige mota.

Harya fara tafiya, ta taho da sauri wajan motar, tsayawa yayi, ta du'ka tana murmushi tace'' ka kulamin da kanka, ko 'kusa banaso ka bari ya tab'a ka''.


'' Magana ta 'karshe banda gane-gane''.


Murmushi kawai yayi mata, yana motar tana d'aga masa hannu ya fice daga gidan, sannan ita ta juya.


Lokacin da *AISHA* ta dawo gida ta tadda *KHAIRAT* tayi murna sosai, a ranar ta kira *MARYAM* har gidan tazo sukasha fira.


 *SADEEK* kuwa suna sauka, ya kira *MOMY* ya sanar mata, sannan ya kira *KHAIRAT* fira suka sha, sai zuba masa shagwab'a take, shi kuma yanajin dad'i, yana lallab'ata.


Tana waya *AISHA* ta shigo suna waya da *HEESHAM*, murmushi kawai takeyi, tana zuba masa shagwab'a, kallon mamaki *KHAIRAT* ta bita dashi, tana mamakin da waye *AISHA* ke waya?.


Sam ta manta ma waya takeyi, har saida tajiyo muryar *SADEEK* yana fad'in'' *BABY* waya d'auke min, hankalin ki, ina magana amma kinyi shuru?.


'' Sorry *HUBBY*''



 '' *MOMY* ce ta shigo tana magana''


'' Owk''


Har suka gama waya, sukayi sallama *AISHA* bata gama tata wayar ba.


 *KHAIRAT* ce ta juyo tana kallonta tace *EASHA* wai dawa kike waya haka, babu story?.


Juya mata baya tayi alamun bazama tayi magana ba.


Murmushin mugunta *KHAIRAT* tayi, sannan ta mi'ka hannun ta, zuwa kunnan *AISHA* ta fizge wayar.


Da sauri *AISHA* tayo kanta tana fad'in'' yi ha'kuri matar yaya, dan Allah ki bani wayata''.


Sakkowa *KHAIRAT* tayi daga gadon tana dariya, itama *AISHA* biyota tayi suka fara zagaye d'akin.


Saida *KHAIRAT* ta gaji sannan ta mi'ka mata wayar, suna maida numfashi suka zube a bakin gadon, 


Harara *AISHA* ta watsa mata tana fad'in'' kawai ina waya da nisful 'kalbina kin wani katse mu''.


Dariya *KHAIRAT* tayi, sannan ta matso kusa da ita tace'' yi ha'kuri toh, kid'an fad'a min waye''.


'' Uhm gulma ajali.... toh doctor *HEESHAM* ne''.


Baki *KHAIRAT* ta saki tace'' lallai wato abin 'yar wariyar launin fata ne''.


'' Ke kin b'oye min, sannan shima *HUBBY* ya b'oye min, babu komai nasan me zanyi masa''.


Dariya *AISHA* tayi tace'' son tsurkunki ne ya sanya aka b'oye miki''.


Bata kulata ba ta nemi waje ta kwanta, ta janyo blanket ta rufe jikinta.



****** ****** ****** ******



Bangaran *SADEEK* kuwa abinda ya kaisu  kawai yakeyi, baya b'ata lokacin sa wajan shashanci, yana kallon abokanan tafiyarsa zasu fita yawace-yawacen su,  amma shiya gwammace ya zauna ya kira *BABYN* sa a waya, susha soyayyar su.


Wata irin kewar matar tashi yake ji, gaba d'aya zaman ya isheshi, kwata-kwata wayar bata gamsar dashi, Allah Allah yake 3 weeks ya cika, a haka har suka kusa cinye kwana kin  da zasuyi.



****** ****** ****** ******


'Bangaran *KHAIRAT* ma haka take gaba d'aya batajin dad'in zaman, wasu lokutan ita da AISHA suna tafiya wajan *MOMCY* su wani idan basuda lecture,


 *MOMY* harta gama fahimtar *KHAIRAT* ta 'kosa  ya dawo, dan har wata 'yar rama tayi.



Duk sanda taje gidan MOMCY da kalar kayan gyaran jikin da zata bata, na ciki dana waje, mayuka da tura rukan datake sanyawa ana had'a mata kuwa ba'a magana, masu tsadar gaske.



'Bangaran MOMY ma haka, don tanaso idan d'an nata ya dawo, ya yaji sauyi, don ita da AISHA da  KHAIRAT da SADEEK duk d'aya ta d'ukesu.



****** ****** ****** ******


Gobe ne ya kama *SADEEK* zai shigo Nigeria, *KHAIRAT* kuwa murnar ta, kasa b'oyiwa tayi, AISHA ce ta rakata anguwar su MARYAM akayi mata, 'kunshi ja da ba'ki mai mugun kyau.


Daga nan suka wuce wajan saloon aka gyara mata gashi, saboda tace batasan kitso, sai yamma li'kis suka dawo.


Da daddare *SADEEK* ya kira *KHAIRAT* yake sanar mata, cewar an d'aga dawowar su, zuwa next week.


Kuka ta sanya masa, da kyar ya iya lallashin ta, ta ha'kura.


Washe gari *MOMY* tace suxo su tayata girki, yau *DADY* zaiyi ba'ki.


Sai wajan 'karfe 3:00 suka kammala, wanka sukaje sukayi, suka shirya, *AISHA* na fesa turare taji wayar ta na ringing, zata d'aukar *KHAIRAT* tayi saurin d'auke wayar tana dariya, saboda taga doctor *HEESHAM* ke kira.


Tashi *AISHA* tayi zata amshi wayar ta, amma ta hanata, zagaye d'akin suka shiga yi, *KHAIRAT* na dariya tayi hanyar parlour da gudu, ko gabanta bata kallo ta shigo parlourn.


Ji tayi ta buge mutum, 'kamshin turaresa ne ya sanya ta d'ago da sauri, suka had'a ido....

 *Page   50* 



 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_



Kai tsaye hospital d'insu ya nufa da ita, da kansa yayi mata duk wani gwaje-gwaje daya dace, sannan aka sanya mata drift.


Tashin farko result ya nuna *KHAIRAT* tana d'auke da ciki harna tsawon 2 mouths, ciwon cikin kuma za'kin datasha jiyane yayi mata yawa.



Murna wajan *SADEEK* kuwa ba'a magana, saida ya dur'kusa kasa yayiwa Allah godiya.


'Dakinda *KHAIRAT* take ya nufa, isketa yayi tana bacci, kallonta yayi, yanajin wani mugun farin ciki a zuciyarsa, 'karasawa yayi inda take ya janyo kujera ya zauna, hannu ya sanya ya shafa fuskarta yana murmushi.


Yana zaune kusan 2 hours, har ruwan da aka sanya mata ya 'kare, da kanshi ya cire mata, sannan ya dawo ya zauna.


Ganin 'karfe 10:00am tayi ne, ya sanya ya ciro wayarsa cikeda zumud'i ya kira *MOMY* suna gama gaisawa ya shaida mata, amma bai fad'a mata cewar *KHAIRAT*  batada lafiya ba.


 *MOMY* tayi matu'kar farin ciki, har murnar ta, ta gaza b'oyuwa, tambayar sa tayi ina *KHAIRAT* take, shaida mata yayi tayi  bacci, addu'a tayiwa *KHAIRAT* sannan tace zata 'kirata idan ta tashi.


Yana aje wayar yaga *KHAIRAT* tana bud'e ido, da sauri ya matsa kusa da ita yana yimata sannu.


Murmushi tayi tace'' *HUBBY* wai meyake damuna?.


Bai b'oye mata ba, ya fad'a mata murna tayi sosai tare da godewa Allah.


Basu wani b'ata lokaci ba, aka sallamesu, bayan yahe ya siyo mata magungunan daya kamata, sannan suka koma gida.


Suna isa gida, ya shiga toilet ya had'a mata ruwan wanka, sannan taje ta shiga toilet d'in.


Kafin ta fito yaje ya had'o musu tea da indomei, ya aje.


Tana fitowa ta shirya, abinci yace tazo taci.


Ma'ke kafad'a tayi tana fad'in'' uhm nidai banason abincin sweet zan sha''.


Lallashin ta ya shiga yi, yana sanar mata sweet d'in data sha, da yawane ya sanya mata ciwon ciki, don haka tayi ha'kuri ta rage sha.


Bata musa masa ba, ta zauna, sukaci abincin, magani ya d'auko mata, tanata kumbure-kumbure da 'kananan kuka, tasha maganin.


Suna kammalawa ya kwashe kayan, sannan ya shiga wanka, yana fitowa ya shirya, sannan ya lallab'ata akan cewar ta bashi 30 mins zaije ya dawo.



Yana fita taji wayarta na ringing, tana dubawa taga sunan *MOMY.*


Da murmushi a fuskarta ta amsa wayar tareda sallama.


Amsawa *MOMY* tayi tana fad'in'' ya 'karfin jiki?.


Saida tayi jim sannan tace'' alhamdulillah'' saboda batasan *SADEEK* ya fad'awa *MOMY* ba.


'' Toh Allah ya raba lafiya''.


Rufe fuskarta tayi da hannu d'aya, ita sam batan cewar har maganar cikin ya sanar mata ba.


Muryar *MOMY* taji ta kuma cewa'' wai *KHAIRAT* yaushe zaki daina jin kunya ta ne?.


'' Uhm *MOMY* nima ai bansan sanda nake jin kunyar ba''.


Dariya *MOMY* tayi tace'' toh yadai kamata a rage''


'' toh *MOMY* ''.



Sallama sukayi bayan *MOMY* ta bata shawar wari akan abubuwan dasuka dace.



Bata jima da gama wayar ba, *SADEEK* ya shigo, 'karasawa yayi inda take yace'' *BABY* hope dai babu inda yake miki ciwo?.



Murmushi tayi tace'' alhamdulillah babu inda yake min ciwo''.



'' Masha Allah''.


'' Amma *HUBBY* shine ka fad'awa *MOMY* ciki gareni, ta kirani duk kunya ta rufeni''.


Dariya yayi yace'' toh ai harsu *DADY* da *MOMCY* na fad'a wa''.



'' Na shiga uku, yaya wannan wace irin rashin kunya ce?.


'' ba wani rashin kunya, ai abun su tayamu farin ciki ne''.



'' Hakane amma nidai bazan iya ba''.



'' Haka kikace, taso muje d'aki kiga wani abu''.


Tashi tayi sukaje d'akin, a bakin mirror suka tsaya, wani d'an madai daicin box ya fito dashi, yana bud'ewa saiga wata dan datsetsiyar sar'ka da d'an kunne, sar'kar ya fara sanya mata sai kuma d'an kunnan.


Ba 'karamin kyau sukayi mata ba, da sauri *KHAIRAT* ta juyo ta rungume shi, tana zuba masa godiya.



****** ****** ****** ******


Duk wata kulawa data dace *SADEEK* yana 'ko'karin bawa *KHAIRAT,* hakan bai sanya ta zamo mai son jiki ba, duk wani abu  data saba yi, bata daina ba, hatta school bata aje ba, tunda exam's zasu fara, batada wata matsala, musamman daya kasance cikin baya bata wahala, inkika gantama bazaki kirata mai ciki ba.


Shan za'ki ne dai tananan tanayi, amma bawai kamar da ba, bawani lokacin ma idan *SADEEK* na gida a b'oye takesha.



Yau su *KHAIRAT* akayi graduation zokiga murna a wajansu, sai hamdala suke ga uban giji, basu had'a wani party ko walima ba, kasan cewar an tsaida bikinsu *AISHA*  sati d'aya da graduation d'in nasu.


Wunin ranar a gidan *MOMCY* sukayi shi, aje maganar graduated sukayi, suka koma maganar biki, suna tsara yadda bikin zai kasance, tunda saura  1 week,


Sai dare *SADEEK* yazo ya d'auki *KHAIRAT,* sannan suma su *MARYAM* suka tafi gida.



Ta kowane b'angare shirye-shirye suke, kwata-kwata *KHAIRAT* bata samun zama, *SADEEK* baiyi gigin hanata ba, saboda shima hakan take ta b'agaransa.


(Niko nace dama bikin  abokai har biyu, ga kuma tilon 'kanwa)


Yau akayi kamu, amare ansha kyau, masha Allah, duk inda suka gilma sai an kallesu.


Su amara kuwa sai kayi tunanin itama amaryce, fad'ar irin kyawun da tayi b'ata lokaci ne, abinka damai ciki, tayi haske gawata 'yar 'kiba data 'kara.


Bayan an kammala kamu kowa ya watse, amare suka koma gida, wata irin gajiya *KHAIRAT* taji ta rufeta, don haka ta fara shirin kwana a gidan.


Koda *SADEEK* ya kirata su wuce gida 'kin d'aga wayar tayi, daga 'karshema ta kashe gaba d'aya tayi kwanciyar ta.


 *MOMCY* ce ta shigo neman *RAHAMA* zasu koma gida ko tana wajan *AISHA* , ta tarar da *KHAIRAT* na shirin bacci.


Nan kuwa *MOMCY* tace bata isa ba, idan ta zauna me zatayi, kuka take shirin yimata wai ita ta gaji, tsaki *MOMCY* tayi ta kira *SADEEK* a waya, ta tambayeshi yana Ina.


Sanar mata yayi yana 'kofar gida, kashe wayar tayi tacewa *KHAIRAT* wallahi idan bata tashi sun tafi ba, saita sab'a mata.


Tana kumbure-kumbure ta fito, ta shige motar suka tafi.


Suna zuwa gida *SADEEK* ya rufeta da fad'a, akan me zata'ki d'aukar wayarsa, 'karshema ta kashe wayar, inda kwanan takeso ai inta fad'a masa baxai hanata ba.


Daga 'karshema yace bazata koma gidan bikin ba, kuka ta sanya masa taba bashi ha'kuri, amma yayi mata banza ya shige d'akinsa ya rufe 'kofa....

 *Page   51* 



 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_



Kuka ta zauna tana yi, da taga kukan ba shine mafita ba sai ta tashi  ta nufi d'akin nasa.


Tayi sa'a bai rufe 'kofar ba, don haka ta shiga, bata ganshi ba, sai motsin ruwa data jiyo a toilet, hakan ne ya tabbatar mata ya shiga wanka.


Tashi tayi ta tafi d'akinta, tayi wanka ta sanya sleeping dress, ta feshe jikinta da turaruka sannan ta, fito.


Tana shiga d'akin sa ta iskeshi har ya kwanta, 'karasawa tayi ta kwanta kusa dashi.

Juya mata baya yayi, murmushi tayi sannan ta sake matsawa tayi hugging d'insa ta baya.


Janye hannun nata yayi, ta kuma mai dasu tana fad'in'' *YAYA* dan Allah kayi ha'kuri, wallahi bazan kuma ba''.


Shuru yayi mata ba tare da yace komai ba, iska ta hura masa a kunne tace'' please dan Allah kayi ha'kuri kayi min magana, Allah badan ka barni naje biki nake baka ha'kuri ba''.


'' Ni kawai inaso ka daina fushi dani ne, batun zuwa biki kuma na ha'kura''.


Nan ma shuru yayi mata, magiya ta shiga yi masa amma ya shareta, kuka ta fara yi, amma bai kulata ba, a zuciye ta tashi zata bar d'akin, janyota yayi yana dariya ta fad'a jikinsa, 'ko'karin mi'kewa take, ya ri'keta yana fad'in'' ina zaki kuma?.


'' Kodan kinga ina dariya?, toh ba ha'kura nayi ba''.

'' Uhm dan Allah kar kace haka, kaga na baka ha'kuri, kuma har kuka nayi''.


'' Shikenan ta wuce, kema kinsan bazan iya fushi da keba, kawai inaso ki fahimci banji dad'in abinda kikayi bane, gani nake kamar kina gudu na ne''.


'' Allah *HUBBY* ba haka bane, gaba d'aya na gaji ne, shiyasa nayi niyyar kwana acan, amma kayi ha'kuri bazan sake b'ata maka rai ba''.


'' Toh Allah yasa''.


'' Amma nidai a daina wahalmin da baby, ke naga ko nayin jikin ki bakya ji, ciki ya kusa kaiwa 5 months amma kina tsalle-tsalle''.


'' Amma na fahimci dan kinga baki da girman ciki ne, ba kowa yake gane kinada ciki ba, shiyasa kike biyewa 'yan mata''.


Dariya tayi tana fad'in'' Ai hakan yafi dad'i''.



****** ****** ****** ******


Washe gari koda wasa batayi shirin zuwa gidan ba, har wajan 12:00pm, shi kuwa *SADEEK* tun safe ya fice.


Sai wajan 1:00pm sannan *SADEEK* ya kirata a waya ya fad'a mata ta shirya ta tafi, godiya ta shiga zuba masa, murmushi yayi ya kashe wayar sa, harga Allah baiyi tunanin zata iya kaiwa har lokacin bata tafi gidan bikin ba koda bai bata izini ba.


Suna gama waya ta had'a kayanta, dama ta gaji da kiran da su *MARYAM* keyi mata.



****** ****** ****** ******


Misalin 'karfe 6:00pm suka fita party, amare da angwaye ansha kyau, duk wanda ka kalli fuskarsa a cikinsu murmushi yake, wajan cike yake ma'kil da 'yan uwa da abokanan arzi'ki.


 *KHAIRAT* kuwa shiga sukayi iri d'aya da *SADEEK* ita material itada *RAHAMA,* shi kuma shadda colour d'in material d'in *KHAIRAT,* shima tare suka d'inka da *SAGEER.*


Duk inda suka gifta kallonsu ake, maganar kud'i kuwa *SADEEK* bai san adadin abinda ya li'kawa angwayen da amaren ba, gimbiyar tashi kuwa ba'a magana, kawai li'ki ya shiga yi mata.


Haka aka watse kowa cike da farin ciki, *KHAIRAT* kuwa bata tsaya wata-wata ba, tana yin sallah ta sace jiki tabi mijinta suka koma gida, saboda bataso ta kuma yin laifi.


****** ****** ****** ******


Washe gari kuwa tun 8:00am *SADEEK* ya d'auki *KHAIRAT* ya kaita gidan, kasancewar yau d'aurin aure.


'Karfe 11:00 dai-dai dumbin jama'a suka shaida d'aurin auran *AISHA da angonta doctor HEESHAM da kuma MARYAM da angota ABDULLAHI,* d'aurin auran daya tara d'umbin jama'a daga b'angarori da dama.


Kai tsaye gidan angwaye suka nufa, 'yan uwa sai tsokanarsu akeyi, *KHAIRAT* ce ta fito da amare akasha hotuna, duk inda tayi *SADEEK* kallonta yake, saboda wani mugun kyau dayaga tayi masa, yauma shadda iri d'aya suka sanya, ai kuwa  sunsha hotuna kamar ba gobe, musamman *KHAIRAT* da amaren.


Suna gama hotuna, aka shiga kawo abincin, kala kala, da drinks kala-kala.


'Karfe biyar dai-dai aka fara shirin kai amare, *MARYAM* aka fara shirin kaiwa kasan cewar gidanta yafi nisa.


Nasiha sosai akayi mata, sannan aka d'auketa zuwa gidansu *ABDUL.* 


Karb'ar mutunci akayi musu a gidan, nasiha sosai mahaifiyar *ABDUL* tayiwa *MARYAM* tareda sauran 'yan uwanta dana mahaifin sa.


Cikeda farin ciki aka wuce da *MARYAM* gidan mijinta, 'yan uwa nayi mata san barka da samun surukai na gari.


Saida su *SADEEK* suka kawo ango sannan, *KHAIRAT* ta fara shirin komawa, domin sukai *AISHA.* 


Lallab'ata *MARYAM* ta shiga yi, dan Allah karta tafi, dariya *KHAIRAT* tayi, domin ta tuna ranar da suka kaita, har kuka tayi musu amma sukayi mata wayo suka gudu.


Itama dabara tayi mata ta fito, sannan ta tura aka d'auko mata jakar ta ba tare da maryam ta gani ba.


A harabar gidan taga *SADEEK* tare da abokanan su, yana ganin sa ya 'karaso inda take, yana fad'in tazo ya mai da ita, bai yadda ta koma a motar wani ba.


Kai tsaye gidan su *DR HEESHAM* ya kaita, kasancewar  nan aka fara kai *AISHA* 

Yana kaita ya juya, saboda yasan motar su d'aya da *AISHA,* itama babu laifi sun samu tarba mai kyau a gidan surukan nata.


Basu wani b'ata lokacin ba, suka mi'ka ta gidanta wanda bai da nisa da nasu *KHAIRAT.* 


Da 'kyar aka raba  *AISHA* da *KHAIRAT* sai kuka take ita a cewar ta *KHAIRAT* ba zata tafi ta barta ba.


Tana fitowa *SADEEK* na 'karsowa, bai jira komai ba, ya wuce gidan su, a cewar sa shi bazai raka ango ba, kar 'kanwar shi ta rainashi.


Babu yadda *KHAIRAT* batayi ba akan ya kaita ta d'ebo kayanta amma ya'ki, yace gobe zaije ya d'auko mata.


****** ****** ****** ******



Masha Allah, haka rayuwa taci gaba da tafiya, abubuwa na sauya launi, da masu dad'i da marasa dad'i, wasu sunyi rashi wasu kuma sun samu 'karuwa, abin dai sai godiyar uban giji.



'Bangaran amare kuwa, abin sai godiya, kowacce na zaune lafiya da mijinta. Kusan kullum *AISHA* na gidan *KHAIRAT* kasan cewar cikinta ya tsufa, harta shiga watan haihuwa.


Kulawa sosai *SADEEK* yake bata, wata rana ma, ko hospital baya zuwa, sosai su *MOMY* suke zuwa dubata akai-akai, *MARYAM* ma na zuwa ko kuma su suje mata.


****** ****** ****** ******


Yau tun asuba *KHAIRAT* ta tashi tana jin ciwo sama-sama, daurewa tayi bata so *SADEEK* ya gane amma duk da haka sai da yaga yanayinta ya sauya.


Da ya tambayeta ko tana jin wani abu ne, ce masa tayi a'a, da kansa yaje ya had'a musu break fast sannan ya kawo mata.


Bata iya cin wani abu ba, sai ruwan tea kawai tasha, babu yadda beyi da ita ba akan ta daure ko indomie taci, amma tace bazata iya ba.


Suna zaune aka kira shi ana neman sa a hospital, yaso yace yanada marar lafiya bazai samu zuwa ba, amma *KHAIRAT* ta hana shi, dole ya shirya ya tafi, amma saida yayiwa *AISHA* waya cewar in ta gama abinda take, tazo ta taya *KHAIRAT* zama, domin shi sam hankalin shi bai kwanta ba.


Bai jima da fita ba, ciwo mai tsanani ya zowa *KHAIRAT* tun tana jurewa har ta kasa, hawaye kawai takeyi tana salati, wayar ta take 'ko'karin d'aukowa amma ta kasa....
