 *Page   52* 



 _BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM_



Wani hawaye mai zafi ne, ya shiga bin kuncinta, sai yanzu take nadamar barin *SADEEK* ya tafi.


Wani sabon ciwon ne ya sake ri'keta, a gigice ta kira sunan Allah da 'karfi, *MARYAM* daketa doka sallama ce ta joyita, a gigice ta fara hawa steps d'in, kai tsaye d'akin *KHAIRAT* ta nufa, kasan cewar can take jiyo sautin numfashin ta.


Tana shiga tayi kan *KHAIRAT* tana tambayar ta lafiya, babu abinda *KHAIRAT* ta iya cemata, sai nuna mata wayarta dake kan mirror tayi.

Babu b'ata lokaci *MARYAM* ta kira wayar *SADEEK,* yana amsawa ta fad'a masa.


Ko 'karasa jin me zatace baiyi ba ya juya, dama ko cikin hospital d'in bai shiga ba.


20 mins ya kaishi gidan, d'akinta ya shiga ya tattara duk wani abin bu'kata, sannan ya sanyawa   *KHAIRAT* hijab ya ciccibe'ta yayi hanyar waje, da sauri *MARYAM* ta bishi.

Motar daya sanya *KHAIRAT* itama ta shiga, sannan yaja motar, addu'ar kawai baba mai gadi ya shiga yiwa *KHAIRAT,* Allah ya sauketa lafiya.


Suna isa hospital d'in, *SADEEK* yayi parking, bai jira komai ba ya bud'e gidan baya ya ciccib'o *KHAIRAT* yayi cikin hospital da ita.


Ita kuwa *MARYAM* tsayawa tayi ta rufe motar, sannan ta shiga ciki, lokacin data shiga har *SADEEK* ya shiga da *KHAIRAT* d'akin haihuwa.


Kayan jikinsa yaje ya sauya, saboda bai yarda wani ya amshi haihuwar ba, wata nose guda d'aya ya samu wacce zata taimaka masa.


 *MARYAM* kuwa tana waje sai zubar hawaye takeyi, tana kuma yimata addu'a,  wayar tace ta shiga ruri, tana dubawa taga *ABDUL* ke kura.


Murya a sanyaye ta amsa tareda sallama, jin muryar ta kamar mai kuka, ya sanya yace'' Baby na meya sameki, naji muryar ki haka?.


Cikin kuka tace'' muna hospital ne, *KHAIRAT* ce zata haihu''.


'' Ayyah toh ki daina kuka kinji, addu'a zakiyi mata, Allah ya sauketa lafiya''.


''Ameen''


'' Yawwa amma dan Allah karki kuma kukan, kina sanyawa kanki damuwa, ko kinaso Babyn mu ya samu matsala ne?


Ai wani sabon kukan ta sanya masa, ita wallahi batason cikin, bazata iya haihuwa ba, lallab'ata yashiga yi, amma fur ta'ki.


Dabara yayi mata, cewar inya dawo zai san yadda za'ayi da cikin, saboda ya fahimci tsoro taji.


Allah sarki baiwar Allah, *KHAIRAT* kusan 4 hrs ana abu d'aya tun tana salati a bayyane, har yakai sai dai tayi a zuci.


 *SADEEK* kuwa wani irin mugun tausayin ta yake ji, ganin abu ya'kici ya'ki cinye wa ne, ya sanya ya d'auko wayar sa, ya kirawo *MOMCY* da *MOMY* ya shaida musu, sannan yace suyi mata addu'a.


Har la'asar *KHAIRAT* bata haihu ba, *MOMCY* kuwa gajiya tayi da kiran waya, don haka ta sanyo mayafinta ta taho, *AISHA* kuwa tunda *MARYAM* ta fada mata, ta sanyawa *DR HEESHAM* kuka a waya, har saidaya barta tazo hospital d'in.


Misalin 5:00pm *KHAIRAT* ta gama gala baita, har hawaye saida *SADEEK*  ya zubar mata, fitowa yayi ya barta da wata doctor daya sanya a kirawo, sallar la'asar yaje yayi, addu'a ya shiga yiwa *KHAIRAT* 


Yana zaune *AISHA* ta taho da sauri tana fad'in yazo, ana nemansa, da sauri ya tashi, yana shiga d'akin yaga jariri a hannun nose d'in, sannan ga wani a hannun doctor d'in.


Hamdala yayi ga uban giji, wajan *KHAIRAT* kawai ya nufa yana kallon yadda wuni d'aya kamanninta suka sauya.



Babu b'ata lokaci aka shirya yara, da kuma mai jego. sannan aka bada izinin shigowa a gansu.


Kowa ya d'auki yaran sai dai yace masha Allah, mace da namiji ne, macen sak kamannin *KHAIRAT* tayo, namiji kuma *SADEEK* haske kawai yafi *SADEEK* d'in.


 *KHAIRAT* kuwa tayi garau kamar ba ita tasha ciwo ba, kallon baby's din take, tanajin wata irin soyayyar su na ratsata, su *MOMCY* kuwa murna suke sosai.


Karfe 8;00pm aka sallamesu, gidan ta aka kaita, da zumar washe gari *MOMCY* zata turo mata 'kanwata.


Haka kuwa akayi, MOMCY ta turo mata 'kanwarta, wacce zata dinga kula da ita, *SADEEK* kuwa tunda akayi haihuwar kullum cikin kashe kud'i yake, har magana *KHAIRAT* tayi masa amma yace mata idan bai kashe ma iyalinsa ba, wazai yiwa?


****** ****** ****** ******


Ranar suna kuwa yara sukaci sunan su, na *HASSAN DA USSAINA, amma ana kiransu HANEEF DA HANEEFA.*


Kayan fitar suna kuwa saida *SADEEK* *KHAIRAT* set 15, haka taita shiga tana fita, ba 'karamin kudi su Dady da Abban *KHAIRAT* suka kashewa jikokin nasu ba, su *MARYAM* da *AISHA* ma haka, maza jensu sun kashe musu kud'i sosai, lokacin kowaccen su tanada 'karamin ciki. 


Washe garin suna *MOMY* ta turo a d'auki *KHAIRAT* yaranta a mai dasu gida, da kyar *SADEEK* ya bari aka tafi dasu don babu yadda zaiyi, gidan su aka kaita, wajan *MOMCY.*


Kusan kullum sai *SADEEK* yaje ganin matarsa da ya ransa, har zuwa lokacin da tayi arba'in aka mai data gida.


****** ****** ****** ******


BAYAN SHEKARA BIYAR


 *KHAIRAT* na gani zaune a tamfatsetsen falon su, kana ganinta kasan cewar hutu ya gama ratsata, zuwa yanzu ta zama cikakkiyar mace.


Wata kyakyawar baby girl da baxata wuce 1 year ba, na gani a jikinta sai wasanta take mata suna dariya.


Sallamar su *MARYAM* ne ta katsesu, wasu yara na gani guda 4 sunyo wajan *KHAIRAT* suka rungume ta.(guda 2 yaran *AISHA* duka maza, d'aya sunan dadyn tane dashi, ana ki ransa *ANNUR* d'ayan kuma Dadyn su  *DR HEESHAM* ana kiransa *ABBA*, sauran biyun kuma na *MARYAM* wato *AFRAH* da *AFFAN)* 



 *AISHA* dake shirin zama ce tace'' ohhh Allah karku yadda mata baby mana''.


Dariya sukayi, *MARYAM* tace'' wai INA *HANEEF* da *HANEEFA* da kuma *SULTAN?.*

       ( yaron data  kuma haifa kenan, daga shi sai wannan baby girl d'in)


 *KHAIRAT* ce tace'' tun jiya sageer da rahama suka zo suka tafi dasu, amma abbansu ya tafi d'auko su''.


Tana rufe baki *SADEEK* ya shigo hannunsa d'auke da sultan, inda su haneef suka tafi da gudu wajan maminsu.


 *SADEEK* na kalla naga yadda ya zama cikakken magidanci, kana ganinsa kasan naira ta gama ji'kashi, kallon *KHAIRAT* yayi ya kashe mata ido d'aya yace'' ko hugging d'inna da aka saba yau baza ayi ba?.


Murmushi kawai tayi, ta nuna masa yaran dake parlourn, kallosu *AISHA* yayi yace'' ku kuma wani salon iskanci ne  zaku shigo kubar maza jenku a 'kofar gida.


Basuyi magana ba, sai *MARYAM* ce ta tashi zataje ta kirasu, *KHAIRAT* ce ta tsaida ita, sannan ta lallab'a *SADEEK* yake ya kirasu.


A parlourn suka zauna, suna shan fira gwanin sha'awa, ga yaransu sai wasa suke....




 *ALHAMDULILLAH*
